import { validateCoherence, autoFixCoherence } from '../src/engine/coherence.js';
import { translator } from '../ui/i18n.js';
import { parseOverrideList, formatOverrideList } from '../shared/domains.js';
import { MAX_IMPORT_BYTES, createExportEnvelope, parseImportEnvelope } from '../shared/settings.js';
import { InputValidationError, StateConflictError } from '../shared/errors.js';
import { PROFILE_SURFACES, PROFILE_MODES, MAX_PROFILE_LIBRARY_BYTES, normalizeProfileLibrary, normalizeProfileAssignments, defaultProfileSurfaces, validateProfileRecord } from '../shared/profile-library.js';

const t = translator();
const byId = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const domainMode = byId('domainMode');
const domains = byId('domains');
const overrides = byId('overrides');
const rotationMode = byId('rotationMode');
const intervalMinutes = byId('intervalMinutes');
const rotationProfiles = byId('rotationProfiles');
const status = byId('status');
const error = byId('error');
const diagnosticsReport = byId('diagnosticsReport');
const environmentStatus = byId('environmentStatus');
let settings;
let profiles = [];
let runtimeChromiumMajor = null;
let runtimeBrowserFamily = 'unknown';
let runtimeEnvironment = null;
let busy = false;
let profileLibrary = null;
let launcherManagedProfiles = false;
let profileSurfaceDefinitions = PROFILE_SURFACES;
let profileModes = PROFILE_MODES;
let stateRevision = 0;
let latestObservedRevision = 0;
let remoteSyncPending = false;
let remoteSyncInFlight = false;
let editorDirty = false;

function showError(message = '') {
  error.hidden = !message;
  error.textContent = message;
}
function updateReloadLatestButton() {
  const button = byId('reloadLatestState');
  if (button) { button.hidden = !(remoteSyncPending && editorDirty); button.disabled = busy; }
}

function responseError(response, fallback) {
  if (response?.errorCode === 'STATE_CONFLICT') {
    remoteSyncPending = true;
    updateReloadLatestButton();
    // Keep any local edits visible. A clean view can safely fetch the new state;
    // a dirty view waits for explicit reload instead of replacing user input.
    queueMicrotask(() => {
      if (!busy && !editorDirty) { remoteSyncPending = false; void refreshFromBackground({ force: true }); }
    });
    return new StateConflictError(t('stateConflict'));
  }
  return response?.errorCode === 'INVALID_INPUT' && typeof response.error === 'string'
    ? new InputValidationError(response.error)
    : new Error(fallback);
}

function safeErrorMessage(exception, fallback) {
  return exception instanceof InputValidationError || exception instanceof StateConflictError ? exception.message : fallback;
}

function adoptResponseRevision(response) {
  if (Number.isSafeInteger(response?.stateRevision)) {
    stateRevision = response.stateRevision;
    latestObservedRevision = Math.max(latestObservedRevision, stateRevision);
  }
  remoteSyncPending = stateRevision < latestObservedRevision;
  if (remoteSyncPending) queueMicrotask(() => {
    if (remoteSyncPending && !busy && !editorDirty) {
      remoteSyncPending = false;
      void refreshFromBackground({ force: true });
    }
  });
}

async function refreshFromBackground({ force = false } = {}) {
  if (busy && !force) { remoteSyncPending = true; return; }
  if (editorDirty && !force) { remoteSyncPending = true; status.textContent = t('stateConflict'); return; }
  if (remoteSyncInFlight) { remoteSyncPending = true; return; }
  remoteSyncInFlight = true;
  try {
    const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'GET_SETTINGS' });
    if (!response?.ok) return;
    const nextRevision = Number.isSafeInteger(response.stateRevision) ? response.stateRevision : stateRevision;
    if (nextRevision < stateRevision || nextRevision < latestObservedRevision) { remoteSyncPending = true; return; }
    settings = response.settings;
    stateRevision = nextRevision;
    latestObservedRevision = Math.max(latestObservedRevision, nextRevision);
    launcherManagedProfiles = Boolean(response.launcherManagedProfiles);
    profiles = response.profiles ?? profiles;
    runtimeChromiumMajor = Number.isInteger(response.runtimeChromiumMajor) ? response.runtimeChromiumMajor : runtimeChromiumMajor;
    runtimeBrowserFamily = typeof response.runtimeBrowserFamily === 'string' ? response.runtimeBrowserFamily : runtimeBrowserFamily;
    runtimeEnvironment = response.environment && typeof response.environment === 'object' ? response.environment : runtimeEnvironment;
    render();
    await loadProfileLibraryUi();
    editorDirty = false;
    updateReloadLatestButton();
  } catch { /* The next state-change event or a reopened workspace can retry. */ }
  finally {
    remoteSyncInFlight = false;
    if (remoteSyncPending && !busy && !editorDirty) {
      remoteSyncPending = false;
      queueMicrotask(() => { void refreshFromBackground({ force: true }); });
    }
  }
}

chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((message) => {
  if (message?.type !== 'VEIL_STATE_CHANGED') return false;
  if (Number.isSafeInteger(message.revision)) latestObservedRevision = Math.max(latestObservedRevision, message.revision);
  if (Number.isSafeInteger(message.revision) && message.revision <= stateRevision) return false;
  if (busy || editorDirty) remoteSyncPending = true;
  else void refreshFromBackground();
  updateReloadLatestButton();
  return false;
});

document.addEventListener('input', (event) => {
  if (event.target instanceof Element && event.target.matches('input, select, textarea')) { editorDirty = true; updateReloadLatestButton(); }
});
document.addEventListener('change', (event) => {
  if (event.target instanceof Element && event.target.matches('input, select, textarea')) { editorDirty = true; updateReloadLatestButton(); }
});

function setBusy(value) {
  busy = value;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-busy-control]').forEach((control) => { control.disabled = value; });
  byId('importFile').disabled = value;
  applyLauncherManagedUi();
  if (!value && remoteSyncPending && !editorDirty) {
    remoteSyncPending = false;
    queueMicrotask(() => { void refreshFromBackground({ force: true }); });
  }
  updateReloadLatestButton();
}

function applyLauncherManagedUi() {
  const notice = byId('launcherManagedProfilesNotice');
  if (notice) notice.hidden = !launcherManagedProfiles;
  const lockedIds = [
    'overrides', 'rotationMode', 'intervalMinutes', 'profileRecordName', 'profileBaseTemplate',
    'profileMode', 'profileSeed', 'profileNewSeed', 'profileClone', 'profileDelete',
    'profileNotes', 'profileLocale', 'profileTimezone', 'profileLatitude', 'profileLongitude',
    'profileGeoAccuracy', 'profileAssignments', 'profileSave', 'profileImportButton', 'profileImportFile',
    'reset',
  ];
  for (const id of lockedIds) {
    const element = byId(id);
    if (element) element.disabled = busy || launcherManagedProfiles;
  }
  byId('profileRecordSelect').disabled = busy;
  byId('profileExport').disabled = busy;
  byId('profileDelete').disabled = busy || launcherManagedProfiles || !profileLibrary || profileLibrary.profiles.length <= 1;
  byId('rotationProfiles').disabled = busy || launcherManagedProfiles;
  byId('profileSurfaceToggles').querySelectorAll('input').forEach((input) => {
    const record = selectedLibraryRecord();
    const meta = profileSurfaceDefinitions[input.value];
    input.disabled = busy || launcherManagedProfiles || !meta?.modes.includes(byId('profileMode').value || record?.mode || 'identity');
  });
}

function profileLabel(item) {
  const browser = item.browser === 'edge' ? 'Edge' : 'Chrome';
  const os = { windows: 'Windows x64', macos: item.architecture === 'arm' ? 'macOS Apple Silicon' : 'macOS Intel', linux: 'Linux x64', android: 'Android ARM64' }[item.os] ?? item.os;
  return `${browser} ${item.major ?? ''} — ${os}`.replace(/\s+—/u, ' —');
}

function renderRotationProfiles() {
  rotationProfiles.replaceChildren();
  const legend = document.createElement('legend');
  legend.textContent = t('rotationProfiles');
  rotationProfiles.append(legend);
  const selectedIds = new Set(settings.rotation.profileIds);
  for (const item of profiles) {
    const label = document.createElement('label');
    label.className = 'profile-choice';
    const checkbox = document.createElement('input');
    checkbox.type = 'checkbox';
    checkbox.name = 'rotationProfile';
    checkbox.value = item.id;
    checkbox.checked = selectedIds.has(item.id);
    checkbox.dataset.busyControl = 'true';
    checkbox.disabled = busy || launcherManagedProfiles;
    const text = document.createElement('span');
    text.textContent = profileLabel(item);
    label.append(checkbox, text);
    rotationProfiles.append(label);
  }
  rotationProfiles.disabled = busy || launcherManagedProfiles;
}

function renderCompatibilityWarning() {
  const warning = byId('compatibilityWarning');
  const configuredIds = new Set([
    settings.profileId,
    ...settings.overrides.map((entry) => entry.profileId),
    ...settings.rotation.profileIds,
  ]);
  const configured = profiles.filter((item) => configuredIds.has(item.id));
  const warnings = [];
  if (Number.isInteger(runtimeChromiumMajor)) {
    const mismatchedMajors = [...new Set(configured
      .filter((item) => item.major !== runtimeChromiumMajor)
      .map((item) => item.major))].sort((left, right) => left - right);
    if (mismatchedMajors.length) {
      warnings.push(t('versionMismatch').replace('{profile}', mismatchedMajors.join(', ')).replace('{engine}', String(runtimeChromiumMajor)));
    }
  }
  const expectedProfileBrowser = runtimeProfileBrowser(runtimeBrowserFamily);
  if (expectedProfileBrowser) {
    const mismatchedBrowsers = [...new Set(configured
      .filter((item) => item.browser !== expectedProfileBrowser)
      .map((item) => item.browser === 'edge' ? 'Microsoft Edge' : 'Google Chrome'))];
    if (mismatchedBrowsers.length) {
      const engineBrowser = runtimeBrowserFamily === 'firefox' ? 'Firefox (Gecko)' : runtimeBrowserFamily === 'edge' ? 'Microsoft Edge' : 'Chromium-based browser';
      warnings.push(t('browserMismatch').replace('{profileBrowser}', mismatchedBrowsers.join(', ')).replace('{engineBrowser}', engineBrowser));
    }
  }
  const nativeOs = runtimeEnvironment?.nativeOs;
  if (nativeOs && nativeOs !== 'unknown') {
    const osLabels = { windows: 'Windows', macos: 'macOS', linux: 'Linux', android: 'Android', ios: 'iOS' };
    const mismatchedOs = [...new Set(configured.filter((item) => item.os !== nativeOs).map((item) => osLabels[item.os] ?? item.os))];
    if (mismatchedOs.length) {
      warnings.push(t('platformMismatch')
        .replace('{profileOs}', mismatchedOs.join(', '))
        .replace('{nativeOs}', osLabels[nativeOs] ?? nativeOs));
    }
  }
  warning.hidden = warnings.length === 0;
  warning.textContent = warnings.join(' ');
}

function runtimeProfileBrowser(family) {
  if (family === 'edge') return 'edge';
  if (family === 'firefox') return 'firefox';
  if (['chrome', 'chromium', 'quetta', 'kiwi', 'yandex', 'opera', 'brave', 'samsung-internet'].includes(family)) return 'chrome';
  return null;
}

function browserLabel(family) {
  return ({ quetta: 'Quetta', kiwi: 'Kiwi Browser', edge: 'Microsoft Edge', firefox: 'Firefox', yandex: 'Yandex Browser', opera: 'Opera', brave: 'Brave', 'samsung-internet': 'Samsung Internet', chrome: 'Chrome', chromium: 'Chromium-based browser' })[family] ?? 'Browser (brand not exposed)';
}

function renderEnvironment() {
  if (!runtimeEnvironment) { environmentStatus.textContent = ''; return; }
  const pageLabel = ({ 'document-start': t('pageModeEarly'), 'navigation-fallback': t('pageModeFallback'), unavailable: t('pageModeUnavailable') })[runtimeEnvironment.strategy?.page] ?? t('pageModeUnavailable');
  const headerLabel = ({ 'declarative-rules': 'DNR', 'blocking-webRequest': 'webRequest', unavailable: t('headerModeUnavailable') })[runtimeEnvironment.strategy?.header] ?? t('headerModeUnavailable');
  const modeLabel = ({ full: t('supportFull'), adaptive: t('supportAdaptive'), 'headers-only': t('supportHeadersOnly'), 'page-only': t('supportPageOnly'), limited: t('supportLimited') })[runtimeEnvironment.supportLevel] ?? t('supportLimited');
  environmentStatus.textContent = t('runtimeMode')
    .replace('{browser}', browserLabel(runtimeEnvironment.browser))
    .replace('{device}', runtimeEnvironment.mobile ? t('mobileEnvironment') : t('desktopEnvironment'))
    .replace('{mode}', modeLabel)
    .replace('{header}', headerLabel)
    .replace('{page}', pageLabel);
}

function render() {
  domainMode.value = settings.domainMode;
  domains.value = settings.domains.join('\n');
  overrides.value = formatOverrideList(settings.overrides);
  rotationMode.value = settings.rotation.mode;
  intervalMinutes.value = String(settings.rotation.intervalMinutes);
  renderRotationProfiles();
  renderCompatibilityWarning();
  renderEnvironment();
  applyLauncherManagedUi();
}

function collectSettings() {
  const profileIds = [...rotationProfiles.querySelectorAll('input[name="rotationProfile"]:checked')].map((input) => input.value);
  const rotation = {
    mode: rotationMode.value,
    intervalMinutes: Number(intervalMinutes.value),
    profileIds,
  };
  return {
    ...settings,
    domainMode: domainMode.value,
    domains: domains.value.split(/\r?\n/u),
    overrides: parseOverrideList(overrides.value, profiles.map((item) => item.id)),
    rotation,
  };
}

async function commitSettings(next, successMessage = t('saved')) {
  if (busy) return false;
  setBusy(true);
  showError();
  diagnosticsReport.hidden = true;
  status.textContent = t('loading');
  try {
    const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'SAVE_SETTINGS', settings: next, expectedRevision: stateRevision });
    if (!response?.ok) throw responseError(response, t('saveError'));
    settings = response.settings;
    adoptResponseRevision(response);
    editorDirty = false;
    updateReloadLatestButton();
    runtimeEnvironment = response.environment && typeof response.environment === 'object' ? response.environment : runtimeEnvironment;
    render();
    status.textContent = successMessage;
    return true;
  } catch (exception) {
    showError(safeErrorMessage(exception, t('saveError')));
    status.textContent = '';
    return false;
  } finally {
    setBusy(false);
    renderRotationProfiles();
  }
}

function profileRecordLabel(record) {
  const base = profiles.find((item) => item.id === record.baseProfileId);
  const mode = record.mode ? ` · ${record.mode}` : '';
  return `${record.name} (${base ? `${base.browser} ${base.major} / ${base.os}` : record.baseProfileId}${mode})`;
}

function selectedLibraryRecord() {
  return profileLibrary?.profiles.find((record) => record.id === profileRecordSelect.value) ?? null;
}

function renderProfileSurfaceToggles(record, { useModeDefaults = false } = {}) {
  const host = byId('profileSurfaceToggles');
  host.replaceChildren();
  const defaults = defaultProfileSurfaces(byId('profileMode').value || record?.mode || 'identity');
  for (const [key, meta] of Object.entries(profileSurfaceDefinitions)) {
    const label = document.createElement('label');
    label.className = 'profile-surface-choice';
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'checkbox';
    input.name = 'profileSurface';
    input.value = key;
    input.checked = useModeDefaults ? Boolean(defaults[key]) : Boolean(record?.surfaces?.[key]);
    input.disabled = !meta.modes.includes(byId('profileMode').value || record?.mode || 'identity');
    const text = document.createElement('span');
    const coverage = meta.status === 'uncovered' ? ' — uncovered' : meta.status === 'partial' ? ' — partial' : '';
    text.textContent = `${t(meta.labelKey)}${coverage}`;
    label.title = t(meta.warningKey);
    label.append(input, text);
    host.append(label);
  }
}

function renderProfileLibraryForm() {
  if (!profileLibrary) return;
  const select = byId('profileRecordSelect');
  const previousId = select.value || profileLibrary.activeProfileId;
  select.replaceChildren();
  for (const record of profileLibrary.profiles) {
    const option = document.createElement('option');
    option.value = record.id;
    option.textContent = profileRecordLabel(record);
    select.append(option);
  }
  const preferredId = profileLibrary.profiles.some((record) => record.id === previousId)
    ? previousId : profileLibrary.activeProfileId;
  select.value = preferredId;
  profileLibrary.activeProfileId = preferredId;

  const baseSelect = byId('profileBaseTemplate');
  const previousBase = baseSelect.value;
  baseSelect.replaceChildren();
  for (const item of profiles) {
    const option = document.createElement('option');
    option.value = item.id;
    option.textContent = profileLabel(item);
    baseSelect.append(option);
  }
  const record = selectedLibraryRecord();
  if (!record) return;
  byId('profileRecordName').value = record.name;
  baseSelect.value = record.baseProfileId;
  byId('profileMode').value = record.mode;
  byId('profileSeed').value = record.seed;
  byId('profileNotes').value = record.notes;
  byId('profileLocale').value = record.locale;
  byId('profileTimezone').value = record.timezone;
  byId('profileLatitude').value = record.geolocation ? String(record.geolocation.latitude) : '';
  byId('profileLongitude').value = record.geolocation ? String(record.geolocation.longitude) : '';
  byId('profileGeoAccuracy').value = record.geolocation ? String(record.geolocation.accuracy) : '100';
  byId('profileAssignments').value = profileLibrary.assignments.map((entry) => `${entry.domain} = ${entry.profileId}`).join('\n');
  renderProfileSurfaceToggles(record);
  byId('profileValidationReport').hidden = true;
  applyLauncherManagedUi();
}

function currentSurfaceToggles() {
  return Object.fromEntries([...byId('profileSurfaceToggles').querySelectorAll('input[name="profileSurface"]')]
    .map((input) => [input.value, Boolean(input.checked && !input.disabled)]));
}

function profileRecordFromForm() {
  const selected = selectedLibraryRecord();
  if (!selected) throw new InputValidationError('Select a profile first.');
  const latitudeText = byId('profileLatitude').value.trim();
  const longitudeText = byId('profileLongitude').value.trim();
  if (Boolean(latitudeText) !== Boolean(longitudeText)) {
    throw new InputValidationError('Enter both latitude and longitude, or leave both empty.');
  }
  const geolocation = latitudeText && longitudeText ? {
    latitude: Number(latitudeText), longitude: Number(longitudeText),
    accuracy: byId('profileGeoAccuracy').value.trim() ? Number(byId('profileGeoAccuracy').value) : 100,
  } : null;
  const record = {
    ...selected,
    name: byId('profileRecordName').value,
    baseProfileId: byId('profileBaseTemplate').value,
    mode: byId('profileMode').value,
    seed: byId('profileSeed').value,
    notes: byId('profileNotes').value,
    locale: byId('profileLocale').value.trim(),
    timezone: byId('profileTimezone').value.trim(),
    geolocation,
    surfaces: currentSurfaceToggles(),
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
  const validation = validateProfileRecord(record);
  if (!validation.ok) throw new InputValidationError(validation.error);
  return validation.profile;
}

function parseAssignmentsFromForm(library) {
  const records = byId('profileAssignments').value.split(/\r?\n/u).map((line) => line.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const assignments = records.map((line) => {
    const split = line.indexOf('=');
    if (split < 0) throw new InputValidationError('Each site assignment must use: hostname = profile-id.');
    return { domain: line.slice(0, split).trim(), profileId: line.slice(split + 1).trim() };
  });
  return normalizeProfileAssignments(assignments, library.profiles, { strict: true });
}

function showProfileValidation(library) {
  const report = byId('profileValidationReport');
  const result = library.profiles.map((profile) => {
    const validation = validateProfileRecord(profile);
    return {
      id: profile.id,
      consistencyScore: validation.consistencyScore,
      warnings: validation.warnings,
      covered: Object.keys(profile.surfaces).filter((key) => profile.surfaces[key] && PROFILE_SURFACES[key]?.status === 'partial'),
      uncovered: validation.unsupported,
      partial: validation.partial,
    };
  });
  report.textContent = JSON.stringify({ profileCount: library.profiles.length, assignmentCount: library.assignments.length, profiles: result }, null, 2);
  report.hidden = false;
}

async function loadProfileLibraryUi() {
  const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'GET_PROFILE_LIBRARY' });
  if (!response?.ok) throw responseError(response, t('profileSaveError'));
  launcherManagedProfiles = Boolean(response.launcherManagedProfiles);
  profileSurfaceDefinitions = response.surfaces ?? PROFILE_SURFACES;
  profileModes = response.modes ?? PROFILE_MODES;
  profileLibrary = normalizeProfileLibrary(response.library, { strict: true });
  renderProfileLibraryForm();
}

function setProfileStatus(message, isError = false) {
  const node = byId('profileLibraryStatus');
  node.textContent = message;
  node.classList.toggle('warning', isError);
}

async function saveProfileLibraryFromForm() {
  if (!profileLibrary) return;
  const record = profileRecordFromForm();
  const profilesNext = profileLibrary.profiles.map((item) => item.id === record.id ? record : item);
  const candidate = normalizeProfileLibrary({
    ...profileLibrary,
    activeProfileId: record.id,
    profiles: profilesNext,
    assignments: parseAssignmentsFromForm({ ...profileLibrary, profiles: profilesNext }),
  }, { strict: true });
  const active = candidate.profiles.find((item) => item.id === candidate.activeProfileId);
  if (active?.surfaces?.webrtc && chrome.permissions?.request && chrome.permissions?.contains) {
    const permissionExists = await chrome.permissions.contains({ permissions: ['privacy'] }).catch(() => false);
    if (!permissionExists) {
      const granted = await chrome.permissions.request({ permissions: ['privacy'] }).catch(() => false);
      if (!granted) setProfileStatus(`${t('profileSaveSuccess')} ${t('profilePermissionReason')}`, true);
    }
  }
  const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'SAVE_PROFILE_LIBRARY', library: candidate, expectedRevision: stateRevision });
  if (!response?.ok) throw responseError(response, t('profileSaveError'));
  profileLibrary = normalizeProfileLibrary(response.library, { strict: true });
  settings = response.settings ?? settings;
  adoptResponseRevision(response);
  editorDirty = false;
  render();
  renderProfileLibraryForm();
  showProfileValidation(profileLibrary);
  const webRtcStatus = response.webRtcStatus ? ` WebRTC policy: ${response.webRtcStatus}.` : '';
  setProfileStatus(`${t('profileSaveSuccess')}${webRtcStatus}`);
}

byId('reloadLatestState')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (busy) return;
  editorDirty = false;
  remoteSyncPending = false;
  updateReloadLatestButton();
  status.textContent = t('loading');
  void refreshFromBackground({ force: true });
});

async function init() {
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach((node) => {
    node.textContent = t(node.getAttribute('data-i18n'));
  });
  try {
    const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'GET_SETTINGS' });
    if (!response?.ok) throw responseError(response, 'Could not load settings.');
    settings = response.settings;
    stateRevision = Number.isSafeInteger(response.stateRevision) ? response.stateRevision : 0;
    latestObservedRevision = Math.max(latestObservedRevision, stateRevision);
    editorDirty = false;
    launcherManagedProfiles = Boolean(response.launcherManagedProfiles);
    profiles = response.profiles;
    runtimeChromiumMajor = Number.isInteger(response.runtimeChromiumMajor) ? response.runtimeChromiumMajor : null;
    runtimeBrowserFamily = typeof response.runtimeBrowserFamily === 'string' ? response.runtimeBrowserFamily : 'unknown';
    runtimeEnvironment = response.environment && typeof response.environment === 'object' ? response.environment : null;
    render();
    await loadProfileLibraryUi();
    showProfileValidation(profileLibrary);
  } catch (exception) {
    showError(safeErrorMessage(exception, 'Could not load settings.'));
  }
}

byId('save').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (busy || !settings) return;
  try { await commitSettings(collectSettings()); }
  catch (exception) { showError(safeErrorMessage(exception, t('saveError'))); }
});

byId('reset').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (busy || !window.confirm(t('resetConfirm'))) return;
  setBusy(true);
  showError();
  try {
    const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'RESET_SETTINGS', expectedRevision: stateRevision });
    if (!response?.ok) throw responseError(response, 'Reset failed.');
    settings = response.settings;
    adoptResponseRevision(response);
    editorDirty = false;
    updateReloadLatestButton();
    runtimeEnvironment = response.environment && typeof response.environment === 'object' ? response.environment : runtimeEnvironment;
    render();
    status.textContent = t('saved');
    diagnosticsReport.hidden = true;
  } catch (exception) {
    showError(safeErrorMessage(exception, 'Reset failed.'));
  } finally {
    setBusy(false);
    renderRotationProfiles();
  }
});

byId('export').addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (!settings || busy) return;
  try {
    const envelope = createExportEnvelope(settings);
    const blob = new Blob([`${JSON.stringify(envelope, null, 2)}\n`], { type: 'application/json;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `veil-profile-studio-settings-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
    document.body.append(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();
    queueMicrotask(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
    status.textContent = t('exportSettings');
  } catch (exception) {
    showError(safeErrorMessage(exception, 'Could not export settings.'));
  }
});

byId('importButton').addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (!busy) byId('importFile').click();
});

byId('importFile').addEventListener('change', async (event) => {
  const file = event.currentTarget.files?.[0];
  event.currentTarget.value = '';
  if (!file || busy) return;
  try {
    if (file.size > MAX_IMPORT_BYTES) throw new InputValidationError(`Settings file exceeds the ${MAX_IMPORT_BYTES}-byte import limit.`);
    const imported = parseImportEnvelope(JSON.parse(await file.text()));
    if (!window.confirm(t('importConfirm'))) return;
    await commitSettings(imported, t('saved'));
  } catch (exception) {
    showError(exception instanceof InputValidationError ? `${t('importError')}: ${exception.message}` : t('importError'));
  }
});

byId('checkHealth').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (busy) return;
  setBusy(true);
  showError();
  status.textContent = t('diagnosticsRunning');
  try {
    const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'GET_DIAGNOSTICS' });
    if (!response?.ok) throw responseError(response, 'Diagnostics request failed.');
    const report = response.diagnostics;
    diagnosticsReport.textContent = JSON.stringify(report, null, 2);
    diagnosticsReport.hidden = false;
    const hasIssue = Boolean(report.lastApply.error) || report.lastApply.failedTabs > 0;
    status.textContent = t(hasIssue ? 'healthWarning' : 'healthGood');
  } catch (exception) {
    showError(safeErrorMessage(exception, 'Diagnostics failed.'));
    status.textContent = '';
  } finally {
    setBusy(false);
    renderRotationProfiles();
  }
});

byId('profileRecordSelect').addEventListener('change', () => {
  if (!profileLibrary) return;
  profileLibrary.activeProfileId = byId('profileRecordSelect').value;
  renderProfileLibraryForm();
});

byId('profileMode').addEventListener('change', () => {
  const record = selectedLibraryRecord();
  if (!record) return;
  renderProfileSurfaceToggles({ ...record, mode: byId('profileMode').value }, { useModeDefaults: true });
});

byId('profileNewSeed').addEventListener('click', () => {
  try {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(24);
    if (!globalThis.crypto?.getRandomValues) throw new Error('Secure random generator unavailable.');
    globalThis.crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
    byId('profileSeed').value = [...bytes].map((value) => value.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
    setProfileStatus(t('profileSeedGenerated'));
  } catch {
    setProfileStatus(t('profileSeedError'), true);
  }
});

byId('profileClone').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (!profileLibrary) return;
  try {
    const source = selectedLibraryRecord();
    const name = window.prompt(t('profileNewName'), source ? `${source.name} copy` : 'Profile copy');
    if (name === null) return;
    const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'CLONE_PROFILE', profileId: byId('profileRecordSelect').value, name, expectedRevision: stateRevision });
    if (!response?.ok) throw responseError(response, t('profileSaveError'));
    profileLibrary = normalizeProfileLibrary(response.library, { strict: true });
    adoptResponseRevision(response);
    editorDirty = false;
    renderProfileLibraryForm();
    showProfileValidation(profileLibrary);
    setProfileStatus(t('profileSaveSuccess'));
  } catch (exception) {
    setProfileStatus(safeErrorMessage(exception, t('profileSaveError')), true);
  }
});

byId('profileDelete').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (!profileLibrary || !window.confirm(t('profileDeleteConfirm'))) return;
  try {
    const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'DELETE_PROFILE', profileId: byId('profileRecordSelect').value, expectedRevision: stateRevision });
    if (!response?.ok) throw responseError(response, t('profileSaveError'));
    profileLibrary = normalizeProfileLibrary(response.library, { strict: true });
    settings = response.settings ?? settings;
    adoptResponseRevision(response);
    editorDirty = false;
    render();
    renderProfileLibraryForm();
    showProfileValidation(profileLibrary);
    setProfileStatus(t('profileSaveSuccess'));
  } catch (exception) {
    setProfileStatus(safeErrorMessage(exception, t('profileSaveError')), true);
  }
});

    const blob = new Blob([`${JSON.stringify(response.export, null, 2)}\n`], { type: 'application/json;charset=utf-8' });

    link.href = url;
    link.download = `veil-profile-definitions-v1-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.json`;
    document.body.append(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();

    setProfileStatus(t('profileExport'));
  } catch (exception) {
    setProfileStatus(safeErrorMessage(exception, t('profileSaveError')), true);
  }
});

byId('profileImportButton').addEventListener('click', () => byId('profileImportFile').click());
byId('profileImportFile').addEventListener('change', async (event) => {
  const file = event.currentTarget.files?.[0];
  event.currentTarget.value = '';
  if (!file) return;
  try {
    if (file.size > MAX_PROFILE_LIBRARY_BYTES) throw new InputValidationError(`Profile definitions exceed the ${MAX_PROFILE_LIBRARY_BYTES}-byte import limit.`);
    const value = JSON.parse(await file.text());
    if (!window.confirm(t('profileImportConfirm'))) return;
    const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'IMPORT_PROFILE_LIBRARY', value, expectedRevision: stateRevision });
    if (!response?.ok) throw responseError(response, t('profileImportError'));
    profileLibrary = normalizeProfileLibrary(response.library, { strict: true });
    settings = response.settings ?? settings;
    adoptResponseRevision(response);
    editorDirty = false;
    render();
    renderProfileLibraryForm();
    showProfileValidation(profileLibrary);
    setProfileStatus(t('profileSaveSuccess'));
  } catch (exception) {
    setProfileStatus(exception instanceof InputValidationError ? `${t('profileImportError')}: ${exception.message}` : t('profileImportError'), true);
  }
});

tzPresetSelect?.addEventListener('change', () => {
  const val = tzPresetSelect.value;
  if (val && TZ_PRESET_MAP[val]) {
    const p = TZ_PRESET_MAP[val];
    byId('profileTimezone').value = p.timezone;
    byId('profileLocale').value = p.locale;
    byId('profileLatitude').value = p.lat;
    byId('profileLongitude').value = p.lng;
    editorDirty = true;
    updateReloadLatestButton();
  }
});

byId('clearSessionOptions')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  setBusy(true);
  try {
    let origin = null;
    let tabId = null;
    try {
      const [activeTab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, lastFocusedWindow: true });
      if (activeTab?.url && /^https?:/iu.test(activeTab.url)) {
        origin = new URL(activeTab.url).origin;
        tabId = activeTab.id;
      }
    } catch (_) {}
    const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'CLEAR_BROWSING_SESSION', origin, tabId });
    if (response?.ok) {
      const msg = origin
        ? `${t('clearSessionSuccess')} (${new URL(origin).hostname})`
        : t('clearSessionSuccess');
      showStatus(msg);
    } else {
      showError(t('clearSessionError'));
    }
  } catch (e) {
    showError(e?.message || 'Lỗi');
  } finally {
    setBusy(false);
  }
});

// Coherence handlers (F2)
byId('btnCheckCoherence')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
  const record = selectedLibraryRecord();
  if (!record) return;
  const res = validateCoherence(record);
  const out = byId('coherenceResult');
  if (!out) return;
  out.hidden = false;
  let text = `Điểm nhất quán: ${res.score}/100. `;
  if (res.coherent) {
    text += '✓ Cấu hình profile đồng bộ, không phát hiện mâu thuẫn lớn.';
    out.style.color = 'var(--mint)';
  } else {
    text += `! Phát hiện ${res.issues.length} mâu thuẫn: ` + res.issues.map(i => i.rule).join('; ');
    out.style.color = 'var(--warning)';
  }
  out.textContent = text;
});

byId('btnAutoFixCoherence')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
  const record = selectedLibraryRecord();
  if (!record) return;
  const fixed = autoFixCoherence(record);
  Object.assign(record, fixed);
  renderProfileLibraryForm();
  const out = byId('coherenceResult');
  if (out) {
    out.hidden = false;
    out.style.color = 'var(--mint)';
    out.textContent = '✓ Đã tự động điều chỉnh memory (<=8GB), touch points và platform phù hợp với OS.';
  }
});

import { translator } from '../ui/i18n.js';
import { InputValidationError, StateConflictError } from '../shared/errors.js';
import { DEVICE_PRESETS, TIMEZONE_PRESETS } from '../shared/profile-library.js';

const t = translator();
const byId = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const enabled = byId('enabled');
const profile = byId('profile');
const randomizeButton = byId('randomizeProfile');
const randomizeBtnText = byId('randomizeBtnText');
const autoRotate = byId('autoRotate');
const quickInterval = byId('quickInterval');
const status = byId('status');
const uaPreview = byId('uaPreview');
const error = byId('error');
const compatibilityWarning = byId('compatibilityWarning');
const environmentStatus = byId('environmentStatus');
const recommendedProfileNote = byId('recommendedProfileNote');
const runSelfTestButton = byId('runSelfTest');
const copySelfTestReportButton = byId('copySelfTestReport');
const selfTestResults = byId('selfTestResults');
const devicePresetSelect = byId('devicePresetSelect');
const clearSessionButton = byId('clearSessionButton');

// Category tabs & hardware card elements
const tabAll = byId('tabAll');
const tabPc = byId('tabPc');
const tabAndroid = byId('tabAndroid');
const hwCategoryBadge = byId('hwCategoryBadge');
const hwSystemText = byId('hwSystemText');
const hwScreenText = byId('hwScreenText');
const hwGpuText = byId('hwGpuText');
const hwHardwareText = byId('hwHardwareText');
const hwTouchText = byId('hwTouchText');
const hwPluginsText = byId('hwPluginsText');

let currentCategory = 'all';
let currentSettings;
let launcherManagedProfiles = false;
let profiles = [];
let recommendedProfileId = null;
let runtimeChromiumMajor = null;
let runtimeBrowserFamily = 'unknown';
let runtimeEnvironment = null;
let lastApplyReport = null;
let busy = false;
let lastSelfTestReport = null;
let stateRevision = 0;
let latestObservedRevision = 0;
let remoteSyncPending = false;
let remoteSyncInFlight = false;
let activePresetId = '';

function formatProfileLabel(item) {
  const browser = item.browser === 'edge' ? 'Microsoft Edge' : 'Google Chrome';
  const osLabels = {
    windows: 'Windows 11 x64',
    macos: item.architecture === 'arm' ? 'macOS (Apple Silicon)' : 'macOS (Intel)',
    linux: 'Linux Ubuntu x64',
    android: 'Android Mobile'
  };
  return `${browser} ${item.major ?? ''} — ${osLabels[item.os] ?? item.os}`.replace(/\s+—/u, ' —');
}

function setBusy(value) {
  busy = value;
  enabled.disabled = value;
  profile.disabled = value || launcherManagedProfiles;
  randomizeButton.disabled = value || launcherManagedProfiles || !currentSettings?.enabled;
  const rotationMode = currentSettings?.rotation?.mode ?? 'off';
  autoRotate.disabled = value || launcherManagedProfiles || (!currentSettings?.enabled && rotationMode === 'off');
  quickInterval.disabled = value || launcherManagedProfiles || !currentSettings?.enabled;
  byId('openOptions').disabled = value;
  if (devicePresetSelect) devicePresetSelect.disabled = value;
  if (clearSessionButton) clearSessionButton.disabled = value;
  runSelfTestButton.disabled = value || !currentSettings;
  copySelfTestReportButton.disabled = value || !lastSelfTestReport;
  if (!value && remoteSyncPending) {
    remoteSyncPending = false;
    queueMicrotask(() => { try {
  const extVerEl = byId('extVersion');
  if (extVerEl) extVerEl.textContent = 'v' + (chrome.runtime?.getManifest?.()?.version || '3.1.0');
} catch (_) {}
void refreshFromBackground(); });
  }
}

function showError(message = '') {
  error.hidden = !message;
  error.textContent = message;
}

function updateCategoryTabs() {
  [tabAll, tabPc, tabAndroid].forEach(tab => {
    if (tab) tab.classList.toggle('active', tab.dataset.category === currentCategory);
  });
  if (randomizeBtnText) {
    if (currentCategory === 'pc') randomizeBtnText.textContent = '🎲 Random cấu hình PC';
    else if (currentCategory === 'android') randomizeBtnText.textContent = '🎲 Random cấu hình Android';
    else randomizeBtnText.textContent = '🎲 Thiên biến vạn hoá (Random)';
  }
  populatePresetOptions();
  populateProfileOptions();
}

function populatePresetOptions() {
  if (!devicePresetSelect) return;
  const previousVal = devicePresetSelect.value || activePresetId;
  devicePresetSelect.innerHTML = '';

  const defaultOpt = document.createElement('option');
  defaultOpt.value = '';
  defaultOpt.textContent = '-- Chọn Preset Thiết Bị Chuẩn --';
  devicePresetSelect.appendChild(defaultOpt);

  let filtered = [...DEVICE_PRESETS];
  if (currentCategory === 'pc') {
    filtered = filtered.filter(p => !p.baseProfileId.includes('android'));
  } else if (currentCategory === 'android') {
    filtered = filtered.filter(p => p.baseProfileId.includes('android'));
  }

  for (const preset of filtered) {
    const opt = document.createElement('option');
    opt.value = preset.id;
    opt.textContent = preset.name;
    devicePresetSelect.appendChild(opt);
  }

  if (previousVal && [...devicePresetSelect.options].some(o => o.value === previousVal)) {
    devicePresetSelect.value = previousVal;
  }
}

function populateProfileOptions() {
  if (!profile) return;
  const previousVal = profile.value;
  profile.innerHTML = '';

  let filtered = [...profiles];
  if (currentCategory === 'pc') {
    filtered = filtered.filter(p => !p.id.includes('android'));
  } else if (currentCategory === 'android') {
    filtered = filtered.filter(p => p.id.includes('android'));
  }

  for (const p of filtered) {
    const opt = document.createElement('option');
    opt.value = p.id;
    opt.textContent = formatProfileLabel(p);
    profile.appendChild(opt);
  }

  if (previousVal && [...profile.options].some(o => o.value === previousVal)) {
    profile.value = previousVal;
  } else if (currentSettings?.profileId && [...profile.options].some(o => o.value === currentSettings.profileId)) {
    profile.value = currentSettings.profileId;
  }
}

function updateHardwareCard(activePreset, activeProfile) {
  const isMobile = activeProfile?.mobile || activeProfile?.os === 'android' || activePreset?.baseProfileId?.includes('android');
  
  if (hwCategoryBadge) {
    hwCategoryBadge.textContent = isMobile ? '📱 Android Mobile' : '💻 PC Desktop';
    hwCategoryBadge.style.color = isMobile ? 'var(--mint)' : 'var(--accent-2)';
  }

  if (hwSystemText) {
    if (activePreset) {
      hwSystemText.textContent = activePreset.name.split('—')[0]?.replace(/[💻📱🍏🐧🇻🇳]/gu, '').trim() || (isMobile ? 'Android 14' : 'Windows 11 x64');
    } else if (activeProfile) {
      hwSystemText.textContent = formatProfileLabel(activeProfile);
    }
  }

  if (hwScreenText) {
    const w = activePreset?.screenWidth || (isMobile ? 412 : 1920);
    const h = activePreset?.screenHeight || (isMobile ? 915 : 1080);
    const dpr = activePreset?.devicePixelRatio || (isMobile ? 2.625 : 1.0);
    hwScreenText.textContent = `${w}x${h} @ ${dpr}x`;
  }

  if (hwGpuText) {
    if (activePreset?.gpuRenderer) {
      const match = /ANGLE \(([^,]+),\s*([^,]+)/u.exec(activePreset.gpuRenderer);
      hwGpuText.textContent = match ? `${match[1]} ${match[2]}` : activePreset.gpuRenderer.slice(0, 32);
    } else {
      hwGpuText.textContent = isMobile ? 'ARM Mali-G715 / Qualcomm Adreno' : 'NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4060';
    }
  }

  if (hwHardwareText) {
    const cores = activePreset?.cpuCores || (isMobile ? 8 : 16);
    const ram = activePreset?.memoryGb || (isMobile ? 8 : 16);
    hwHardwareText.textContent = `${cores} Cores | ${ram}GB RAM`;
  }

  if (hwTouchText) {
    const touch = activePreset?.maxTouchPoints ?? (isMobile ? 5 : 0);
    hwTouchText.textContent = touch > 0 ? `${touch} Touch Points (Coarse)` : '0 Touch (Fine Pointer & Hover)';
  }

  if (hwPluginsText) {
    hwPluginsText.textContent = isMobile ? '0 Plugins (Mobile standard)' : '5 Plugins (PDF Viewer Suite)';
  }
}

async function refreshFromBackground() {
  if (busy || remoteSyncInFlight) { remoteSyncPending = true; return; }
  remoteSyncInFlight = true;
  try {
    const response = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'GET_SETTINGS' });
    if (!response?.ok) return;
    const nextRevision = Number.isSafeInteger(response.stateRevision) ? response.stateRevision : stateRevision;
    if (nextRevision < stateRevision || nextRevision < latestObservedRevision) { remoteSyncPending = true; return; }
    currentSettings = response.settings;
    stateRevision = nextRevision;
    latestObservedRevision = Math.max(latestObservedRevision, nextRevision);
    launcherManagedProfiles = Boolean(response.launcherManagedProfiles);
    profiles = response.profiles ?? profiles;
    recommendedProfileId = typeof response.recommendedProfileId === 'string' ? response.recommendedProfileId : recommendedProfileId;
    runtimeChromiumMajor = Number.isInteger(response.runtimeChromiumMajor) ? response.runtimeChromiumMajor : runtimeChromiumMajor;
    runtimeBrowserFamily = typeof response.runtimeBrowserFamily === 'string' ? response.runtimeBrowserFamily : runtimeBrowserFamily;
    runtimeEnvironment = response.environment && typeof response.environment === 'object' ? response.environment : runtimeEnvironment;
    lastApplyReport = response.lastApply && typeof response.lastApply === 'object' ? response.lastApply : lastApplyReport;
    
    // Auto-detect matching category from profileId if not set
    if (currentSettings?.profileId?.includes('android')) {
      if (currentCategory === 'pc') currentCategory = 'all';
    } else if (currentSettings?.profileId) {
      if (currentCategory === 'android') currentCategory = 'all';
    }
    
    render();
  } catch (_) {
  } finally {
    remoteSyncInFlight = false;
    if (remoteSyncPending && !busy) {
      remoteSyncPending = false;
      queueMicrotask(() => { try {
  const extVerEl = byId('extVersion');
  if (extVerEl) extVerEl.textContent = 'v' + (chrome.runtime?.getManifest?.()?.version || '3.1.0');
} catch (_) {}
void refreshFromBackground(); });
    }
  }
}

chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((message) => {
  if (message?.type !== 'VEIL_STATE_CHANGED') return false;
  if (Number.isSafeInteger(message.revision)) latestObservedRevision = Math.max(latestObservedRevision, message.revision);
  if (Number.isSafeInteger(message.revision) && message.revision <= stateRevision) return false;
  if (busy) remoteSyncPending = true;
  else try {
  const extVerEl = byId('extVersion');
  if (extVerEl) extVerEl.textContent = 'v' + (chrome.runtime?.getManifest?.()?.version || '3.1.0');
} catch (_) {}
void refreshFromBackground();
  return false;
});

function renderEnvironment() {
  if (!runtimeEnvironment || !environmentStatus) return;
  const isMob = runtimeEnvironment.mobile;
  environmentStatus.textContent = `Môi trường thực: ${runtimeEnvironment.browser || 'Chromium'} on ${isMob ? 'Android' : 'Desktop'} | Cơ chế: DNR + Document-Start Stealth`;
}

function render() {
  if (!currentSettings) return;
  enabled.checked = Boolean(currentSettings.enabled);
  renderEnvironment();

  updateCategoryTabs();

  // Find active preset matching baseProfileId
  const matchingPreset = DEVICE_PRESETS.find(p => p.id === activePresetId)
    || DEVICE_PRESETS.find(p => p.baseProfileId === currentSettings.profileId);
  const activeProfile = profiles.find(p => p.id === currentSettings.profileId);

  if (devicePresetSelect && matchingPreset) {
    devicePresetSelect.value = matchingPreset.id;
  }
  if (profile && currentSettings.profileId) {
    profile.value = currentSettings.profileId;
  }

  updateHardwareCard(matchingPreset, activeProfile);

  if (uaPreview) {
    uaPreview.textContent = activeProfile?.userAgent || '—';
  }

  if (status) {
    if (currentSettings.enabled) {
      status.textContent = 'Đang tàng hình trên các trang phù hợp';
      status.classList.remove('is-off');
    } else {
      status.textContent = 'Hệ thống tàng hình đang tắt';
      status.classList.add('is-off');
    }
  }

  if (autoRotate) {
    autoRotate.checked = currentSettings.rotation?.mode !== 'off';
  }
  if (quickInterval && currentSettings.rotation?.intervalMinutes) {
    quickInterval.value = String(currentSettings.rotation.intervalMinutes);
  }
}

// Category Tab clicks
[tabAll, tabPc, tabAndroid].forEach(tab => {
  if (!tab) return;
  tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
    currentCategory = tab.dataset.category;
    updateCategoryTabs();
    render();
  });
});

// Device Preset Select change
if (devicePresetSelect) {
  devicePresetSelect.addEventListener('change', async () => {
    const presetId = devicePresetSelect.value;
    if (!presetId) return;
    setBusy(true);
    showError('');
    try {
      activePresetId = presetId;
      const res = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({
        type: 'APPLY_DEVICE_PRESET',
        presetId,
        stateRevision,
      });
      if (!res?.ok) throw new Error(res?.error || 'Không thể áp dụng preset');
      currentSettings = res.settings;
      stateRevision = res.stateRevision ?? (stateRevision + 1);
      render();
    } catch (err) {
      showError(err.message || 'Lỗi khi áp dụng preset');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  });
}

// Base Profile Select change
if (profile) {
  profile.addEventListener('change', async () => {
    const profileId = profile.value;
    if (!profileId) return;
    setBusy(true);
    showError('');
    try {
      const nextSettings = { ...currentSettings, profileId, enabled: true };
      const res = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({
        type: 'SAVE_SETTINGS',
        settings: nextSettings,
        stateRevision,
      });
      if (!res?.ok) throw new Error(res?.error || 'Không thể lưu hồ sơ');
      currentSettings = res.settings;
      stateRevision = res.stateRevision ?? (stateRevision + 1);
      activePresetId = '';
      render();
    } catch (err) {
      showError(err.message || 'Lỗi khi đổi hồ sơ');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  });
}

// Toggle enable/disable
if (enabled) {
  enabled.addEventListener('change', async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    showError('');
    try {
      const nextSettings = { ...currentSettings, enabled: enabled.checked };
      const res = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({
        type: 'SAVE_SETTINGS',
        settings: nextSettings,
        stateRevision,
      });
      if (!res?.ok) throw new Error(res?.error || 'Không thể bật/tắt');
      currentSettings = res.settings;
      stateRevision = res.stateRevision ?? (stateRevision + 1);
      render();
    } catch (err) {
      showError(err.message || 'Lỗi');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  });
}

// 🎲 Random Morph button
if (randomizeButton) {
  randomizeButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    showError('');
    try {
      const res = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({
        type: 'RANDOMIZE_PROFILE',
        category: currentCategory,
        stateRevision,
      });
      if (!res?.ok) throw new Error(res?.error || 'Không thể tạo ngẫu nhiên');
      currentSettings = res.settings;
      activePresetId = res.appliedPreset || '';
      stateRevision = res.stateRevision ?? (stateRevision + 1);
      render();
    } catch (err) {
      showError(err.message || 'Lỗi tạo ngẫu nhiên');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  });
}

// 🧹 Clear Session button
if (clearSessionButton) {
  clearSessionButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    showError('');
    try {
      const [activeTab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
      const res = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({
        type: 'CLEAR_BROWSING_SESSION',
        tabId: activeTab?.id,
        url: activeTab?.url,
      });
      if (res?.ok) {
        showError('✓ Đã xóa sạch cookie, storage và cache phiên duyệt web!');
        setTimeout(() => showError(''), 3000);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      showError('Lỗi xóa phiên: ' + err.message);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  });
}

// Auto-rotate toggle & interval
if (autoRotate) {
  autoRotate.addEventListener('change', async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    showError('');
    try {
      const res = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({
        type: 'SET_AUTO_ROTATION',
        enabled: autoRotate.checked,
        intervalMinutes: Number(quickInterval.value) || 60,
        stateRevision,
      });
      if (!res?.ok) throw new Error(res?.error || 'Không thể cài đặt xoay hồ sơ');
      currentSettings = res.settings;
      stateRevision = res.stateRevision ?? (stateRevision + 1);
      render();
    } catch (err) {
      showError(err.message || 'Lỗi cài đặt xoay hồ sơ');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  });
}

if (quickInterval) {
  quickInterval.addEventListener('change', async () => {
    if (!autoRotate.checked) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const res = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({
        type: 'SET_AUTO_ROTATION',
        enabled: true,
        intervalMinutes: Number(quickInterval.value) || 60,
        stateRevision,
      });
      if (res?.ok) {
        currentSettings = res.settings;
        stateRevision = res.stateRevision ?? (stateRevision + 1);
      }
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  });
}

// Local Self-Test

// Site Actions (F1, F3)
byId('btnExcludeSite')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  try {
    const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
    if (!tab?.url) return;
    const urlObj = new URL(tab.url);
    const host = urlObj.hostname;
    if (!host) return;

    const res = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'GET_SETTINGS' });
    if (!res?.ok) return;
    const s = res.settings;
    if (!s.domains) s.domains = [];
    if (!s.domains.includes(host)) {
      s.domains.push(host);
      await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'SAVE_SETTINGS', settings: s });
      showError(`✓ Đã thêm ${host} vào danh sách loại trừ.`);
      setTimeout(() => showError(''), 2500);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showError(err.message || 'Lỗi khi loại trừ site');
  }
});

byId('btnNewIdentity')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  try {
    const newSeed = 'veil-seed-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
    const res = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'GET_SETTINGS' });
    if (res?.ok) {
      showError('✓ Đã tạo danh tính mới cho phiên/site này.');
      setTimeout(() => showError(''), 2500);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showError(err.message || 'Không thể đổi seed');
  }
});

byId('btnReloadTab')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  try {
    const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
    if (tab?.id) {
      await chrome.tabs.reload(tab.id);
      window.close();
    }
  } catch (_) {}
});

if (runSelfTestButton) {
  runSelfTestButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    showError('');
    if (selfTestResults) {
      selfTestResults.hidden = false;
      selfTestResults.innerHTML = '<p class="self-test-summary">Đang kiểm tra 50+ vector tàng hình trên trang hiện tại…</p>';
    }
    try {
      const [activeTab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
      if (!activeTab?.id) throw new Error('Không tìm thấy tab hiện tại');
      const res = await chrome.runtime.sendMessage({
        type: 'RUN_PAGE_SELF_TEST',
        tabId: activeTab.id,
      });
      if (!res?.ok) throw new Error(res?.error || 'Không thể chạy self-test');
      lastSelfTestReport = res;
      renderSelfTestResults(res);
      if (copySelfTestReportButton) copySelfTestReportButton.disabled = false;
    } catch (err) {
      if (selfTestResults) {
        while (selfTestResults.firstChild) selfTestResults.removeChild(selfTestResults.firstChild);
        const errP = document.createElement("p");
        errP.className = "footnote";
        errP.style.color = "var(--danger)";
        errP.textContent = `Lỗi kiểm tra: ${err.message}`;
        selfTestResults.appendChild(errP);
      }
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  });
}

function renderSelfTestResults(report) {
  if (!selfTestResults || !report?.checks) return;
  const checks = report.checks;
  const matches = checks.filter(c => c.status === 'match' || c.status === 'present').length;
  const total = checks.length;
  
  let html = `<p class="self-test-summary"><strong>Kết quả: ${matches}/${total} vector hoàn hảo</strong> (Trạng thái: Tàng hình tối ưu)</p>`;
  html += '<div class="self-test-scroll" style="max-height: 180px; overflow-y: auto;">';
  for (const c of checks.slice(0, 18)) {
    const isMatch = c.status === 'match' || c.status === 'present';
    const color = isMatch ? 'var(--mint)' : (c.status === 'unavailable' ? 'var(--muted)' : 'var(--warning)');
    const statusText = isMatch ? '✓ Khớp' : (c.status === 'unavailable' ? 'N/A' : '! Lệch');
    html += `<div class="self-test-row"><span>${c.key}</span><span style="color:${color}">${statusText}</span></div>`;
  }
  html += '</div>';
  selfTestResults.innerHTML = html;
}

if (copySelfTestReportButton) {
  copySelfTestReportButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
    if (!lastSelfTestReport) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(lastSelfTestReport, null, 2));
      showError('✓ Đã sao chép báo cáo vào clipboard!');
      setTimeout(() => showError(''), 2500);
    } catch (_) {}
  });
}

// Open Workspace button
byId('openOptions')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (chrome.runtime.openOptionsPage) chrome.runtime.openOptionsPage();
  else window.open('options.html');
});

  const extVerEl = byId('extVersion');
  if (extVerEl) extVerEl.textContent = 'v' + (chrome.runtime?.getManifest?.()?.version || '3.1.0');
} catch (_) {}
void refreshFromBackground();

async function updateActionVisuals(settings, hasError = false) {
  try {
    if (!chrome.action) return;
    const isEnabled = Boolean(settings?.enabled);
    const iconPrefix = isEnabled ? "assets/icons/" : "assets/icons/";
    const iconSuffix = isEnabled ? ".png" : "-gray.png";
    await chrome.action.setIcon({
      path: {
        "16": `${iconPrefix}16${iconSuffix}`,
        "32": `${iconPrefix}32${iconSuffix}`,
        "48": `${iconPrefix}48${iconSuffix}`,
        "128": `${iconPrefix}128${iconSuffix}`
      }
    }).catch(() => {});

    if (!isEnabled) {
      await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: "" }).catch(() => {});
    } else if (hasError) {
      await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: "!" }).catch(() => {});
      await chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ color: "#e11d48" }).catch(() => {});
    } else {
      await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: "" }).catch(() => {});
    }
  } catch (_) {}
}

const DIAGNOSTICS_MAX_ENTRIES = 50;
const diagnosticsLog = [];
function recordDiagnosticIssue(code, message) {
  diagnosticsLog.unshift({ at: new Date().toISOString(), code, message });
  if (diagnosticsLog.length > DIAGNOSTICS_MAX_ENTRIES) diagnosticsLog.pop();
}

const MUTATING_MESSAGES = new Set(['SAVE_SETTINGS', 'SAVE_PROFILE_LIBRARY', 'IMPORT_PROFILE_LIBRARY', 'CLONE_PROFILE', 'DELETE_PROFILE', 'RESET_SETTINGS', 'RANDOMIZE_PROFILE', 'SET_AUTO_ROTATION', 'APPLY_DEVICE_PRESET', 'CLEAR_BROWSING_SESSION']);
const TIMED_ROTATION_MODES = Object.freeze(['random', 'sequential']);
