function stableJson(value) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(stableJson).join(',')}]`;
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    return `{${Object.keys(value).sort().map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${stableJson(value[key])}`).join(',')}}`;
  }
  return JSON.stringify(value);
}

function isJsonUnderByteLimit(value, maxBytes) {
  try {
    const json = JSON.stringify(value);
    if (typeof json !== 'string') return false;
    return new TextEncoder().encode(json).byteLength <= maxBytes;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

async function getStateRevision() {
  try {
    const stored = await chrome.storage.local.get(STATE_REVISION_KEY);
    const revision = stored?.[STATE_REVISION_KEY];
    return Number.isSafeInteger(revision) && revision >= 0 ? revision : 0;
  } catch {
    return 0;
  }
}

async function publishStateChanged(reason = 'settings-applied') {
  const previous = await getStateRevision();
  const revision = previous < Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER ? previous + 1 : 1;
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ [STATE_REVISION_KEY]: revision });
  // Broadcast is best-effort. The durable revision remains authoritative even
  // if a popup closes or a browser fork does not implement runtime messaging.
  if (typeof chrome.runtime?.sendMessage === 'function') {
    try {
      const pending = chrome.runtime.sendMessage({
        type: 'VEIL_STATE_CHANGED',
        revision,
        reason,
        at: new Date().toISOString(),
      });
      if (pending && typeof pending.catch === 'function') void pending.catch(() => {});
    } catch { /* state is already committed; do not roll back a successful apply */ }
  }
  return revision;
}

function cloneSettings(settings) {
  try { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(settings)); }
  catch { return null; }
}

async function writeSettingsSnapshot(settings) {
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ [SETTINGS_KEY]: settings });
}
const LEGACY_ALARM_NAMES = Object.freeze(['renew-user-agent', 'update-remote-list', 'update-latest-browser-versions']);
const runExclusive = createSerialQueue();
const DETECTED_CAPABILITIES = detectApiCapabilities(chrome, globalThis.navigator);
let pageInjectionStrategy = DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.initialStrategy.page;
let effectiveHeaderStrategy = DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.initialStrategy.header;
let effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback = DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities.matchOriginAsFallback;
let lastApplyReport = { at: null, attemptedTabs: 0, appliedTabs: 0, notApplicableTabs: 0, failedTabs: 0, issue: null, error: null };
let lastAppliedSettings = null;
let externalSettingsReconcileQueued = false;
let externalSettingsChangeVersion = 0;

function detectRuntimeChromiumMajor() {
  const userAgent = globalThis.navigator?.userAgent;
  if (typeof userAgent !== 'string') return null;
  const match = /(?:Chrome|Chromium)\/(\d+)(?:\.|\s|$)/u.exec(userAgent);
  if (!match) return null;
  const major = Number(match[1]);
  return Number.isSafeInteger(major) && major > 0 ? major : null;
}

function detectRuntimeBrowserFamily() {
  return DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.identity.family;
}

function currentCapabilityReport() {
  return makeCapabilityReport(DETECTED_CAPABILITIES, {
    header: effectiveHeaderStrategy,
    page: pageInjectionStrategy,
    matchOriginAsFallback: effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback,
  });
}

function readBundledLauncherLibrary() {
  if (!LAUNCHER_PROFILE_CONFIG || LAUNCHER_PROFILE_CONFIG.format !== 'veil-launcher-profile-bundle'
      || LAUNCHER_PROFILE_CONFIG.version !== 1 || !LAUNCHER_PROFILE_CONFIG.library) return null;
  try {
    const library = normalizeProfileLibrary(LAUNCHER_PROFILE_CONFIG.library, { strict: true });
    const active = library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === library.activeProfileId);
    return active ? library : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function assertNotLauncherManagedProfiles() {
  if (readBundledLauncherLibrary()) {
    throw new InputValidationError('This browser instance uses a static profile bundle. Edit profile identity, seed, mode and site assignments with the desktop launcher, then relaunch this profile.');
  }
}

async function loadSettings() {
  const stored = await chrome.storage.local.get([SETTINGS_KEY, PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY, ...LEGACY_SETTINGS_KEYS]);
  const hasCurrent = Object.hasOwn(stored, SETTINGS_KEY) && stored[SETTINGS_KEY] != null;
  const legacyValue = LEGACY_SETTINGS_KEYS.map((key) => stored[key]).find((value) => value != null);
  const bundledLibrary = readBundledLauncherLibrary();
  const hasExistingValue = hasCurrent || legacyValue != null || stored[PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY] != null;
  if (!hasExistingValue) {
    if (bundledLibrary) {
      const active = bundledLibrary.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === bundledLibrary.activeProfileId);
      // A launcher-generated extension lives in this profile's fresh, isolated
      // user-data-dir. Its presence is an explicit launch action, so initialize
      // the selected profile enabled. Once any settings exist, never overwrite
      // user changes with the build-time definition on later worker restarts.
      const bundledSettings = normalizeSettings({
        ...createFirstRunSettings(DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.identity),
        enabled: true,
        profileId: active.baseProfileId,
        domainMode: 'blocklist',
        domains: [],
        overrides: settingsOverridesFromLibrary(bundledLibrary),
      }, { strict: true });
      await chrome.storage.local.set({
        [SETTINGS_KEY]: bundledSettings,
        [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: bundledLibrary,
      });
      return bundledSettings;
    }
  }
  let raw = hasCurrent
    ? stored[SETTINGS_KEY]
    : legacyValue ?? createFirstRunSettings(DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.identity);
  // Before 1.3.0, an existing partial record that omitted `enabled` normalized
  // to true. Preserve that behaviour only for stored/migrated records; fresh
  // installs and explicit Reset use an explicit disabled value.
  if (hasExistingValue && raw && typeof raw === 'object' && !Array.isArray(raw) && !Object.hasOwn(raw, 'enabled')) {
    raw = { ...raw, enabled: DEFAULT_SETTINGS.enabled };
  }
  let normalized = normalizeSettings(raw);
  if (bundledLibrary) {
    // Launcher-managed profile definitions are authoritative for each launch.
    // Preserve user-managed enable/blocklist/rotation settings, but synchronize
    // profile assignment mapping and library from the fresh bundle to avoid a
    // seed/profile mismatch with the static document_start script.
    const active = bundledLibrary.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === bundledLibrary.activeProfileId);
    if (active) normalized = normalizeSettings({
      ...normalized,
      profileId: active.baseProfileId,
      overrides: settingsOverridesFromLibrary(bundledLibrary),
      rotation: { ...normalized.rotation, mode: 'off', profileIds: [] },
    }, { strict: true });
    if (JSON.stringify(stored[PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY] ?? null) !== JSON.stringify(bundledLibrary)) {
      await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: bundledLibrary });
    }
  }
  // Copy legacy configuration into the new product namespace before using it.
  // The old key is retained as a recovery copy and is never deleted automatically.
  if (!hasCurrent || JSON.stringify(raw) !== JSON.stringify(normalized)) {
    await writeSettingsSnapshot(normalized);
  }
  return normalized;
}

async function getProfileLibrary(settingsHint = null) {
  const stored = await chrome.storage.local.get(PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY);
  if (stored[PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY] != null) {
    try {
      const normalized = normalizeProfileLibrary(stored[PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]);
      if (JSON.stringify(normalized) !== JSON.stringify(stored[PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY])) {
        await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: normalized });
      }
      return normalized;
    } catch {
      // Preserve the raw value in a separate recovery key before using a safe
      // in-memory fallback. Never overwrite the invalid source silently.
      try {
        const recovery = await chrome.storage.local.get(PROFILE_LIBRARY_RECOVERY_KEY);
        if (recovery[PROFILE_LIBRARY_RECOVERY_KEY] == null) {
          await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_RECOVERY_KEY]: stored[PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY] });
        }
      } catch { /* diagnostics reports that persistent recovery could not be guaranteed */ }
      const settings = settingsHint ?? await loadSettings();
      return createDefaultProfileLibrary(settings.profileId, settings.overrides);
    }
  }
  const settings = settingsHint ?? await loadSettings();
  const library = createDefaultProfileLibrary(settings.profileId, settings.overrides);
  await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: library });
  return library;
}

function profileForBase(library, baseProfileId) {
  const active = library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === library.activeProfileId);
  if (active?.baseProfileId === baseProfileId) return active;
  return library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.baseProfileId === baseProfileId) ?? null;
}

function settingsOverridesFromLibrary(library) {
  return library.assignments.map((assignment) => {
    const profile = library.profiles.find((item) => item.id === assignment.profileId);
    return profile ? { domain: assignment.domain, profileId: profile.baseProfileId } : null;
  }).filter(Boolean);
}

async function reconcileProfileLibraryWithSettings(settings, existingLibrary = null) {
  const library = existingLibrary ?? await getProfileLibrary(settings);
  const assignments = settings.overrides.map((override) => {
    const existing = library.assignments.find((entry) => entry.domain === override.domain);
    const existingRecord = existing ? library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === existing.profileId) : null;
    const matching = existingRecord?.baseProfileId === override.profileId
      ? existingRecord
      : library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.baseProfileId === override.profileId);
    return { domain: override.domain, profileId: matching?.id ?? override.profileId };
  });
  const activeRecord = library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === library.activeProfileId);
  const nextActive = activeRecord?.baseProfileId === settings.profileId
    ? activeRecord.id
    : library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.baseProfileId === settings.profileId)?.id ?? library.activeProfileId;
  const next = normalizeProfileLibrary({ ...library, activeProfileId: nextActive, assignments });
  if (JSON.stringify(next) !== JSON.stringify(library)) {
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: next });
  }
  return next;
}

async function applyWebRtcPrivacyPolicy(profile) {
  const privacy = chrome.privacy?.network?.webRTCIPHandlingPolicy;
  if (!privacy || typeof privacy.clear !== 'function' || typeof privacy.set !== 'function') return 'unavailable';
  try {
    const permissionGranted = typeof chrome.permissions?.contains === 'function'
      ? await chrome.permissions.contains({ permissions: ['privacy'] })
      : false;
    if (!permissionGranted) return profile?.surfaces?.webrtc ? 'permission-required' : 'unavailable';
    if (profile?.surfaces?.webrtc) {
      await privacy.set({ value: 'default_public_interface_only' });
      return 'browser-wide-strict';
    }
    await privacy.clear({});
    return 'cleared-to-browser-default';
  } catch {
    return 'unavailable';
  }
}

async function updateContentScripts(settings) {
  const scripting = chrome.scripting;
  const capabilities = DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities;
  const requestOriginFallback = Boolean(capabilities.matchOriginAsFallback);
  const launcherProfileBundle = Boolean(readBundledLauncherLibrary());
  let registrations = buildContentScriptRegistrations(settings, { matchOriginAsFallback: requestOriginFallback, launcherProfileBundle });
  effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback = false;

  // Mobile Chromium forks do not always expose the complete dynamic scripting
  // API. Prefer guaranteed document_start registration, then use a guarded
  // navigation-time MAIN-world injection fallback when the required methods exist.
  if (!capabilities.dynamicContentScripts || !capabilities.scriptingExecuteMain) {
    const canReadRegistry = typeof scripting?.getRegisteredContentScripts === 'function';
    const canRemoveRegistry = typeof scripting?.unregisterContentScripts === 'function';
    const hasPartialRegistryApi = canReadRegistry
      || canRemoveRegistry
      || typeof scripting?.registerContentScripts === 'function';
    // If a fork exposes only part of the registry API, we cannot prove that an
    // old persistent script is absent. Do not add a second navigation injector
    // on top of a registration we cannot inspect/remove.
    if (hasPartialRegistryApi && !(canReadRegistry && canRemoveRegistry)) {
      pageInjectionStrategy = 'unavailable';
      return { count: 0, strategy: pageInjectionStrategy, issue: 'PARTIAL_SCRIPT_REGISTRY_API' };
    }
    if (canReadRegistry && canRemoveRegistry) {
      try {
        const existing = await scripting.getRegisteredContentScripts();
        const oldOwned = existing.filter((script) => isOwnedContentScriptId(script.id));
        if (oldOwned.length) await scripting.unregisterContentScripts({ ids: oldOwned.map((script) => script.id) });
      } catch {
        pageInjectionStrategy = 'unavailable';
        return { count: 0, strategy: pageInjectionStrategy, issue: 'SCRIPT_CLEANUP_FAILED' };
      }
    }
    pageInjectionStrategy = capabilities.scriptingExecuteMain && capabilities.webNavigationCommitted
      ? 'navigation-fallback'
      : 'unavailable';
    effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback = false;
    return {
      count: 0,
      strategy: pageInjectionStrategy,
      issue: pageInjectionStrategy === 'navigation-fallback'
        ? 'DOCUMENT_START_UNAVAILABLE'
        : 'PAGE_SCRIPT_API_UNAVAILABLE',
    };
  }

  let existing;
  try {
    existing = await scripting.getRegisteredContentScripts();
  } catch {
    // A callable API may still be unimplemented or permission-denied by a fork.
    // Without a trusted registry snapshot, neither replacement nor fallback is safe.
    pageInjectionStrategy = 'unavailable';
    lastApplyReport = {
      ...lastApplyReport,
      at: new Date().toISOString(),
      error: 'SCRIPT_REGISTRY_QUERY_FAILED',
      strategies: { header: effectiveHeaderStrategy, page: pageInjectionStrategy },
      issues: [...new Set([...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? []), 'SCRIPT_REGISTRY_QUERY_FAILED'])],
    };
    throw new Error('SCRIPT_REGISTRY_QUERY_FAILED');
  }
  const oldOwned = existing.filter((script) => isOwnedContentScriptId(script.id));
  if (!oldOwned.length && !registrations.length) {
    pageInjectionStrategy = 'document-start';
    effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback = requestOriginFallback;
    return { count: 0, strategy: pageInjectionStrategy, issue: null };
  }

  try {
    if (oldOwned.length) await scripting.unregisterContentScripts({ ids: oldOwned.map((script) => script.id) });
    if (registrations.length) await scripting.registerContentScripts(registrations);
    pageInjectionStrategy = 'document-start';
    if (requestOriginFallback && registrations.length) {
      try {
        const confirmed = await scripting.getRegisteredContentScripts();
        const byId = new Map(confirmed.filter((script) => isOwnedContentScriptId(script.id)).map((script) => [script.id, script]));
        effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback = registrations.every((registration) =>
          byId.get(registration.id)?.matchOriginAsFallback === true);
      } catch {
        effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback = false;
      }
      if (!effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback) {
        return { count: registrations.length, strategy: pageInjectionStrategy, issue: 'MATCH_ORIGIN_AS_FALLBACK_NOT_CONFIRMED' };
      }
    }
    const issue = registrations.length && !effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback
      ? 'MATCH_ORIGIN_AS_FALLBACK_UNAVAILABLE'
      : null;
    return { count: registrations.length, strategy: pageInjectionStrategy, issue };
  } catch (registrationError) {
    // A transient/duplicate/validation failure must remain transactional. Only
    // downgrade when the browser explicitly signals an unsupported MAIN-world
    // or registration option; otherwise restore the previous known-good set.
    const message = typeof registrationError?.message === 'string' ? registrationError.message : '';
    const unsupportedOriginFallback = requestOriginFallback
      && /matchOriginAsFallback/iu.test(message)
      && /(?:unsupported|not supported|unknown|invalid|unrecognized|unexpected)/iu.test(message);
    if (unsupportedOriginFallback) {
      try {
        const afterFailure = await scripting.getRegisteredContentScripts();
        const remainingOwned = afterFailure.filter((script) => isOwnedContentScriptId(script.id));
        if (remainingOwned.length) await scripting.unregisterContentScripts({ ids: remainingOwned.map((script) => script.id) });
        registrations = buildContentScriptRegistrations(settings, { matchOriginAsFallback: false, launcherProfileBundle });
        if (registrations.length) await scripting.registerContentScripts(registrations);
        pageInjectionStrategy = 'document-start';
        effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback = false;
        return {
          count: registrations.length,
          strategy: pageInjectionStrategy,
          issue: 'MATCH_ORIGIN_AS_FALLBACK_UNAVAILABLE',
        };
      } catch {
        try {
          const afterFallbackFailure = await scripting.getRegisteredContentScripts();
          const remainingOwned = afterFallbackFailure.filter((script) => isOwnedContentScriptId(script.id));
          if (remainingOwned.length) await scripting.unregisterContentScripts({ ids: remainingOwned.map((script) => script.id) });
          if (oldOwned.length) await scripting.registerContentScripts(oldOwned);
        } catch {
          throw new Error('CONTENT_SCRIPT_REPLACEMENT_FAILED');
        }
        throw new Error('CONTENT_SCRIPT_REGISTRATION_FAILED');
      }
    }
    const unsupportedWorld = /(?:unsupported|not supported|unknown property|invalid value|unexpected property)[^\n]{0,100}world|world[^\n]{0,100}(?:unsupported|not supported|unknown|invalid)|register(?:ed)?content(?:scripts)?[^\n]{0,100}(?:unsupported|not supported)/iu.test(message);
    if (!unsupportedWorld) {
      try {
        const afterFailure = await scripting.getRegisteredContentScripts();
        const remainingOwned = afterFailure.filter((script) => isOwnedContentScriptId(script.id));
        if (remainingOwned.length) await scripting.unregisterContentScripts({ ids: remainingOwned.map((script) => script.id) });
        if (oldOwned.length) await scripting.registerContentScripts(oldOwned);
        effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback = oldOwned.length > 0
          && oldOwned.every((script) => script.matchOriginAsFallback === true);
      } catch {
        throw new Error('CONTENT_SCRIPT_REPLACEMENT_FAILED');
      }
      throw new Error('CONTENT_SCRIPT_REGISTRATION_FAILED');
    }

    try {
      const afterFailure = await scripting.getRegisteredContentScripts();
      const remainingOwned = afterFailure.filter((script) => isOwnedContentScriptId(script.id));
      if (remainingOwned.length) await scripting.unregisterContentScripts({ ids: remainingOwned.map((script) => script.id) });
    } catch {
      throw new Error('CONTENT_SCRIPT_CLEANUP_FAILED');
    }
    pageInjectionStrategy = capabilities.scriptingExecuteMain && capabilities.webNavigationCommitted
      ? 'navigation-fallback'
      : 'unavailable';
    effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback = false;
    return {
      count: 0,
      strategy: pageInjectionStrategy,
      issue: pageInjectionStrategy === 'navigation-fallback' ? 'DYNAMIC_SCRIPT_REGISTRATION_FALLBACK' : 'PAGE_SCRIPT_API_UNAVAILABLE',
    };
  }
}

async function updateDnrRules(settings) {
  if (!DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities.dynamicNetRequest) {
    effectiveHeaderStrategy = 'unavailable';
    return { count: 0, strategy: effectiveHeaderStrategy, issue: 'DNR_API_UNAVAILABLE' };
  }

  let currentRules;
  try {
    currentRules = await chrome.declarativeNetRequest.getDynamicRules();
  } catch {
    // Without a rule snapshot we cannot know whether the old profile remains
    // active. Abort this settings apply rather than change JS while headers may
    // still represent an unknown previous profile.
    effectiveHeaderStrategy = 'unavailable';
    pageInjectionStrategy = 'unavailable';
    lastApplyReport = {
      ...lastApplyReport,
      at: new Date().toISOString(),
      error: 'DNR_RULES_READ_FAILED',
      strategies: { header: effectiveHeaderStrategy, page: pageInjectionStrategy },
      issues: [...new Set([...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? []), 'DNR_RULES_READ_FAILED'])],
    };
    throw new Error('DNR_RULES_READ_FAILED');
  }
  // Remove only our reserved ID range; unrelated extension rules are untouched.
  const removeRuleIds = currentRules.filter((rule) => rule.id >= 1 && rule.id <= 1199).map((rule) => rule.id);
  const profileLibrary = await getProfileLibrary(settings);
  const addRules = buildDnrRules(settings, PROFILES, DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.identity.nativeOs, profileLibrary);
  try {
    await chrome.declarativeNetRequest.updateDynamicRules({ removeRuleIds, addRules });
  } catch {
    // Some mobile forks expose the DNR namespace but implement only part of it.
    // Keep page scripting alive rather than aborting the whole settings apply.
    // Best-effort cleanup prevents a previous profile from remaining active after
    // a replacement failure; unrelated rule IDs are never touched.
    let issue = 'DNR_RULE_UPDATE_FAILED';
    if (removeRuleIds.length) {
      try {
        await chrome.declarativeNetRequest.updateDynamicRules({ removeRuleIds, addRules: [] });
      } catch {
        issue = 'DNR_RULE_CLEANUP_FAILED';
      }
    }
    effectiveHeaderStrategy = 'unavailable';
    if (issue === 'DNR_RULE_CLEANUP_FAILED') {
      // Old request rules may still be live. Do not apply a different JS profile
      // on top of unknown headers; let persistAndApply roll settings back.
      lastApplyReport = {
        ...lastApplyReport,
        at: new Date().toISOString(),
        error: issue,
        strategies: { header: effectiveHeaderStrategy, page: pageInjectionStrategy },
        issues: [...new Set([...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? []), issue])],
      };
      throw new Error(issue);
    }
    return { count: 0, strategy: effectiveHeaderStrategy, issue };
  }
  effectiveHeaderStrategy = 'declarative-rules';
  return { count: addRules.length, strategy: effectiveHeaderStrategy, issue: null };
}

async function updateOpenTabs(settings) {
  // There is intentionally no page-reachable manager or hot-patch handle. A
  // document that has already executed a profile must be reloaded to change or
  // remove those JavaScript descriptors. Update DNR/registrations immediately,
  // but report the reload requirement rather than injecting a second patch on
  // top of the first one.
  if (!DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities.tabsQuery) {
    return { attemptedTabs: 0, appliedTabs: 0, notApplicableTabs: 0, failedTabs: 0, issue: 'TAB_QUERY_UNAVAILABLE' };
  }
  let tabs;
  try {
    tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ url: ['http://*/*', 'https://*/*'] });
  } catch {
    return { attemptedTabs: 0, appliedTabs: 0, notApplicableTabs: 0, failedTabs: 0, issue: 'TAB_QUERY_FAILED' };
  }
  const liveTabs = (Array.isArray(tabs) ? tabs : []).filter((tab) => Number.isInteger(tab.id)
    && tab.discarded !== true && typeof tab.url === 'string' && /^https?:/iu.test(tab.url)
    && !isUrlReserved(tab.url));
  return {
    attemptedTabs: liveTabs.length,
    appliedTabs: 0,
    notApplicableTabs: 0,
    failedTabs: 0,
    issue: liveTabs.length ? 'PAGE_RELOAD_REQUIRED' : null,
  };
}

async function syncRotationAlarm(settings, { preserveExisting = false } = {}) {
  const shouldRotate = settings.enabled && TIMED_ROTATION_MODES.includes(settings.rotation.mode);
  // An absent alarms API is irrelevant while rotation is off.
  if (!shouldRotate) {
    if (typeof chrome.alarms?.clear === 'function') {
      try { await chrome.alarms.clear(ROTATION_ALARM); } catch { /* optional capability */ }
    }
    return true;
  }
  if (!DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities.alarms) return false;
  // Some Chromium builds do not reliably preserve alarms across restart or
  // extension updates. A service-worker wake checks the named alarm first so
  // it does not reset an existing countdown on every cold start.
  if (preserveExisting && typeof chrome.alarms?.get === 'function') {
    try {
      if (await chrome.alarms.get(ROTATION_ALARM)) return true;
    } catch {
      // A named create replaces the same alarm, so re-creating is a safe fallback.
    }
  }
  await chrome.alarms.create(ROTATION_ALARM, {
    delayInMinutes: settings.rotation.intervalMinutes,
    periodInMinutes: settings.rotation.intervalMinutes,
  });
  return true;
}

async function applySettings(settings, { previousSettings = null } = {}) {
  const issues = new Set();
  const updateProfileLayers = profileApplicationChanged(previousSettings, settings);
  const updateAlarm = rotationChanged(previousSettings, settings);
  let dnr = { count: lastApplyReport.dynamicRuleCount ?? 0, issue: null };
  let scripts = { count: lastApplyReport.registeredScriptCount ?? 0, issue: null };
  let tabs = { attemptedTabs: 0, appliedTabs: 0, notApplicableTabs: 0, failedTabs: 0, issue: null };

  if (updateProfileLayers) {
    const dnrResult = await updateDnrRules(settings);
    dnr = dnrResult;
    if (dnr.issue) issues.add(dnr.issue);
    const scriptResult = await updateContentScripts(settings);
    scripts = scriptResult;
    if (scripts.issue) issues.add(scripts.issue);
    const tabResult = await updateOpenTabs(settings);
    tabs = tabResult;
    if (tabs.issue) issues.add(tabs.issue);
  }

  const rotationAvailable = updateAlarm ? await syncRotationAlarm(settings) : true;
  if (!rotationAvailable) {
    issues.add('ALARMS_UNAVAILABLE');
    if (TIMED_ROTATION_MODES.includes(settings.rotation.mode)) {
      throw new Error('ROTATION_ALARMS_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
  }

  lastApplyReport = {
    at: new Date().toISOString(),
    attemptedTabs: tabs.attemptedTabs,
    appliedTabs: tabs.appliedTabs,
    notApplicableTabs: tabs.notApplicableTabs,
    failedTabs: tabs.failedTabs,
    dynamicRuleCount: dnr.count,
    registeredScriptCount: scripts.count,
    skippedLayers: {
      requestRules: !updateProfileLayers,
      contentScripts: !updateProfileLayers,
      openTabs: !updateProfileLayers,
      rotationAlarm: !updateAlarm,
    },
    strategies: { header: effectiveHeaderStrategy, page: pageInjectionStrategy },
    issues: [...issues],
    issue: issues.values().next().value ?? null,
    error: issues.values().next().value ?? null,
  };
  lastAppliedSettings = cloneSettings(settings);
}

function profileApplicationChanged(previous, next) {
  if (!previous) return true;
  return ['enabled', 'profileId', 'domainMode', 'domains', 'overrides']
    .some((key) => JSON.stringify(previous[key]) !== JSON.stringify(next[key]));
}

function rotationChanged(previous, next) {
  if (!previous) return true;
  return JSON.stringify(previous.rotation) !== JSON.stringify(next.rotation) || previous.enabled !== next.enabled;
}

async function persistAndApply(nextSettings, { forceProfileApply = false } = {}) {
  if (TIMED_ROTATION_MODES.includes(nextSettings.rotation.mode)
      && !DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities.alarms) {
    throw new InputValidationError('This browser cannot schedule background alarms, so timed rotation is unavailable. Manual profile changes still work.');
  }
  const previous = await loadSettings();
  let applyStarted = false;
  try {
    await writeSettingsSnapshot(nextSettings);
    applyStarted = true;
    await applySettings(nextSettings, { previousSettings: forceProfileApply ? null : previous });
    await publishStateChanged('settings-applied');
    return nextSettings;
  } catch (error) {
    const failedApply = { ...lastApplyReport, issues: [...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? [])] };
    const operationIssue = applyStarted ? 'APPLY_FAILED' : 'SETTINGS_PERSIST_FAILED';
    let storageRollbackFailed = false;
    let runtimeRollbackFailed = false;
    try {
      await writeSettingsSnapshot(previous);
    } catch {
      storageRollbackFailed = true;
    }
    // Attempt to return the browser-side rules/scripts to the prior state even
    // when storage reports an error after a possible partial write.
    try {
      await applySettings(previous);
    } catch {
      runtimeRollbackFailed = true;
    }
    const rollbackFailed = storageRollbackFailed || runtimeRollbackFailed;
    const rollbackIssues = [
      ...(storageRollbackFailed ? ['STORAGE_ROLLBACK_FAILED'] : []),
      ...(runtimeRollbackFailed ? ['RUNTIME_ROLLBACK_FAILED'] : []),
      ...(rollbackFailed ? ['ROLLBACK_FAILED'] : [applyStarted ? 'APPLY_FAILED_ROLLED_BACK' : 'SETTINGS_PERSIST_FAILED_ROLLED_BACK']),
    ];
    lastApplyReport = {
      ...lastApplyReport,
      at: new Date().toISOString(),
      error: rollbackFailed ? 'ROLLBACK_FAILED' : 'OPERATION_FAILED',
      issues: [...new Set([...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? []), ...(failedApply.issues ?? []), operationIssue, ...rollbackIssues])],
    };
    // Keep only fixed codes: browser exceptions can contain URLs and internal paths.
    throw error;
  }
}

let sessionStartTime = 0;

async function getOrInitSessionStartTime(forceNew = false) {
  if (!forceNew && sessionStartTime > 0) return sessionStartTime;
  try {
    if (chrome.storage?.session) {
      const stored = await chrome.storage.session.get('veilSessionStartTime');
      if (!forceNew && typeof stored?.veilSessionStartTime === 'number' && stored.veilSessionStartTime > 0) {
        sessionStartTime = stored.veilSessionStartTime;
        return sessionStartTime;
      }
      sessionStartTime = Date.now();
      await chrome.storage.session.set({ veilSessionStartTime: sessionStartTime });
      return sessionStartTime;
    }
  } catch (_) {}
  if (forceNew || sessionStartTime <= 0) sessionStartTime = Date.now();
  return sessionStartTime;
}

async function initialize({ startNewSession = false } = {}) {
  return runExclusive(async () => {
    await getOrInitSessionStartTime(startNewSession);
    // If an installation reused an extension ID, remove stale alarms from the
    // previous product; they are not part of Veil's scheduler.
    if (typeof chrome.alarms?.clear === 'function') {
      await Promise.allSettled(LEGACY_ALARM_NAMES.map((name) => chrome.alarms.clear(name)));
    }
    let settings = await loadSettings();
    let disabledUnsupportedRotation = false;
    if (TIMED_ROTATION_MODES.includes(settings.rotation.mode) && !DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities.alarms) {
      settings = normalizeSettings({
        ...settings,
        rotation: { ...settings.rotation, mode: 'off' },
      }, { strict: true });
      await writeSettingsSnapshot(settings);
      disabledUnsupportedRotation = true;
    }
    if (startNewSession && settings.enabled && settings.rotation.mode === 'session') {
      const profileId = rotateProfile(settings);
      settings = normalizeSettings({ ...settings, profileId }, { strict: true });
      await persistAndApply(settings);
      return;
    }
    await applySettings(settings);
    if (disabledUnsupportedRotation) {
      lastApplyReport = {
        ...lastApplyReport,
        error: 'ALARMS_UNAVAILABLE',
        issues: [...new Set([...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? []), 'ALARMS_UNAVAILABLE'])],
      };
    }
  });
}

function getRotationCandidateIds(settings) {
  const nativeOs = DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.identity.nativeOs;
  const staysOnNativeOs = (profileId) => {
    const candidate = PROFILES[profileId];
    return candidate && (nativeOs === 'unknown' || candidate.os === nativeOs);
  };
  const configured = settings.rotation.profileIds.filter(staysOnNativeOs);
  if (configured.length >= 2) return [...configured];
  if (settings.rotation.profileIds.length > 0 && nativeOs !== 'unknown') return [];
  return compatibleRotationProfileIds(
    settings.profileId,
    PROFILES,
    DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.identity.major,
    nativeOs,
  );
}

async function ensureRotationAlarmOnWorkerStart() {
  return runExclusive(async () => {
    let settings = await loadSettings();
    let disabledUnsupportedRotation = false;
    if (TIMED_ROTATION_MODES.includes(settings.rotation.mode) && !DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities.alarms) {
      settings = normalizeSettings({ ...settings, rotation: { ...settings.rotation, mode: 'off' } }, { strict: true });
      await writeSettingsSnapshot(settings);
      disabledUnsupportedRotation = true;
    }

    const available = await syncRotationAlarm(settings, { preserveExisting: true });
    if (!available && TIMED_ROTATION_MODES.includes(settings.rotation.mode)) {
      throw new Error('ROTATION_ALARMS_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
    if (disabledUnsupportedRotation) {
      lastApplyReport = {
        ...lastApplyReport,
        at: new Date().toISOString(),
        error: 'ALARMS_UNAVAILABLE',
        issues: [...new Set([...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? []), 'ALARMS_UNAVAILABLE'])],
      };
    }
  });
}

function hostnameConditionMatches(condition, hostname) {
  const matchesDomain = (domain) => typeof domain === 'string'
    && (hostname === domain || hostname.endsWith(`.${domain}`));
  if (Array.isArray(condition?.requestDomains) && !condition.requestDomains.some(matchesDomain)) return false;
  if (Array.isArray(condition?.excludedRequestDomains) && condition.excludedRequestDomains.some(matchesDomain)) return false;
  if (!Array.isArray(condition?.resourceTypes) || !condition.resourceTypes.includes('main_frame')) return false;
  if (Array.isArray(condition?.excludedResourceTypes) && condition.excludedResourceTypes.includes('main_frame')) return false;
  // Initiator rules cannot prove a top-level request because the navigation initiator
  // depends on how the user reached the page. Only target-domain rules count here.
  if (Array.isArray(condition?.initiatorDomains)) return false;
  return true;
}

function ruleSetsHeaderValue(rule, headerName, value) {
  return rule?.action?.type === 'modifyHeaders'
    && Array.isArray(rule.action.requestHeaders)
    && rule.action.requestHeaders.some((header) => header?.operation === 'set'
      && header.header === headerName && header.value === value);
}

function ruleSetsUserAgentFor(rule, userAgent) {
  return ruleSetsHeaderValue(rule, 'user-agent', userAgent);
}

function hasExpectedMainFrameHeaderRule(rules, hostname, headerName, value) {
  const matching = rules.filter((rule) => hostnameConditionMatches(rule.condition, hostname));
  const candidates = matching.filter((rule) => ruleSetsHeaderValue(rule, headerName, value));
  candidates.sort((left, right) => right.priority - left.priority);
  const bestCandidate = candidates[0];
  if (!bestCandidate) return false;
  const higherAllowRule = matching.some((rule) => ['allow', 'allowAllRequests'].includes(rule.action?.type)
    && Number.isFinite(rule.priority) && rule.priority >= bestCandidate.priority);
  if (higherAllowRule) return false;
  const highestHeaderRule = matching
    .filter((rule) => rule?.action?.type === 'modifyHeaders'
      && Array.isArray(rule.action.requestHeaders)
      && rule.action.requestHeaders.some((header) => header?.operation === 'set' && header.header === headerName))
    .sort((left, right) => right.priority - left.priority)[0];
  return Boolean(highestHeaderRule && ruleSetsHeaderValue(highestHeaderRule, headerName, value));
}

function hasExpectedMainFrameRule(rules, hostname, profile) {
  const matching = rules.filter((rule) => hostnameConditionMatches(rule.condition, hostname));
  const candidateRules = matching.filter((rule) => ruleSetsUserAgentFor(rule, profile.userAgent));
  candidateRules.sort((left, right) => right.priority - left.priority);
  const bestCandidate = candidateRules[0];
  if (!bestCandidate) return false;
  const higherAllowRule = matching.some((rule) => ['allow', 'allowAllRequests'].includes(rule.action?.type)
    && Number.isFinite(rule.priority) && rule.priority >= bestCandidate.priority);
  if (higherAllowRule) return false;
  const highestProfileRule = matching
    .filter((rule) => rule.action?.type === 'modifyHeaders'
      && Array.isArray(rule.action.requestHeaders)
      && rule.action.requestHeaders.some((header) => header?.operation === 'set' && header.header === 'user-agent'))
    .sort((left, right) => right.priority - left.priority)[0];
  return Boolean(highestProfileRule && ruleSetsUserAgentFor(highestProfileRule, profile.userAgent));
}

function safeSelfTestChecks(checks) {
  const incoming = Array.isArray(checks) ? checks : [];
  const byKey = new Map(incoming
    .filter((item) => item && SELF_TEST_CHECK_KEYS.includes(item.key) && SELF_TEST_STATUSES.has(item.status))
    .map((item) => [item.key, item.status]));
  return SELF_TEST_CHECK_KEYS.map((key) => ({ key, status: byKey.get(key) ?? 'unavailable' }));
}

function makeUnavailableSelfTest(outcome, {
  profileId = null,
  httpRuleSnapshot = 'unavailable',
  acceptLanguageRuleSnapshot = 'unavailable',
} = {}) {
  return {
    ok: true,
    outcome,
    profileId,
    httpRuleSnapshot,
    acceptLanguageRuleSnapshot,
    workerCoverage: 'native-uncontrolled',
    networkRequestsSent: false,
    checks: SELF_TEST_CHECK_KEYS.map((key) => ({ key, status: 'unavailable' })),
  };
}

async function runCurrentPageSelfTest() {
  let tabs;
  try {
    tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true, url: ['http://*/*', 'https://*/*'] });
  } catch {
    return makeUnavailableSelfTest('tab-query-unavailable');
  }
  const tab = tabs.find((candidate) => candidate?.active === true
    && Number.isInteger(candidate.id)
    && typeof candidate.url === 'string'
    && /^https?:/iu.test(candidate.url));
  if (!tab) return makeUnavailableSelfTest('no-active-web-page', { httpRuleSnapshot: 'not-applicable', acceptLanguageRuleSnapshot: 'not-applicable' });

  const settings = await loadSettings();
  if (!settings.enabled) return makeUnavailableSelfTest('profile-controls-disabled', { httpRuleSnapshot: 'not-applicable', acceptLanguageRuleSnapshot: 'not-applicable' });
  const profileId = getProfileIdForUrl(tab.url, settings);
  if (!profileId || !PROFILES[profileId]) return makeUnavailableSelfTest('no-profile-for-this-site', { httpRuleSnapshot: 'not-applicable', acceptLanguageRuleSnapshot: 'not-applicable' });
  const profile = PROFILES[profileId];
  const library = await getProfileLibrary(settings);
  const configuredRecord = profileForUrl(library, tab.url, profileId);
  const intlConfigured = Boolean(configuredRecord
    && configuredRecord.baseProfileId === profileId
    && configuredRecord.mode === 'full'
    && configuredRecord.surfaces?.intl === true);
  const expectedLocale = intlConfigured && configuredRecord.locale ? configuredRecord.locale : null;
  const expectedTimezone = intlConfigured && configuredRecord.timezone ? configuredRecord.timezone : null;
  const acceptLanguageForLocale = (locale) => {
    if (typeof locale !== 'string' || !locale) return '';
    const baseLanguage = locale.split('-')[0];
    return locale === baseLanguage ? locale : `${locale},${baseLanguage};q=0.9`;
  };
  const expectedAcceptLanguage = expectedLocale ? acceptLanguageForLocale(expectedLocale) : '';
  let httpRuleSnapshot = 'unavailable';
  let acceptLanguageRuleSnapshot = expectedAcceptLanguage ? 'unavailable' : 'not-applicable';
  try {
    if (typeof chrome.declarativeNetRequest?.getDynamicRules === 'function') {
      const rules = await chrome.declarativeNetRequest.getDynamicRules();
      const hostname = new URL(tab.url).hostname.toLowerCase();
      httpRuleSnapshot = hasExpectedMainFrameRule(rules, hostname, profile) ? 'rule-present' : 'rule-missing';
      if (expectedAcceptLanguage) {
        acceptLanguageRuleSnapshot = hasExpectedMainFrameHeaderRule(rules, hostname, 'accept-language', expectedAcceptLanguage)
          ? 'rule-present' : 'rule-missing';
      }
    }
  } catch {
    httpRuleSnapshot = 'unavailable';
    if (expectedAcceptLanguage) acceptLanguageRuleSnapshot = 'unavailable';
  }

  const expected = {
    os: profile.os,
    userAgent: profile.userAgent,
    appVersion: profile.userAgent.replace(/^Mozilla\//u, ''),
    vendor: profile.vendor,
    brands: profile.brands.map(({ brand, version }) => ({ brand, version })),
    mobile: profile.mobile,
    uaPlatform: profile.platform,
    uaFullVersion: profile.highEntropy.uaFullVersion,
    fullVersionList: profile.highEntropy.fullVersionList.map(({ brand, version }) => ({ brand, version })),
    locale: expectedLocale,
    timezone: expectedTimezone,
  };
  let rawChecks = [];
  let pageInjectionSucceeded = false;
  let workerCoverage = 'unavailable';
  if (DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities.scriptingExecuteMain && typeof chrome.scripting?.executeScript === 'function') {
    try {
      const results = await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
        target: { tabId: tab.id, frameIds: [0] },
        world: 'MAIN',
        func: runPageSelfTest,
        args: [expected],
      });
      const testResult = results?.find((result) => result?.frameId === 0)?.result
        ?? results?.[0]?.result;
      rawChecks = testResult?.checks ?? [];
      workerCoverage = testResult?.workerCoverage ?? 'unavailable';
      pageInjectionSucceeded = Array.isArray(rawChecks) && rawChecks.length > 0;
    } catch {
      rawChecks = [];
    }
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    outcome: pageInjectionSucceeded ? 'tested' : 'page-script-unavailable',
    profileId,
    httpRuleSnapshot,
    acceptLanguageRuleSnapshot,
    workerCoverage,
    networkRequestsSent: false,
    checks: safeSelfTestChecks(rawChecks),
  };
}

async function diagnostics() {
  const settings = await loadSettings();
  let registeredScripts = [];
  let dynamicRules = [];
  let alarm = null;
  if (typeof chrome.scripting?.getRegisteredContentScripts === 'function') {
    try { registeredScripts = await chrome.scripting.getRegisteredContentScripts(); } catch { /* fixed diagnostic fallback */ }
  }
  if (typeof chrome.declarativeNetRequest?.getDynamicRules === 'function') {
    try { dynamicRules = await chrome.declarativeNetRequest.getDynamicRules(); } catch { /* fixed diagnostic fallback */ }
  }
  if (typeof chrome.alarms?.get === 'function') {
    try { alarm = await chrome.alarms.get(ROTATION_ALARM); } catch { /* fixed diagnostic fallback */ }
  }
  const environment = currentCapabilityReport();
  const profileLibrary = await getProfileLibrary(settings);
  const recoveryState = await chrome.storage.local.get(PROFILE_LIBRARY_RECOVERY_KEY).catch(() => ({}));
  const coverageMatrix = Object.entries(PROFILE_SURFACES).map(([surface, definition]) => ({
    surface,
    modes: [...definition.modes],
    status: definition.status,
    warningKey: definition.warningKey,
  }));
  const profileChecks = profileLibrary.profiles.map((profile) => {
    const validation = validateProfileRecord(profile);
    const coverage = profileCoverage(profile);
    return {
      id: profile.id,
      mode: profile.mode,
      consistencyScore: validation.consistencyScore,
      warnings: validation.warnings,
      contradictions: validation.warnings.filter((warning) => /native remains|native is|mismatch|uncovered|partially implemented/iu.test(warning)),
      validation,
      coverage,
    };
  });
  const activeProfile = profileChecks.find((entry) => entry.id === profileLibrary.activeProfileId) ?? null;
  return {
    stateRevision: await getStateRevision(),
    profileLibrary: {
      schemaVersion: profileLibrary.schemaVersion,
      profileCount: profileLibrary.profiles.length,
      activeProfileId: profileLibrary.activeProfileId,
      activeConsistencyScore: activeProfile?.consistencyScore ?? 0,
      assignmentCount: profileLibrary.assignments.length,
      profiles: profileChecks,
      coverageMatrix,
      recoveryCopyPresent: recoveryState[PROFILE_LIBRARY_RECOVERY_KEY] != null,
      privacyApi: typeof chrome.privacy?.network?.webRTCIPHandlingPolicy?.set === 'function' ? 'available-if-permission-granted' : 'unavailable',
    },
    settings: {
      enabled: settings.enabled,
      profileId: settings.profileId,
      domainMode: settings.domainMode,
      domainCount: settings.domains.length,
      overrideCount: settings.overrides.length,
      rotationMode: settings.rotation.mode,
      rotationPreferredMode: settings.rotation.preferredMode,
      rotationProfileCount: settings.rotation.profileIds.length,
    },
    environment,
    runtime: {
      managedRuleCount: dynamicRules.filter((rule) => rule.id >= 1 && rule.id <= 1199).length,
      registeredScriptCount: registeredScripts.filter((script) => isOwnedContentScriptId(script.id)).length,
      rotationAlarmScheduled: Boolean(alarm),
      api: { ...DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities, matchOriginAsFallback: effectiveMatchOriginAsFallback },
    },
    lastApply: lastApplyReport,
  };
}

async function handleMessage(message) {
  if (!message || typeof message.type !== 'string') throw new InputValidationError('Invalid extension message.');
  if (MUTATING_MESSAGES.has(message.type) && Object.hasOwn(message, 'expectedRevision')) {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(message.expectedRevision) || message.expectedRevision < 0) {
      throw new InputValidationError('The state revision is invalid. Reload the extension workspace and retry.');
    }
    const currentRevision = await getStateRevision();
    if (message.expectedRevision !== currentRevision) throw new StateConflictError();
  }
  
  if (message.type === GET_DIAGNOSTICS_LOG) {
    return { ok: true, log: [...diagnosticsLog], report: lastApplyReport };
  }
  if (message.type === CLEAR_DIAGNOSTICS_LOG) {
    diagnosticsLog.length = 0;
    return { ok: true };
  }

  if (message.type === 'GET_SETTINGS') {
    const settings = await loadSettings();
    return {
      ok: true,
      settings,
      stateRevision: await getStateRevision(),
      launcherManagedProfiles: Boolean(readBundledLauncherLibrary()),
      runtimeChromiumMajor: detectRuntimeChromiumMajor(),
      runtimeBrowserFamily: detectRuntimeBrowserFamily(),
      recommendedProfileId: recommendProfileId(DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.identity),
      environment: currentCapabilityReport(),
      lastApply: lastApplyReport,
      devicePresets: DEVICE_PRESETS,
      timezonePresets: TIMEZONE_PRESETS,
      profiles: Object.values(PROFILES).map(({ id, browser, os, major, architecture, bitness, mobile, userAgent, highEntropy }) => ({
        id, browser, os, major, architecture, bitness, mobile,
        userAgent, fullVersion: highEntropy.uaFullVersion,
      })),
    };
  }
  if (message.type === 'GET_PROFILE_LIBRARY') {
    const settings = await loadSettings();
    return { ok: true, library: await getProfileLibrary(settings), surfaces: PROFILE_SURFACES, modes: PROFILE_MODES, launcherManagedProfiles: Boolean(readBundledLauncherLibrary()) };
  }
  if (message.type === 'SAVE_PROFILE_LIBRARY') {
    const settings = await loadSettings();
    assertNotLauncherManagedProfiles();
    const oldValue = (await chrome.storage.local.get(PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY))[PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY] ?? null;
    const library = normalizeProfileLibrary(message.library ?? {}, { strict: true });
    const active = library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === library.activeProfileId);
    if (!active) throw new InputValidationError('Select an existing active profile.');
    const mappedOverrides = settingsOverridesFromLibrary(library);
    const nextSettings = normalizeSettings({ ...settings, profileId: active.baseProfileId, overrides: mappedOverrides }, { strict: true });
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: library });
    try {
      const saved = await persistAndApply(nextSettings, { forceProfileApply: true });
      const webRtcStatus = await applyWebRtcPrivacyPolicy(active);
      return { ok: true, library, settings: saved, stateRevision: await getStateRevision(), webRtcStatus, environment: currentCapabilityReport(), lastApply: lastApplyReport };
    } catch (error) {
      if (oldValue === null) await chrome.storage.local.remove(PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY);
      else await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: oldValue });
      throw error;
    }
  }
  if (message.type === 'EXPORT_PROFILE_LIBRARY') {
    const settings = await loadSettings();
    const library = await getProfileLibrary(settings);
    return { ok: true, export: createProfileExport(library) };
  }
  if (message.type === 'IMPORT_PROFILE_LIBRARY') {
    assertNotLauncherManagedProfiles();
    if (!message.value || !isJsonUnderByteLimit(message.value, MAX_PROFILE_LIBRARY_BYTES)) throw new InputValidationError('Profile definition import exceeds the 256 KiB limit.');
    const settings = await loadSettings();
    const imported = parseProfileImport(message.value);
    const oldValue = (await chrome.storage.local.get(PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY))[PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY] ?? null;
    const active = imported.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === imported.activeProfileId);
    const nextSettings = normalizeSettings({ ...settings, profileId: active.baseProfileId, overrides: settingsOverridesFromLibrary(imported) }, { strict: true });
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: imported });
    try {
      const saved = await persistAndApply(nextSettings, { forceProfileApply: true });
      const webRtcStatus = await applyWebRtcPrivacyPolicy(active);
      return { ok: true, library: imported, settings: saved, stateRevision: await getStateRevision(), webRtcStatus, environment: currentCapabilityReport(), lastApply: lastApplyReport };
    } catch (error) {
      if (oldValue === null) await chrome.storage.local.remove(PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY);
      else await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: oldValue });
      throw error;
    }
  }
  if (message.type === 'CLEAR_BROWSING_SESSION') {
    let cleared = false;
    let targetOrigin = typeof message.origin === 'string' && /^https?:/iu.test(message.origin) ? message.origin : null;
    if (!targetOrigin) {
      try {
        const [activeTab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, lastFocusedWindow: true });
        if (activeTab?.url && /^https?:/iu.test(activeTab.url)) {
          targetOrigin = new URL(activeTab.url).origin;
        }
      } catch (_) {}
    }
    try {
      if (chrome.browsingData?.remove) {
        if (targetOrigin) {
          await chrome.browsingData.remove(
            { origins: [targetOrigin] },
            { cache: true, cookies: true, localStorage: true }
          );
          cleared = true;
        } else {
          const sessionStart = await getOrInitSessionStartTime();
          const since = sessionStart > 0 ? sessionStart : (Date.now() - 3600000);
          await chrome.browsingData.remove(
            { since },
            { cache: true, cookies: true, localStorage: true }
          );
          cleared = true;
        }
      }
    } catch (clearErr) {
      // B18 FIX: Never silently expand scope to browser-wide when origin clear fails!
      recordDiagnosticIssue("ORIGIN_DATA_CLEAR_FAILED", clearErr?.message || "Failed to clear origin browsing data");
      cleared = false;
    }

    if (message.tabId && typeof chrome.scripting?.executeScript === 'function') {
      try {
        await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
          target: { tabId: message.tabId },
          func: () => {
            try { sessionStorage.clear(); } catch (_) {}
            try { localStorage.clear(); } catch (_) {}
          },
        });
      } catch (_) {}
    }

    return { ok: true, cleared, origin: targetOrigin || 'session' };
  }
  if (message.type === 'APPLY_DEVICE_PRESET') {
    const settings = await loadSettings();
    assertNotLauncherManagedProfiles();
    const preset = DEVICE_PRESETS.find((p) => p.id === message.presetId);
    if (!preset) throw new InputValidationError('Unknown device preset.');
    const library = await getProfileLibrary(settings);
    let targetRecord = library.profiles.find((p) => p.id === preset.id);
    if (!targetRecord) {
      targetRecord = createProfileRecord(preset.baseProfileId, {
        id: preset.id,
        name: preset.name,
        mode: preset.mode,
        locale: preset.locale,
        timezone: preset.timezone,
        geolocation: preset.geolocation,
        notes: preset.notes,
        screenWidth: preset.screenWidth,
        screenHeight: preset.screenHeight,
        devicePixelRatio: preset.devicePixelRatio,
        cpuCores: preset.cpuCores,
        memoryGb: preset.memoryGb,
        maxTouchPoints: preset.maxTouchPoints,
        gpuVendor: preset.gpuVendor,
        gpuRenderer: preset.gpuRenderer,
      });
      library.profiles.push(targetRecord);
    } else {
      targetRecord = normalizeProfileRecord({
        ...targetRecord,
        name: preset.name,
        mode: preset.mode,
        locale: preset.locale,
        timezone: preset.timezone,
        geolocation: preset.geolocation,
        notes: preset.notes,
        screenWidth: preset.screenWidth,
        screenHeight: preset.screenHeight,
        devicePixelRatio: preset.devicePixelRatio,
        cpuCores: preset.cpuCores,
        memoryGb: preset.memoryGb,
        maxTouchPoints: preset.maxTouchPoints,
        gpuVendor: preset.gpuVendor,
        gpuRenderer: preset.gpuRenderer,
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      }, { strict: true });
      const idx = library.profiles.findIndex((p) => p.id === preset.id);
      library.profiles[idx] = targetRecord;
    }
    library.activeProfileId = targetRecord.id;
    const nextSettings = normalizeSettings({ ...settings, profileId: targetRecord.baseProfileId, enabled: true }, { strict: true });
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: library });
    const saved = await persistAndApply(nextSettings, { forceProfileApply: true });
    const webRtcStatus = await applyWebRtcPrivacyPolicy(targetRecord);
    return { ok: true, library, settings: saved, stateRevision: await getStateRevision(), webRtcStatus, environment: currentCapabilityReport(), lastApply: lastApplyReport };
  }
  if (message.type === 'CLONE_PROFILE') {
    const settings = await loadSettings();
    assertNotLauncherManagedProfiles();
    const library = await getProfileLibrary(settings);
    const source = library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === message.profileId);
    if (!source) throw new InputValidationError('Unknown source profile.');
    if (library.profiles.length >= 100) throw new InputValidationError('A profile library may contain at most 100 profiles.');
    const baseSlug = `${source.id}-copy`;
    let id = baseSlug;
    let suffix = 2;
    while (library.profiles.some((entry) => entry.id === id)) { id = `${baseSlug}-${suffix}`; suffix += 1; }
    const clone = cloneProfileRecord(source, { id, name: typeof message.name === 'string' ? message.name : `${source.name} copy` });
    const next = normalizeProfileLibrary({ ...library, profiles: [...library.profiles, clone], activeProfileId: clone.id }, { strict: true });
    const nextSettings = normalizeSettings({ ...settings, profileId: clone.baseProfileId, overrides: settingsOverridesFromLibrary(next) }, { strict: true });
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: next });
    try {
      const saved = await persistAndApply(nextSettings, { forceProfileApply: true });
      const webRtcStatus = await applyWebRtcPrivacyPolicy(clone);
      return { ok: true, library: next, settings: saved, stateRevision: await getStateRevision(), webRtcStatus };
    } catch (error) {
      await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: library });
      throw error;
    }
  }
  if (message.type === 'DELETE_PROFILE') {
    const settings = await loadSettings();
    assertNotLauncherManagedProfiles();
    const library = await getProfileLibrary(settings);
    if (library.profiles.length <= 1) throw new InputValidationError('The last profile cannot be deleted.');
    if (!library.profiles.some((entry) => entry.id === message.profileId)) throw new InputValidationError('Unknown profile.');
    const profiles = library.profiles.filter((entry) => entry.id !== message.profileId);
    const assignments = library.assignments.filter((entry) => entry.profileId !== message.profileId);
    const activeProfileId = library.activeProfileId === message.profileId ? profiles[0].id : library.activeProfileId;
    const next = normalizeProfileLibrary({ ...library, profiles, assignments, activeProfileId }, { strict: true });
    const active = next.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === next.activeProfileId);
    const oldSettings = settings;
    const nextSettings = normalizeSettings({ ...settings, profileId: active.baseProfileId, overrides: settingsOverridesFromLibrary(next) }, { strict: true });
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: next });
    try {
      const saved = await persistAndApply(nextSettings, { forceProfileApply: true });
      return { ok: true, library: next, settings: saved, stateRevision: await getStateRevision() };
    } catch (error) {
      await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: library });
      await writeSettingsSnapshot(oldSettings);
      throw error;
    }
  }
  if (message.type === 'RUN_SELF_TEST' || message.type === 'RUN_PAGE_SELF_TEST') return await runCurrentPageSelfTest();
  if (message.type === 'GET_DIAGNOSTICS') return { ok: true, diagnostics: await diagnostics() };
  if (message.type === 'SAVE_SETTINGS') {
    let next = normalizeSettings(message.settings ?? {}, { strict: true });
    const launcherLibrary = readBundledLauncherLibrary();
    if (launcherLibrary) {
      const active = launcherLibrary.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === launcherLibrary.activeProfileId);
      const expectedOverrides = settingsOverridesFromLibrary(launcherLibrary);
      if (!active || next.profileId !== active.baseProfileId
          || stableJson(next.overrides) !== stableJson(expectedOverrides)
          || next.rotation.mode !== 'off') {
        throw new InputValidationError('This static launcher profile cannot be changed from extension settings. Change site assignments/profile/rotation with the desktop launcher and relaunch.');
      }
    }
    const storedBefore = await chrome.storage.local.get(PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY);
    const originalLibraryValue = storedBefore[PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY] ?? null;
    let library = await getProfileLibrary(next);
    try {
      library = await reconcileProfileLibraryWithSettings(next, library);
      const saved = await persistAndApply(next, { forceProfileApply: false });
      return { ok: true, settings: saved, stateRevision: await getStateRevision(), environment: currentCapabilityReport(), lastApply: lastApplyReport };
    } catch (error) {
      try {
        if (originalLibraryValue === null) await chrome.storage.local.remove(PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY);
        else await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: originalLibraryValue });
      } catch { /* keep a fixed diagnostic below; do not echo storage exceptions */ }
      lastApplyReport = {
        ...lastApplyReport,
        at: new Date().toISOString(),
        error: lastApplyReport.error ?? 'SETTINGS_PERSIST_FAILED',
        issues: [...new Set([...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? []), 'SETTINGS_PERSIST_FAILED', 'SETTINGS_PERSIST_FAILED_ROLLED_BACK'])],
      };
      throw error;
    }
  }
  if (message.type === 'RANDOMIZE_PROFILE') {
    assertNotLauncherManagedProfiles();
    const settings = await loadSettings();
    const category = message.category || 'all';
    let candidatePresets = [...DEVICE_PRESETS];
    if (category === 'pc' || category === 'desktop') {
      candidatePresets = candidatePresets.filter((p) => !p.baseProfileId.includes('android'));
    } else if (category === 'android' || category === 'mobile') {
      candidatePresets = candidatePresets.filter((p) => p.baseProfileId.includes('android'));
    }
    if (!candidatePresets.length) candidatePresets = [...DEVICE_PRESETS];
    const chosenPreset = candidatePresets[Math.floor(Math.random() * candidatePresets.length)];
    const library = await getProfileLibrary(settings);
    let targetRecord = library.profiles.find((p) => p.id === chosenPreset.id);
    if (!targetRecord) {
      targetRecord = createProfileRecord(chosenPreset.baseProfileId, {
        id: chosenPreset.id,
        name: chosenPreset.name,
        mode: chosenPreset.mode,
        locale: chosenPreset.locale,
        timezone: chosenPreset.timezone,
        geolocation: chosenPreset.geolocation,
        notes: chosenPreset.notes,
        screenWidth: chosenPreset.screenWidth,
        screenHeight: chosenPreset.screenHeight,
        devicePixelRatio: chosenPreset.devicePixelRatio,
        cpuCores: chosenPreset.cpuCores,
        memoryGb: chosenPreset.memoryGb,
        maxTouchPoints: chosenPreset.maxTouchPoints,
        gpuVendor: chosenPreset.gpuVendor,
        gpuRenderer: chosenPreset.gpuRenderer,
        seed: newSeed(),
      });
      library.profiles.push(targetRecord);
    } else {
      targetRecord = normalizeProfileRecord({
        ...targetRecord,
        seed: newSeed(),
        name: chosenPreset.name,
        mode: chosenPreset.mode,
        locale: chosenPreset.locale,
        timezone: chosenPreset.timezone,
        geolocation: chosenPreset.geolocation,
        notes: chosenPreset.notes,
        screenWidth: chosenPreset.screenWidth,
        screenHeight: chosenPreset.screenHeight,
        devicePixelRatio: chosenPreset.devicePixelRatio,
        cpuCores: chosenPreset.cpuCores,
        memoryGb: chosenPreset.memoryGb,
        maxTouchPoints: chosenPreset.maxTouchPoints,
        gpuVendor: chosenPreset.gpuVendor,
        gpuRenderer: chosenPreset.gpuRenderer,
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      }, { strict: true });
      const idx = library.profiles.findIndex((p) => p.id === chosenPreset.id);
      library.profiles[idx] = targetRecord;
    }
    library.activeProfileId = targetRecord.id;
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: library });
    const nextSettings = normalizeSettings({ ...settings, profileId: targetRecord.baseProfileId, enabled: true }, { strict: true });
    const saved = await persistAndApply(nextSettings, { forceProfileApply: true });
    return {
      ok: true,
      settings: saved,
      library,
      appliedPreset: chosenPreset.id,
      stateRevision: await getStateRevision(),
      environment: currentCapabilityReport(),
      lastApply: lastApplyReport,
      devicePresets: DEVICE_PRESETS,
      timezonePresets: TIMEZONE_PRESETS,
    };
  }
  if (message.type === 'SET_AUTO_ROTATION') {
    if (message.enabled === true) assertNotLauncherManagedProfiles();
    if (typeof message.enabled !== 'boolean') throw new InputValidationError('Automatic rotation must be enabled or disabled.');
    const settings = await loadSettings();
    const intervalMinutes = message.intervalMinutes ?? settings.rotation.intervalMinutes;
    if (!Number.isInteger(intervalMinutes) || intervalMinutes < 1 || intervalMinutes > 1440) {
      throw new InputValidationError('Rotation interval must be an integer from 1 to 1440 minutes.');
    }
    let rotation;
    if (message.enabled) {
      if (!settings.enabled) throw new InputValidationError('Enable profile controls before turning on automatic rotation.');
      if (!DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities.alarms) {
        throw new InputValidationError('This browser cannot schedule background alarms, so timed rotation is unavailable.');
      }
      const profileIds = getRotationCandidateIds(settings);
      if (profileIds.length < 2) {
        throw new InputValidationError('At least two profiles are required for automatic rotation. Open the workspace to select profiles.');
      }
      const preferredMode = settings.rotation.mode === 'sequential'
        ? 'sequential'
        : settings.rotation.preferredMode === 'sequential'
          ? 'sequential'
          : 'random';
      rotation = { ...settings.rotation, mode: preferredMode, preferredMode, intervalMinutes, profileIds };
    } else {
      const preferredMode = ['random', 'sequential'].includes(settings.rotation.mode)
        ? settings.rotation.mode
        : settings.rotation.preferredMode ?? 'random';
      rotation = { ...settings.rotation, mode: 'off', preferredMode, intervalMinutes };
    }
    const next = normalizeSettings({ ...settings, rotation }, { strict: true });
    const saved = await persistAndApply(next);
    return { ok: true, settings: saved, stateRevision: await getStateRevision(), environment: currentCapabilityReport(), lastApply: lastApplyReport };
  }
  if (message.type === 'RESET_SETTINGS') {
    assertNotLauncherManagedProfiles();
    const next = createFirstRunSettings(DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.identity);
    const library = await getProfileLibrary(next);
    const defaultRecord = library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.baseProfileId === next.profileId) ?? library.profiles[0];
    const resetLibrary = normalizeProfileLibrary({
      ...library,
      activeProfileId: defaultRecord.id,
      assignments: [],
      profiles: library.profiles.map((entry) => entry.id === defaultRecord.id
        ? { ...entry, mode: 'identity', surfaces: Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(entry.surfaces).map((key) => [key, false])), updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() }
        : entry),
    }, { strict: true });
    await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: resetLibrary });
    try {
      const saved = await persistAndApply(next, { forceProfileApply: true });
      await applyWebRtcPrivacyPolicy(null);
      return { ok: true, settings: saved, stateRevision: await getStateRevision(), recommendedProfileId: recommendProfileId(DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.identity), environment: currentCapabilityReport(), lastApply: lastApplyReport };
    } catch (error) {
      await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: library });
      throw error;
    }
  }
  throw new InputValidationError('Unsupported extension message.');
}

if (DETECTED_CAPABILITIES.capabilities.webNavigationCommitted) {
  chrome.webNavigation.onCommitted.addListener((details) => {
    if (!Number.isInteger(details?.tabId)
        || !Number.isInteger(details?.frameId)
        || typeof details.url !== 'string'
        || !/^https?:/iu.test(details.url)
        || isUrlReserved(details.url)) return;
    void runExclusive(async () => {
      const settings = await loadSettings();
      if (!settings.enabled) return;
      const baseProfileId = getProfileIdForUrl(details.url, settings);
      if (!baseProfileId || !PROFILES[baseProfileId]) return;
      if (typeof chrome.scripting?.executeScript !== 'function') return;
      const library = await getProfileLibrary(settings);
      if (readBundledLauncherLibrary() && pageInjectionStrategy === 'document-start') return;
      const layer = profileForUrl(library, details.url, baseProfileId);
      const needsBaseFallback = pageInjectionStrategy === 'navigation-fallback';
      const needsHardening = Boolean(layer && layer.baseProfileId === baseProfileId && layer.mode !== 'identity'
        && Object.values(layer.surfaces).some(Boolean));
      if (!needsBaseFallback && !needsHardening) return;
      const config = needsBaseFallback
        ? { ...getProfile(baseProfileId), mode: layer?.mode ?? 'identity', seed: layer?.seed, surfaces: layer?.surfaces, locale: layer?.locale, timezone: layer?.timezone, geolocation: layer?.geolocation, screenWidth: layer?.screenWidth, screenHeight: layer?.screenHeight, devicePixelRatio: layer?.devicePixelRatio, cpuCores: layer?.cpuCores, memoryGb: layer?.memoryGb, maxTouchPoints: layer?.maxTouchPoints, gpuVendor: layer?.gpuVendor, gpuRenderer: layer?.gpuRenderer }
        : { os: getProfile(baseProfileId).os, mobile: getProfile(baseProfileId).mobile, architecture: getProfile(baseProfileId).architecture, hardeningOnly: true, mode: layer.mode, seed: layer.seed, surfaces: layer.surfaces, locale: layer.locale, timezone: layer.timezone, geolocation: layer.geolocation, screenWidth: layer?.screenWidth, screenHeight: layer?.screenHeight, devicePixelRatio: layer?.devicePixelRatio, cpuCores: layer?.cpuCores, memoryGb: layer?.memoryGb, maxTouchPoints: layer?.maxTouchPoints, gpuVendor: layer?.gpuVendor, gpuRenderer: layer?.gpuRenderer };
      await chrome.scripting.executeScript({
        target: { tabId: details.tabId, frameIds: [details.frameId] },
        world: 'MAIN',
        func: applyProfileToPage,
        args: [config],
        injectImmediately: true,
      });
    }).catch((error) => {
      const message = typeof error?.message === 'string' ? error.message : '';
      const mainWorldUnsupported = /(?:world.{0,80}(?:unsupported|not supported|invalid)|(?:unsupported|not supported).{0,80}world|world.{0,80}MAIN)/iu.test(message);
      const code = mainWorldUnsupported ? 'MAIN_WORLD_UNAVAILABLE' : 'NAVIGATION_INJECTION_FAILED';
      if (mainWorldUnsupported) pageInjectionStrategy = 'unavailable';
      lastApplyReport = {
        ...lastApplyReport,
        at: new Date().toISOString(),
        error: code,
        strategies: { header: effectiveHeaderStrategy, page: pageInjectionStrategy },
        issues: [...new Set([...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? []), code])],
      };
    });
  });
}


chrome.runtime.onInstalled.addListener((details) => {
  void initialize({ startNewSession: details.reason === 'install' }).catch((error) => {
    lastApplyReport = { ...lastApplyReport, at: new Date().toISOString(), error: getSafeFailureCode(error) };
  });
});
chrome.runtime.onStartup.addListener(() => {
  void initialize({ startNewSession: true }).catch((error) => {
    lastApplyReport = { ...lastApplyReport, at: new Date().toISOString(), error: getSafeFailureCode(error) };
  });
});
if (typeof chrome.alarms?.onAlarm?.addListener === 'function') chrome.alarms.onAlarm.addListener((alarm) => {
  if (alarm.name !== ROTATION_ALARM) return;
  void runExclusive(async () => {
    const settings = await loadSettings();
    if (!settings.enabled || !TIMED_ROTATION_MODES.includes(settings.rotation.mode)) {
      await syncRotationAlarm(settings);
      return;
    }
    const profileId = rotateProfile(settings);
    const next = normalizeSettings({ ...settings, profileId }, { strict: true });
    await persistAndApply(next);
  }).catch((error) => {
    lastApplyReport = { ...lastApplyReport, at: new Date().toISOString(), error: getSafeFailureCode(error) };
  });
});

// Alarm persistence differs across browser forks and engine versions. Recheck
// on every service-worker cold start; this does not touch browsing history or
// reset the timer if the named alarm is already present.
void ensureRotationAlarmOnWorkerStart().catch((error) => {
  lastApplyReport = {
    ...lastApplyReport,
    at: new Date().toISOString(),
    error: getSafeFailureCode(error),
    issues: [...new Set([...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? []), 'ROTATION_ALARM_RECOVERY_FAILED'])],
  };
});

// Reconcile direct settings writes made outside the normal message API.
// Coalesce bursts by reading the latest value inside the serial queue instead of
// trusting a potentially stale value from the first onChanged event. A generation
// counter ensures a second write arriving during reconciliation is not dropped.
async function reconcileExternalSettingsSnapshot() {
  if (!lastAppliedSettings) return;
  const stored = await chrome.storage.local.get(SETTINGS_KEY);
  const rawNext = stored?.[SETTINGS_KEY];
  let next;
  try {
    next = normalizeSettings(rawNext, { strict: true });
  } catch {
    await writeSettingsSnapshot(lastAppliedSettings);
    lastApplyReport = {
      ...lastApplyReport,
      at: new Date().toISOString(),
      error: 'EXTERNAL_SETTINGS_REJECTED',
      issues: [...new Set([...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? []), 'EXTERNAL_SETTINGS_REJECTED'])],
    };
    await publishStateChanged('external-settings-rejected');
    return;
  }
  if (stableJson(next) === stableJson(lastAppliedSettings)) return;

  // Keep the last-known-good settings and profile library until browser-side
  // reconciliation succeeds, so direct storage edits cannot split these snapshots.
  let libraryBefore;
  try {
    libraryBefore = await getProfileLibrary(lastAppliedSettings);
    await writeSettingsSnapshot(lastAppliedSettings);
    await reconcileProfileLibraryWithSettings(next, libraryBefore);
    await persistAndApply(next, { forceProfileApply: true });
  } catch {
    try {
      if (libraryBefore) await chrome.storage.local.set({ [PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY]: libraryBefore });
      await writeSettingsSnapshot(lastAppliedSettings);
    } catch { /* Keep the public diagnostic fixed even if recovery storage fails. */ }
    // persistAndApply already attempts runtime rollback; expose only a fixed code.
    lastApplyReport = {
      ...lastApplyReport,
      at: new Date().toISOString(),
      error: 'EXTERNAL_SETTINGS_APPLY_FAILED',
      issues: [...new Set([...(lastApplyReport.issues ?? []), 'EXTERNAL_SETTINGS_APPLY_FAILED'])],
    };
    await publishStateChanged('external-settings-apply-failed');
  }
}

function scheduleExternalSettingsReconciliation() {
  if (externalSettingsReconcileQueued) return;
  externalSettingsReconcileQueued = true;
  let processedVersion = -1;
  void runExclusive(async () => {
    while (processedVersion !== externalSettingsChangeVersion) {
      processedVersion = externalSettingsChangeVersion;
      await reconcileExternalSettingsSnapshot();
    }
  }).catch(() => {
    // Do not leak storage/API exceptions; a later change or workspace open can retry.
  }).finally(() => {
    externalSettingsReconcileQueued = false;
    if (processedVersion !== externalSettingsChangeVersion) scheduleExternalSettingsReconciliation();
  });
}

if (typeof chrome.storage?.onChanged?.addListener === 'function') {
  chrome.storage.onChanged.addListener((changes, areaName) => {
    if (areaName !== 'local' || !changes?.[SETTINGS_KEY]) return;
    externalSettingsChangeVersion += 1;
    scheduleExternalSettingsReconciliation();
  });
}

chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((message, sender, sendResponse) => {
  if (message?.type === 'VEIL_STATE_CHANGED') return false;
  if (sender.id !== chrome.runtime.id) return false;
  void runExclusive(() => handleMessage(message)).then(sendResponse, (error) => {
    const operation = message?.type === 'GET_SETTINGS' ? 'load'
      : message?.type === 'GET_DIAGNOSTICS' ? 'diagnostics'
        : message?.type === 'RESET_SETTINGS' ? 'reset' : 'save';
    sendResponse({ ok: false, errorCode: getSafeFailureCode(error), error: getSafePublicError(error, operation) });
  });
  return true;
});
