/**
 * Veil Profile Studio Universal v3.1.0 - Main World Profile Engine
 *
 * Implements:
 * - Clean separation between Identity and Hardening (Fixes B1, B2).
 * - Per-site deterministic 128-bit seed (cyrb128 + sfc32) (Fixes B2, Section 5.2).
 * - Removed all navigator.webdriver overrides (Section 2 boundary compliance).
 * - Removed Battery API spoofing (Fixes B13).
 * - Idempotent AudioBuffer jitter using WeakMap cache (Fixes B6).
 * - Safe Screen properties without layout breakage (Fixes B8).
 * - Robust Timezone & Intl synchronization with Date.UTC (Fixes B7).
 * - Geolocation prototype fidelity without native prompts (Fixes B10).
 * - Modern WebGL masking with WEBGL_debug_renderer_info check (Fixes B11).
 * - Clamped deviceMemory <= 8 (Fixes B12).
 * - WebRTC safe callbacks and removeEventListener listener map (Fixes B9).
 * - Dedicated & SharedWorker identity prelude injection with safe fallback (Fixes B4, Section 5.5).
 */

export function applyProfileToPage(profile) {
  const pageGlobal = (typeof this !== 'undefined' && this?.navigator) ? this : globalThis;
  const nav = pageGlobal.navigator;
  if (!nav) return { applied: false, reason: 'navigator-unavailable' };

  // --- 1. STEALTH PROTOTYPE & TOSTRING ENGINE ---
  const TO_STRING_TAG = Symbol.for('__veil_native_tostring__');
  const customToStringMap = new WeakMap();
  const patchedFunctions = new WeakSet();

  const nativeToString = Function.prototype.toString;
  if (!nativeToString[TO_STRING_TAG]) {
    try {
      const wrappedToString = new Proxy(nativeToString, {
        apply(target, thisArg, args) {
          if (typeof thisArg !== 'function') {
            return Reflect.apply(target, thisArg, args);
          }
          const custom = customToStringMap.get(thisArg);
          if (custom !== undefined) return custom;
          return Reflect.apply(target, thisArg, args);
        },
      });
      Object.defineProperty(wrappedToString, TO_STRING_TAG, { value: true });
      customToStringMap.set(wrappedToString, 'function toString() { [native code] }');
      customToStringMap.set(nativeToString, 'function toString() { [native code] }');
      patchedFunctions.add(wrappedToString);
      patchedFunctions.add(nativeToString);
      Object.defineProperty(Function.prototype, 'toString', {
        value: wrappedToString,
        writable: true,
        configurable: true,
        enumerable: false,
      });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  const wrapNative = (original, handler, customSource = null) => {
    if (typeof original !== 'function') return original;
    if (patchedFunctions.has(original)) return original;
    const fnName = customSource || original.name || '';
    const wrapped = new Proxy(original, handler);
    customToStringMap.set(wrapped, `function ${fnName}() { [native code] }`);
    customToStringMap.set(original, `function ${fnName}() { [native code] }`);
    patchedFunctions.add(wrapped);
    return wrapped;
  };

  const makeNativeGetter = (name, getterFn, TargetClass = null) => {
    const holder = {
      [name]() {
        if (TargetClass && !(this instanceof TargetClass) && this !== TargetClass.prototype) {
          throw new TypeError(`'get ${name}' called on an object that does not implement interface ${TargetClass.name || 'Target'}.`);
        }
        return getterFn.call(this);
      }
    };
    const method = holder[name];
    customToStringMap.set(method, `function get ${name}() { [native code] }`);
    patchedFunctions.add(method);
    return method;
  };

  const makeNativeMethod = (name, length, fn, TargetClass = null) => {
    const holder = {
      [name](...args) {
        if (TargetClass && !(this instanceof TargetClass) && this !== TargetClass.prototype) {
          throw new TypeError(`'${name}' called on an object that does not implement interface ${TargetClass.name || 'Target'}.`);
        }
        return fn.apply(this, args);
      }
    };
    const method = holder[name];
    try { Object.defineProperty(method, 'length', { value: length, configurable: true }); } catch (_) {}
    customToStringMap.set(method, `function ${name}() { [native code] }`);
    patchedFunctions.add(method);
    return method;
  };

  // --- 2. SEED ENGINE (128-bit cyrb128 + sfc32 with Per-site Isolation) ---
  const cyrb128 = (str) => {
    let h1 = 1779033703, h2 = 3144134277, h3 = 1013904242, h4 = 2773480762;
    for (let i = 0, k; i < str.length; i++) {
      k = str.charCodeAt(i);
      h1 = h2 ^ Math.imul(h1 ^ k, 597399067);
      h2 = h3 ^ Math.imul(h2 ^ k, 2869860233);
      h3 = h4 ^ Math.imul(h3 ^ k, 951274213);
      h4 = h1 ^ Math.imul(h4 ^ k, 2716044179);
    }
    h1 = Math.imul(h3 ^ (h1 >>> 18), 597399067);
    h2 = Math.imul(h4 ^ (h2 >>> 22), 2869860233);
    h3 = Math.imul(h1 ^ (h3 >>> 17), 951274213);
    h4 = Math.imul(h2 ^ (h4 >>> 19), 2716044179);
    return [(h1 ^ h2 ^ h3 ^ h4) >>> 0, (h2 ^ h1) >>> 0, (h3 ^ h1) >>> 0, (h4 ^ h1) >>> 0];
  };

  const sfc32 = (a, b, c, d) => {
    return function() {
      a >>>= 0; b >>>= 0; c >>>= 0; d >>>= 0;
      let t = (a + b | 0) + d | 0;
      d = d + 1 | 0;
      a = b ^ b >>> 9;
      b = c + (c << 3) | 0;
      c = (c << 21 | c >>> 11);
      c = c + t | 0;
      return (t >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    };
  };

  const siteOrigin = pageGlobal.location?.hostname || 'local';
  const rawSeed = typeof profile.seed === 'string' && profile.seed.length >= 8
    ? profile.seed : 'veil-stealth-seed-default-v3.1.0';
  const effectiveSeedStr = `${rawSeed}::site=${siteOrigin}`;
  const [s1, s2, s3, s4] = cyrb128(effectiveSeedStr);

  const createRng = (label) => {
    const [a, b, c, d] = cyrb128(`${effectiveSeedStr}::${label}`);
    return sfc32(a, b, c, d);
  };

  const isMobile = Boolean(profile.mobile || profile.os === 'android');
  const targetOs = profile.os || (isMobile ? 'android' : 'windows');
  const navPrototype = pageGlobal.Navigator?.prototype || Object.getPrototypeOf(nav);
  const NavClass = pageGlobal.Navigator || Object;

  const defineNavGetter = (prop, getter) => {
    try { delete nav[prop]; } catch (_) {}
    try {
      Object.defineProperty(navPrototype, prop, {
        get: getter,
        configurable: true,
        enumerable: true
      });
    } catch (_) {}
  };

  // --- 3. IDENTITY LAYER (Skipped if hardeningOnly is set - Fixes B1) ---
  if (!profile.hardeningOnly) {
    if (typeof profile.userAgent === 'string' && profile.userAgent) {
      defineNavGetter('userAgent', makeNativeGetter('userAgent', () => profile.userAgent, NavClass));
      defineNavGetter('appVersion', makeNativeGetter('appVersion', () => profile.userAgent.replace(/^Mozilla\//u, ''), NavClass));
    }
    const targetPlatform = profile.platform || (
      profile.os === 'windows' ? 'Win32'
      : profile.os === 'macos' ? 'MacIntel'
      : profile.os === 'linux' ? 'Linux x86_64'
      : 'Linux armv8l'
    );
    defineNavGetter('platform', makeNativeGetter('platform', () => targetPlatform, NavClass));
    defineNavGetter('vendor', makeNativeGetter('vendor', () => profile.vendor || 'Google Inc.', NavClass));

    // User-Agent Client Hints
    const uaData = nav.userAgentData;
    if (uaData && typeof uaData === 'object') {
      const uaDataProto = Object.getPrototypeOf(uaData) || uaData;
      const UaClass = pageGlobal.NavigatorUAData || Object;
      const brands = Array.isArray(profile.brands) ? profile.brands.map(b => ({ brand: b.brand, version: b.version })) : [];
      const chPlatform = profile.os === 'windows' ? 'Windows' : profile.os === 'macos' ? 'macOS' : profile.os === 'linux' ? 'Linux' : profile.os === 'android' ? 'Android' : 'Unknown';

      try {
        Object.defineProperty(uaDataProto, 'brands', { get: makeNativeGetter('brands', () => Object.freeze(brands.map(b => ({ ...b }))), UaClass), configurable: true, enumerable: true });
        Object.defineProperty(uaDataProto, 'mobile', { get: makeNativeGetter('mobile', () => isMobile, UaClass), configurable: true, enumerable: true });
        Object.defineProperty(uaDataProto, 'platform', { get: makeNativeGetter('platform', () => chPlatform, UaClass), configurable: true, enumerable: true });

        const he = profile.highEntropy || {};
        const fullList = Array.isArray(he.fullVersionList) ? he.fullVersionList.map(b => ({ brand: b.brand, version: b.version })) : brands;
        const getHeValues = function(hints) {
          if (!Array.isArray(hints)) return Promise.reject(new TypeError("parameter 1 is not iterable."));
          const res = { brands: brands.map(b => ({ ...b })), mobile: isMobile, platform: chPlatform };
          for (const h of hints) {
            if (h === 'architecture') res.architecture = he.architecture || 'x86';
            else if (h === 'bitness') res.bitness = he.bitness || '64';
            else if (h === 'model') res.model = he.model || '';
            else if (h === 'platformVersion') res.platformVersion = he.platformVersion || '15.0.0';
            else if (h === 'fullVersionList') res.fullVersionList = fullList.map(b => ({ ...b }));
            else if (h === 'wow64') res.wow64 = Boolean(he.wow64);
            else if (h === 'formFactors') res.formFactors = Array.isArray(he.formFactors) ? [...he.formFactors] : (isMobile ? ['Mobile'] : ['Desktop']);
          }
          return Promise.resolve(res);
        };
        Object.defineProperty(uaDataProto, 'getHighEntropyValues', { value: makeNativeMethod('getHighEntropyValues', 1, getHeValues, UaClass), writable: true, configurable: true, enumerable: true });
        Object.defineProperty(uaDataProto, 'toJSON', { value: makeNativeMethod('toJSON', 0, () => ({ brands: brands.map(b => ({ ...b })), mobile: isMobile, platform: chPlatform }), UaClass), writable: true, configurable: true, enumerable: true });
      } catch (_) {}
    }
  }

  // If in pure identity mode, stop here!
  if (profile.mode === 'identity') {
    return { applied: true, mode: 'identity', profileId: profile.id };
  }

  const surfaces = profile.surfaces || {};

  // --- 4. HARDWARE CONCURRENCY & CLAMPED MEMORY (Fixes B12) ---
  if (surfaces.hardware) {
    const cores = Number.isInteger(profile.cpuCores) && profile.cpuCores > 0 ? profile.cpuCores : (isMobile ? 8 : 12);
    let ram = Number(profile.memoryGb) || 8;
    // Chromium clamps deviceMemory to [0.25, 0.5, 1, 2, 4, 8]
    if (ram > 8) ram = 8;
    defineNavGetter('hardwareConcurrency', makeNativeGetter('hardwareConcurrency', () => cores, NavClass));
    defineNavGetter('deviceMemory', makeNativeGetter('deviceMemory', () => ram, NavClass));
  }

  // --- 5. SCREEN STEALTH (Fixes B8: No forcing innerWidth/innerHeight/clientWidth) ---
  if (surfaces.screen) {
    const targetWidth = profile.screenWidth || (isMobile ? 412 : 1920);
    const targetHeight = profile.screenHeight || (isMobile ? 915 : 1080);
    const targetAvailWidth = profile.screenAvailWidth || targetWidth;
    const targetAvailHeight = profile.screenAvailHeight || (isMobile ? targetHeight : Math.max(720, targetHeight - 40));
    const targetDpr = profile.devicePixelRatio || (isMobile ? 2.625 : 1.0);

    const screenProto = pageGlobal.Screen?.prototype || Object.getPrototypeOf(pageGlobal.screen);
    const ScreenClass = pageGlobal.Screen || Object;

    const defineScreenGetter = (prop, getter) => {
      try { delete pageGlobal.screen[prop]; } catch (_) {}
      if (screenProto) {
        try { Object.defineProperty(screenProto, prop, { get: getter, configurable: true, enumerable: true }); } catch (_) {}
      }
    };

    defineScreenGetter('width', makeNativeGetter('width', () => targetWidth, ScreenClass));
    defineScreenGetter('height', makeNativeGetter('height', () => targetHeight, ScreenClass));
    defineScreenGetter('availWidth', makeNativeGetter('availWidth', () => targetAvailWidth, ScreenClass));
    defineScreenGetter('availHeight', makeNativeGetter('availHeight', () => targetAvailHeight, ScreenClass));
    defineScreenGetter('colorDepth', makeNativeGetter('colorDepth', () => 24, ScreenClass));
    defineScreenGetter('pixelDepth', makeNativeGetter('pixelDepth', () => 24, ScreenClass));

    try {
      delete pageGlobal.devicePixelRatio;
      Object.defineProperty(pageGlobal, 'devicePixelRatio', { get: makeNativeGetter('devicePixelRatio', () => targetDpr), configurable: true, enumerable: true });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // --- 6. POINTER & MEDIA QUERY ALIGNMENT (Fixes B5) ---
  if (surfaces.touch || isMobile) {
    if (isMobile) {
      defineNavGetter('maxTouchPoints', makeNativeGetter('maxTouchPoints', () => 5, NavClass));
    }
    if (pageGlobal.MediaQueryList?.prototype) {
      const mqlProto = pageGlobal.MediaQueryList.prototype;
      const origMatches = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(mqlProto, 'matches')?.get;
      if (origMatches) {
        Object.defineProperty(mqlProto, 'matches', {
          get: wrapNative(origMatches, {
            apply(target, thisArg, args) {
              const query = String(thisArg?.media || '').toLowerCase();
              if (query === '(pointer: coarse)') return isMobile;
              if (query === '(pointer: fine)') return !isMobile;
              if (query === '(hover: none)') return isMobile;
              if (query === '(hover: hover)') return !isMobile;
              return Reflect.apply(target, thisArg, args);
            }
          }, 'get matches'),
          configurable: true,
          enumerable: true
        });
      }
    }
  }

  // --- 7. IDEMPOTENT AUDIO PROTECTION (Fixes B6) ---
  if (surfaces.audio) {
    const audioBufferProto = pageGlobal.AudioBuffer?.prototype;
    if (audioBufferProto) {
      const channelCache = new WeakMap();
      const origGetChannelData = audioBufferProto.getChannelData;
      if (origGetChannelData) {
        audioBufferProto.getChannelData = wrapNative(origGetChannelData, {
          apply(target, receiver, args) {
            const nativeData = Reflect.apply(target, receiver, args);
            if (!nativeData || nativeData.length === 0) return nativeData;
            const ch = Number(args[0]) || 0;

            let bufferMap = channelCache.get(receiver);
            if (!bufferMap) {
              bufferMap = new Map();
              channelCache.set(receiver, bufferMap);
            }
            if (bufferMap.has(ch)) return bufferMap.get(ch);

            // Copy to avoid mutating native buffer repeatedly
            const copy = new Float32Array(nativeData);
            const rng = createRng(`audio-${ch}`);
            const step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(copy.length / 32));
            const offset = Math.floor(rng() * step);
            for (let i = offset; i < copy.length; i += step) {
              copy[i] += (rng() * 2 - 1) * 0.00005;
            }
            bufferMap.set(ch, copy);
            return copy;
          }
        }, 'getChannelData');
      }
    }
  }

  // --- 8. CANVAS PROTECTION (Fixes B15) ---
  if (surfaces.canvas) {
    const perturbImageData = (imgData, width, height, label) => {
      const data = imgData?.data;
      if (!data || typeof data.length !== 'number' || data.length < 4 || !width || !height) return imgData;
      const pixels = Math.floor(data.length / 4);
      const rng = createRng(`canvas-${label}-${width}x${height}`);
      const count = Math.min(3, pixels);
      for (let s = 0; s < count; s++) {
        const pIdx = Math.floor(rng() * pixels);
        const bIdx = pIdx * 4;
        if (data[bIdx + 3] > 10) {
          const ch = Math.floor(rng() * 3);
          const delta = rng() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
          data[bIdx + ch] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, data[bIdx + ch] + delta));
        }
      }
      return imgData;
    };

    const ctxProto = pageGlobal.CanvasRenderingContext2D?.prototype;
    if (ctxProto?.getImageData) {
      ctxProto.getImageData = wrapNative(ctxProto.getImageData, {
        apply(target, receiver, args) {
          const res = Reflect.apply(target, receiver, args);
          return perturbImageData(res, Number(args[2]), Number(args[3]), '2d-getImageData');
        }
      }, 'getImageData');
    }

    const offProto = pageGlobal.OffscreenCanvasRenderingContext2D?.prototype;
    if (offProto?.getImageData) {
      offProto.getImageData = wrapNative(offProto.getImageData, {
        apply(target, receiver, args) {
          const res = Reflect.apply(target, receiver, args);
          return perturbImageData(res, Number(args[2]), Number(args[3]), 'offscreen-getImageData');
        }
      }, 'getImageData');
    }
  }

  // --- 9. WEBGL PROTECTION (Fixes B11) ---
  if (surfaces.webgl) {
    const UNMASKED_VENDOR_WEBGL = 0x9245;
    const UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL = 0x9246;
    const targetGpu = (() => {
      if (profile.gpuVendor && profile.gpuRenderer) return { vendor: profile.gpuVendor, renderer: profile.gpuRenderer };
      if (targetOs === 'android') return { vendor: 'Google Inc. (ARM)', renderer: 'ANGLE (ARM, Mali-G715-Immortalis MC11, OpenGL ES 3.2)' };
      if (targetOs === 'macos') return { vendor: 'Google Inc. (Apple)', renderer: 'ANGLE (Apple, ANGLE Metal Renderer: Apple M3, Version 14.0)' };
      if (targetOs === 'linux') return { vendor: 'Google Inc. (Mesa)', renderer: 'ANGLE (Mesa, Mesa Intel(R) UHD Graphics 630 (CFL GT2), OpenGL 4.6)' };
      return { vendor: 'Google Inc. (NVIDIA)', renderer: 'ANGLE (NVIDIA, NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4060 Direct3D11 vs_5_0 ps_5_0, D3D11)' };
    })();

    const requestedExts = new WeakMap();
    const glProtos = [pageGlobal.WebGLRenderingContext?.prototype, pageGlobal.WebGL2RenderingContext?.prototype].filter(Boolean);

    for (const proto of glProtos) {
      const origGetExt = proto.getExtension;
      if (origGetExt) {
        proto.getExtension = wrapNative(origGetExt, {
          apply(target, receiver, args) {
            const extName = String(args[0] || '').toLowerCase();
            const ext = Reflect.apply(target, receiver, args);
            if (ext && extName.includes('debug_renderer_info')) {
              let set = requestedExts.get(receiver);
              if (!set) { set = new Set(); requestedExts.set(receiver, set); }
              set.add('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');
            }
            return ext;
          }
        }, 'getExtension');
      }

      const origGetParam = proto.getParameter;
      if (origGetParam) {
        proto.getParameter = wrapNative(origGetParam, {
          apply(target, receiver, args) {
            const p = args[0];
            if (p === UNMASKED_VENDOR_WEBGL) {
              const hasExt = requestedExts.get(receiver)?.has('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');
              return hasExt ? targetGpu.vendor : Reflect.apply(target, receiver, args);
            }
            if (p === UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL) {
              const hasExt = requestedExts.get(receiver)?.has('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');
              return hasExt ? targetGpu.renderer : Reflect.apply(target, receiver, args);
            }
            return Reflect.apply(target, receiver, args);
          }
        }, 'getParameter');
      }
    }
  }

  // --- 10. WEBRTC PROTECTION (Fixes B9) ---
  if (surfaces.webrtc) {
    const rtcProto = pageGlobal.RTCPeerConnection?.prototype;
    if (rtcProto) {
      const listenerMap = new WeakMap();
      const onIceHandlerMap = new WeakMap();

      const filterSdp = (sdp) => {
        if (typeof sdp !== 'string') return sdp;
        return sdp.replace(/a=candidate:[^\r\n]*typ host[^\r\n]*\r?\n/gu, '').replace(/candidate:[^\r\n]*typ host[^\r\n]*/gu, '');
      };

      const origCreateOffer = rtcProto.createOffer;
      if (origCreateOffer) {
        rtcProto.createOffer = wrapNative(origCreateOffer, {
          apply(target, receiver, args) {
            const ret = Reflect.apply(target, receiver, args);
            if (ret && typeof ret.then === 'function') {
              return ret.then(desc => { if (desc?.sdp) desc.sdp = filterSdp(desc.sdp); return desc; });
            }
            return ret;
          }
        }, 'createOffer');
      }

      const origOnIce = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(rtcProto, 'onicecandidate');
      if (origOnIce?.set) {
        Object.defineProperty(rtcProto, 'onicecandidate', {
          get() { return onIceHandlerMap.get(this) || null; },
          set(handler) {
            onIceHandlerMap.set(this, handler);
            if (typeof handler !== 'function') return origOnIce.set.call(this, handler);
            const wrapped = function(event) {
              if (event?.candidate?.candidate?.includes('typ host')) return;
              return handler.apply(this, arguments);
            };
            return origOnIce.set.call(this, wrapped);
          },
          configurable: true,
          enumerable: true
        });
      }

      const origAddEv = rtcProto.addEventListener;
      const origRemEv = rtcProto.removeEventListener;
      if (origAddEv && origRemEv) {
        rtcProto.addEventListener = wrapNative(origAddEv, {
          apply(target, receiver, args) {
            if (args[0] === 'icecandidate' && typeof args[1] === 'function') {
              const uHandler = args[1];
              const wrapped = function(e) {
                if (e?.candidate?.candidate?.includes('typ host')) return;
                return uHandler.apply(this, arguments);
              };
              listenerMap.set(uHandler, wrapped);
              return Reflect.apply(target, receiver, [args[0], wrapped, args[2]]);
            }
            return Reflect.apply(target, receiver, args);
          }
        }, 'addEventListener');

        rtcProto.removeEventListener = wrapNative(origRemEv, {
          apply(target, receiver, args) {
            if (args[0] === 'icecandidate' && typeof args[1] === 'function') {
              const wrapped = listenerMap.get(args[1]) || args[1];
              return Reflect.apply(target, receiver, [args[0], wrapped, args[2]]);
            }
            return Reflect.apply(target, receiver, args);
          }
        }, 'removeEventListener');
      }
    }
  }

  // --- 11. TIMEZONE & INTL SYNCHRONIZATION (Fixes B7) ---
  if (surfaces.intl && (profile.timezone || profile.locale)) {
    const tz = profile.timezone || 'UTC';
    const loc = profile.locale || 'en-US';

    const calcOffset = (d) => {
      try {
        const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: tz, year: 'numeric', month: 'numeric', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: 'numeric', second: 'numeric', hourCycle: 'h23' });
        const parts = dtf.formatToParts(d);
        const getV = (t) => parseInt(parts.find(p => p.type === t)?.value || '0', 10);
        const targetUtc = Date.UTC(getV('year'), getV('month') - 1, getV('day'), getV('hour'), getV('minute'), getV('second'));
        return Math.round((d.getTime() - targetUtc) / 60000);
      } catch (_) {
        return d.getTimezoneOffset();
      }
    };

    const dateProto = pageGlobal.Date?.prototype;
    if (dateProto?.getTimezoneOffset) {
      dateProto.getTimezoneOffset = wrapNative(dateProto.getTimezoneOffset, {
        apply(target, receiver, args) {
          if (!(receiver instanceof Date)) return Reflect.apply(target, receiver, args);
          return calcOffset(receiver);
        }
      }, 'getTimezoneOffset');
    }

    if (pageGlobal.Intl?.DateTimeFormat) {
      const NativeDTF = pageGlobal.Intl.DateTimeFormat;
      const normalizeArgs = (args) => {
        const next = [...args];
        if (next[0] === undefined && loc) next[0] = loc;
        const opts = next[1] && typeof next[1] === 'object' ? { ...next[1] } : {};
        if (opts.timeZone === undefined && tz) opts.timeZone = tz;
        next[1] = opts;
        return next;
      };
      const WrappedDTF = wrapNative(NativeDTF, {
        apply(target, thisArg, args) { return Reflect.apply(target, thisArg, normalizeArgs(args)); },
        construct(target, args, newTarget) { return Reflect.construct(target, normalizeArgs(args), newTarget === WrappedDTF ? target : newTarget); }
      }, 'DateTimeFormat');
      WrappedDTF.prototype = NativeDTF.prototype;
      WrappedDTF.supportedLocalesOf = NativeDTF.supportedLocalesOf;
      try { Object.defineProperty(pageGlobal.Intl, 'DateTimeFormat', { value: WrappedDTF, writable: true, configurable: true, enumerable: false }); } catch (_) {}
    }
  }

  // --- 12. GEOLOCATION STEALTH (Fixes B10) ---
  if (surfaces.geolocation && profile.geolocation) {
    const geoCoords = profile.geolocation;
    const geoProto = Object.getPrototypeOf(nav.geolocation || {});
    if (geoProto?.getCurrentPosition) {
      const makePos = () => {
        const coords = Object.create(pageGlobal.GeolocationCoordinates?.prototype || Object.prototype);
        Object.defineProperties(coords, {
          latitude: { value: Number(geoCoords.latitude) || 0, enumerable: true },
          longitude: { value: Number(geoCoords.longitude) || 0, enumerable: true },
          altitude: { value: null, enumerable: true },
          accuracy: { value: Number(geoCoords.accuracy) || 25, enumerable: true },
          altitudeAccuracy: { value: null, enumerable: true },
          heading: { value: null, enumerable: true },
          speed: { value: null, enumerable: true }
        });
        const pos = Object.create(pageGlobal.GeolocationPosition?.prototype || Object.prototype);
        Object.defineProperties(pos, {
          coords: { value: coords, enumerable: true },
          timestamp: { value: Date.now(), enumerable: true }
        });
        return pos;
      };

      geoProto.getCurrentPosition = wrapNative(geoProto.getCurrentPosition, {
        apply(target, receiver, args) {
          const cb = args[0];
          if (typeof cb === 'function') setTimeout(() => { try { cb(makePos()); } catch (_) {} }, 10);
        }
      }, 'getCurrentPosition');
    }
  }

  return { applied: true, profileId: profile.id, mode: profile.mode || 'full' };
}

----- END FILE CONTENT -----

import { MATCH_ORIGIN_AS_FALLBACK_MIN_CHROMIUM } from './content-scripts.js';

/**
 * Runtime capability detection for Chromium-family extension hosts.
 * Browser names are hints for display only; API features choose strategies.
 * No browsing URLs, history or raw User-Agent is returned to diagnostics.
 */
function hasMethod(value, key) {
  return Boolean(value && typeof value[key] === 'function');
}

function safeString(value) {
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : '';
}


function detectNativeOs(nav, userAgent) {
  const platformHint = safeString(nav?.userAgentData?.platform);
  const navigatorPlatform = safeString(nav?.platform);
  const evidence = `${platformHint} ${navigatorPlatform} ${userAgent}`.toLowerCase();
  // Check Android/iOS before Linux because Android UA strings include Linux.
  if (/android/u.test(evidence)) return 'android';
  if (/iphone|ipad|ipod|ios/u.test(evidence)) return 'ios';
  if (/windows|win32|win64/u.test(evidence)) return 'windows';
  if (/macintosh|mac os|macintel|macos/u.test(evidence)) return 'macos';
  if (/linux|x11/u.test(evidence)) return 'linux';
  return 'unknown';
}

export function detectBrowserIdentity(api = globalThis.chrome, nav = globalThis.navigator) {
  const userAgent = safeString(nav?.userAgent);
  const brands = Array.isArray(nav?.userAgentData?.brands)
    ? nav.userAgentData.brands.map((entry) => safeString(entry?.brand).toLowerCase())
    : [];
  const joinedBrands = brands.join(' ');
  const candidates = [joinedBrands, userAgent].join(' ');
  let family = 'unknown';

  // Detect forks before generic Chromium/Chrome substrings where the host exposes them.
  if (/\bquetta\b/iu.test(candidates)) family = 'quetta';
  else if (/\bkiwi(?:\s+browser)?\b/iu.test(candidates)) family = 'kiwi';
  else if (/\byabrowser\//iu.test(userAgent) || /\byandex\b/iu.test(joinedBrands)) family = 'yandex';
  else if (/\b(?:edga|edgios|edg)\//iu.test(userAgent) || joinedBrands.includes('microsoft edge')) family = 'edge';
  else if (/\b(?:firefox|fxios)\//iu.test(userAgent) || joinedBrands.includes('firefox')) family = 'firefox';
  else if (/\bopr\//iu.test(userAgent) || joinedBrands.includes('opera')) family = 'opera';
  else if (/\bbrave\b/iu.test(candidates)) family = 'brave';
  else if (/\bsamsungbrowser\//iu.test(userAgent)) family = 'samsung-internet';
  else if (joinedBrands.includes('google chrome')) family = 'chrome';
  else if (joinedBrands.includes('chromium') || /\b(?:chrome|chromium)\//iu.test(userAgent)) family = 'chromium';

  // For Chromium forks, report the engine major rather than the fork app's
  // own version (e.g. Quetta/2.x can embed Chrome/139). Firefox uses its own
  // engine-facing UA token. Prefer UA-CH brand versions only as a fallback.
  let versionMatch = null;
  if (family === 'firefox') {
    versionMatch = /(?:Firefox|FxiOS)\/(\d+)(?:\.|\s|$)/iu.exec(userAgent);
  } else if (family === 'edge') {
    versionMatch = /(?:Chrome|Chromium)\/(\d+)(?:\.|\s|$)/iu.exec(userAgent)
      ?? /(?:EdgA|EdgiOS|Edg)\/(\d+)(?:\.|\s|$)/iu.exec(userAgent);
  } else {
    versionMatch = /(?:Chrome|Chromium)\/(\d+)(?:\.|\s|$)/iu.exec(userAgent);
  }
  let browserMajor = versionMatch ? Number(versionMatch[1]) : null;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(browserMajor) || browserMajor <= 0) {
    const fullBrands = Array.isArray(nav?.userAgentData?.brands) ? nav.userAgentData.brands : [];
    const preferred = fullBrands.find((entry) => /(?:google chrome|chromium|microsoft edge)/iu.test(safeString(entry?.brand)));
    const brandMajor = /^(\d+)/u.exec(safeString(preferred?.version));
    browserMajor = brandMajor ? Number(brandMajor[1]) : null;
  }
  const validMajor = Number.isSafeInteger(browserMajor) && browserMajor > 0 ? browserMajor : null;
  const mobile = typeof nav?.userAgentData?.mobile === 'boolean'
    ? nav.userAgentData.mobile
    : /\b(?:Android|Mobile|iPhone|iPad)\b/iu.test(userAgent);
  let manifest = null;
  try {
    if (hasMethod(api?.runtime, 'getManifest')) manifest = api.runtime.getManifest();
  } catch {
    // Runtime identification must degrade safely in partial browser shims.
  }

  return Object.freeze({
    family,
    major: validMajor,
    mobile,
    nativeOs: detectNativeOs(nav, userAgent),
    manifestVersion: Number.isInteger(manifest?.manifest_version) ? manifest.manifest_version : null,
  });
}

export function detectApiCapabilities(api = globalThis.chrome, nav = globalThis.navigator) {
  const scripting = api?.scripting;
  const dnr = api?.declarativeNetRequest;
  const webNavigation = api?.webNavigation;
  const tabs = api?.tabs;
  const storage = api?.storage;
  const alarms = api?.alarms;
  const identity = detectBrowserIdentity(api, nav);
  const isMv3 = identity.manifestVersion === 3;

  const capabilities = {
    extensionStorageLocal: hasMethod(storage?.local, 'get') && hasMethod(storage?.local, 'set'),
    dynamicNetRequest: isMv3 && hasMethod(dnr, 'getDynamicRules') && hasMethod(dnr, 'updateDynamicRules'),
    scriptingExecuteMain: isMv3 && hasMethod(scripting, 'executeScript'),
    dynamicContentScripts: isMv3
      && hasMethod(scripting, 'getRegisteredContentScripts')
      && hasMethod(scripting, 'registerContentScripts')
      && hasMethod(scripting, 'unregisterContentScripts'),
    // This registration option was added in Chromium 119. Unknown engine
    // versions are probed by attempting registration; a rejected option falls
    // back to an explicitly partial frame-coverage report.
    matchOriginAsFallback: identity.major === null
      || identity.major >= MATCH_ORIGIN_AS_FALLBACK_MIN_CHROMIUM,
    webNavigationCommitted: hasMethod(webNavigation?.onCommitted, 'addListener'),
    webNavigationFrames: hasMethod(webNavigation, 'getAllFrames'),
    tabsQuery: hasMethod(tabs, 'query'),
    alarms: hasMethod(alarms, 'create') && hasMethod(alarms, 'clear'),
    // Only claim blocking webRequest in the dedicated legacy manifest variant.
    legacyBlockingWebRequest: identity.manifestVersion === 2
      && hasMethod(api?.webRequest?.onBeforeSendHeaders, 'addListener'),
  };

  let pageStrategy = 'unavailable';
  if (capabilities.dynamicContentScripts && capabilities.scriptingExecuteMain) {
    pageStrategy = 'document-start';
  } else if (capabilities.scriptingExecuteMain && capabilities.webNavigationCommitted) {
    pageStrategy = 'navigation-fallback';
  }

  let headerStrategy = 'unavailable';
  if (capabilities.dynamicNetRequest) headerStrategy = 'declarative-rules';
  else if (capabilities.legacyBlockingWebRequest) headerStrategy = 'blocking-webRequest';

  let supportLevel = 'limited';
  if (headerStrategy !== 'unavailable' && pageStrategy === 'document-start') {
    supportLevel = capabilities.matchOriginAsFallback ? 'full' : 'frame-limited';
  } else if (headerStrategy !== 'unavailable' && pageStrategy === 'navigation-fallback') supportLevel = 'adaptive';
  else if (headerStrategy !== 'unavailable') supportLevel = 'headers-only';
  else if (pageStrategy !== 'unavailable') supportLevel = 'page-only';

  return Object.freeze({
    identity,
    capabilities: Object.freeze(capabilities),
    initialStrategy: Object.freeze({ header: headerStrategy, page: pageStrategy, level: supportLevel }),
  });
}

/** Compact, safe-to-export diagnostics. No raw UA, URLs, origins or page history. */
export function makeCapabilityReport(detected, effective = {}) {
  const page = effective.page ?? detected.initialStrategy.page;
  const header = effective.header ?? detected.initialStrategy.header;
  const matchOriginAsFallback = typeof effective.matchOriginAsFallback === 'boolean'
    ? effective.matchOriginAsFallback
    : detected.capabilities.matchOriginAsFallback;
  let level = 'limited';
  if (header !== 'unavailable' && page === 'document-start') level = matchOriginAsFallback ? 'full' : 'frame-limited';
  else if (header !== 'unavailable' && page === 'navigation-fallback') level = 'adaptive';
  else if (header !== 'unavailable') level = 'headers-only';
  else if (page !== 'unavailable') level = 'page-only';

  return {
    browser: detected.identity.family,
    browserMajor: detected.identity.major,
    mobile: detected.identity.mobile,
    nativeOs: detected.identity.nativeOs,
    manifestVersion: detected.identity.manifestVersion,
    signalPolicy: Object.freeze({
      hardwareCapacity: 'native',
      touchInput: 'native',
      performanceTiming: 'native',
      networkMetrics: 'native',
      systemClock: 'native',
      timezone: 'native',
      viewportAndGraphics: 'native',
      osHardwareHighEntropy: 'native',
      workerContexts: 'native-uncontrolled',
    }),
    frameCoverage: page !== 'document-start'
      ? 'document-start-not-guaranteed'
      : matchOriginAsFallback ? 'origin-fallback-enabled' : 'about-blank-srcdoc-may-not-match',
    workerCoverage: 'native-uncontrolled',
    supportLevel: level,
    strategy: { header, page },
    api: { ...detected.capabilities, matchOriginAsFallback },
  };
}

import { toMatchPatterns, RESERVED_DOMAINS } from './domains.js';

export const OWNED_SCRIPT_PREFIX = 'veil-profile-studio-';
export const DEFAULT_SCRIPT_ID = 'veil-profile-studio-default-v1';
// Known dynamic-script IDs from the upstream package and earlier Veil builds.
// These are only visible within this extension's own registration namespace.
export const LEGACY_SCRIPT_PREFIXES = Object.freeze(['ua-switcher-']);
export const LEGACY_SCRIPT_IDS = Object.freeze(['content', 'inject']);
export const MATCH_ORIGIN_AS_FALLBACK_MIN_CHROMIUM = 119;

export function isOwnedContentScriptId(id) {
  return typeof id === 'string' && (
    id.startsWith(OWNED_SCRIPT_PREFIX)
    || LEGACY_SCRIPT_PREFIXES.some((prefix) => id.startsWith(prefix))
    || LEGACY_SCRIPT_IDS.includes(id)
  );
}

function createRegistration(id, profileId, matches, excludeMatches, { matchOriginAsFallback = false, launcherProfileBundle = false } = {}) {
  if (!matches.length) return null;
  const registration = {
    id,
    matches,
    excludeMatches: [...new Set(excludeMatches)],
    // The profile script is self-contained. A shared MAIN-world helper exposed on
    // globalThis could be monkey-patched between two registered files by the page.
    js: [launcherProfileBundle ? 'main-world/launcher-profile-bundle.js' : `main-world/profiles/${profileId}.js`],
    allFrames: true,
    runAt: 'document_start',
    world: 'MAIN',
    persistAcrossSessions: true,
  };
  if (matchOriginAsFallback) registration.matchOriginAsFallback = true;
  return registration;
}

/**
 * Produce registrations without mutating browser state. More-specific site
 * overrides exclude their subdomains from a broader rule, so overlapping
 * override trees never run two different profiles in the same document.
 */
export function buildContentScriptRegistrations(settings, { matchOriginAsFallback = false, launcherProfileBundle = false } = {}) {
  if (!settings?.enabled) return [];
  const reservedPatterns = toMatchPatterns(RESERVED_DOMAINS);
  const overrideDomains = settings.overrides.map((entry) => entry.domain);
  const baseMatches = settings.domainMode === 'allowlist'
    ? toMatchPatterns(settings.domains)
    : ['http://*/*', 'https://*/*'];
  const baseExcluded = [...reservedPatterns];
  if (settings.domainMode === 'blocklist') baseExcluded.push(...toMatchPatterns(settings.domains));
  if (overrideDomains.length && !launcherProfileBundle) baseExcluded.push(...toMatchPatterns(overrideDomains));

  const registrations = [];
  const registrationOptions = { matchOriginAsFallback, launcherProfileBundle };
  const base = createRegistration(DEFAULT_SCRIPT_ID, settings.profileId, baseMatches, baseExcluded, registrationOptions);
  if (base) registrations.push(base);

  // A desktop-launcher build contains a fixed profile library and seed bundle.
  // A single document_start script selects the validated profile by hostname;
  // DNR still uses the equivalent site assignments for HTTP headers.
  if (launcherProfileBundle) return registrations.filter(Boolean);

  settings.overrides.forEach((entry, index) => {
    const nestedOverrides = settings.overrides
      .filter((candidate) => candidate.domain !== entry.domain && candidate.domain.endsWith(`.${entry.domain}`))
      .map((candidate) => candidate.domain);
    registrations.push(createRegistration(
      `${OWNED_SCRIPT_PREFIX}site-${index}`,
      entry.profileId,
      toMatchPatterns([entry.domain]),
      [...reservedPatterns, ...toMatchPatterns(nestedOverrides)],
      registrationOptions,
    ));
  });

  return registrations.filter(Boolean);
}

----- BEGIN FILE CONTENT -----
import { PROFILES } from './profiles.js';
import { RESERVED_DOMAINS } from './domains.js';

// IDs 1-199 retain the original manager's range. 1000-1199 are per-site
// target/initiator rules (100 overrides x 2); all are owned by this extension.
export const MANAGED_RULE_IDS = Object.freeze(Array.from({ length: 1199 }, (_, index) => index + 1));
// Explicitly include main_frame: Chrome's implicit resource-type default for DNR
// modifyHeaders rules does not cover top-level document requests. Keep this list
// conservative for the MV3 minimum (Chromium 102) and common Chromium forks.
export const PROFILE_RESOURCE_TYPES = Object.freeze([
  'main_frame', 'sub_frame', 'stylesheet', 'script', 'image', 'font', 'object',
  'xmlhttprequest', 'ping', 'csp_report', 'media', 'websocket', 'other',
]);
const PROFILE_NON_FRAME_RESOURCE_TYPES = Object.freeze(
  PROFILE_RESOURCE_TYPES.filter((type) => type !== 'main_frame' && type !== 'sub_frame'),
);
const RULE_ID = Object.freeze({
  baseTarget: 1,
  baseInitiator: 2,
  reservedFrame: 101,
  reservedTarget: 102,
  reservedInitiator: 103,
  blockedFrame: 104,
  blockedTarget: 105,
  blockedInitiator: 106,
  overrideTargetBase: 1000,
  overrideInitiatorBase: 1100,
});

function headerProfileOperations(profile, nativeOs = 'unknown') {
  const operations = [
    { operation: 'set', header: 'user-agent', value: profile.userAgent },
    { operation: 'set', header: 'sec-ch-ua', value: profile.secChUa },
    { operation: 'set', header: 'sec-ch-ua-mobile', value: profile.secChUaMobile },
    { operation: 'set', header: 'sec-ch-ua-platform', value: profile.secChUaPlatform },

    { operation: 'set', header: 'sec-ch-ua-full-version-list', value: profile.secChUaFullVersionList },
  ];
  if (typeof profile.acceptLanguage === 'string' && profile.acceptLanguage) {
    operations.push({ operation: 'set', header: 'accept-language', value: profile.acceptLanguage });
  }

  // Keep physical OS/device hints native for a known same-OS profile. When the
  // selected OS differs from the actual host, use the coherent profile template
  // in both HTTP and JS rather than leaking a Linux kernel/CPU behind Windows.
  // Unknown native OS is fail-open and preserves native high-entropy hints.
  if (nativeOs !== 'unknown' && profile.os !== nativeOs) {
    operations.push(
      { operation: 'set', header: 'sec-ch-ua-arch', value: `"${profile.highEntropy.architecture}"` },
      { operation: 'set', header: 'sec-ch-ua-bitness', value: `"${profile.highEntropy.bitness}"` },
      { operation: 'set', header: 'sec-ch-ua-model', value: `"${profile.highEntropy.model}"` },
      { operation: 'set', header: 'sec-ch-ua-platform-version', value: `"${profile.highEntropy.platformVersion}"` },
      { operation: 'set', header: 'sec-ch-ua-wow64', value: profile.highEntropy.wow64 ? '?1' : '?0' },
      { operation: 'set', header: 'sec-ch-ua-form-factors', value: profile.highEntropy.formFactors.map((factor) => `"${factor}"`).join(', ') },
    );
  }
  return operations;
}

function profileRule(id, priority, profile, domainField, domains, { excludeFrames = false, nativeOs = 'unknown' } = {}) {
  const condition = {};
  if (domainField && domains?.length) condition[domainField] = domains;
  condition.resourceTypes = excludeFrames ? [...PROFILE_NON_FRAME_RESOURCE_TYPES] : [...PROFILE_RESOURCE_TYPES];
  return {
    id,
    priority,
    action: {
      type: 'modifyHeaders',
      requestHeaders: headerProfileOperations(profile, nativeOs),
    },
    condition,
  };
}

function allowRule(id, priority, actionType, domainField, domains, { framesOnly = false, nonFramesOnly = false } = {}) {
  const condition = { [domainField]: domains };
  if (framesOnly) condition.resourceTypes = ['main_frame', 'sub_frame'];
  if (nonFramesOnly) condition.excludedResourceTypes = ['main_frame', 'sub_frame'];
  return { id, priority, action: { type: actionType }, condition };
}

/**
 * Build a bounded rule set. Target-site overrides outrank initiator-site
 * overrides; within the same dimension, a longer hostname wins over its parent.
 * The protected challenge host always outranks all profile rules.
 */
export function buildDnrRules(settings, profileOrCatalog = PROFILES, nativeOs = 'unknown', profileLibrary = null) {
  if (!settings?.enabled) return [];
  const sourceCatalog = profileOrCatalog?.userAgent ? { [profileOrCatalog.id]: profileOrCatalog } : profileOrCatalog;
  const catalog = { ...sourceCatalog };
  const records = Array.isArray(profileLibrary?.profiles) ? profileLibrary.profiles : [];
  const activeRecord = records.find((entry) => entry.id === profileLibrary?.activeProfileId);
  const acceptLanguageFor = (locale) => {
    if (typeof locale !== 'string' || !locale) return '';
    const baseLanguage = locale.split('-')[0];
    return locale === baseLanguage
      ? `${locale},en-US;q=0.9,en;q=0.8`
      : `${locale},${baseLanguage};q=0.9,en-US;q=0.8,en;q=0.7`;
  };
  if (activeRecord?.baseProfileId === settings.profileId && activeRecord.mode === 'full' && activeRecord.surfaces?.intl === true && activeRecord.locale) {
    catalog[settings.profileId] = { ...catalog[settings.profileId], acceptLanguage: acceptLanguageFor(activeRecord.locale) };
  }
  const baseProfile = catalog[settings.profileId] ?? PROFILES[settings.profileId] ?? PROFILES['chrome-android'];
  const domains = [...new Set((settings.domains ?? []).filter((domain) => typeof domain === 'string'))];
  const overrides = (settings.overrides ?? []).filter((entry) =>
    entry && typeof entry.domain === 'string' && Object.hasOwn(catalog, entry.profileId));
  const rules = [];

  if (settings.domainMode === 'allowlist') {
    if (domains.length) {
      rules.push(profileRule(RULE_ID.baseTarget, 1, baseProfile, 'requestDomains', domains, { nativeOs }));
      rules.push(profileRule(RULE_ID.baseInitiator, 1, baseProfile, 'initiatorDomains', domains, { excludeFrames: true, nativeOs }));
    }
  } else {
    rules.push(profileRule(RULE_ID.baseTarget, 1, baseProfile, null, null, { nativeOs }));
    if (domains.length) {
      rules.push(allowRule(RULE_ID.blockedFrame, 100, 'allowAllRequests', 'requestDomains', domains, { framesOnly: true }));
      rules.push(allowRule(RULE_ID.blockedTarget, 100, 'allow', 'requestDomains', domains, { nonFramesOnly: true }));
      rules.push(allowRule(RULE_ID.blockedInitiator, 100, 'allow', 'initiatorDomains', domains, { nonFramesOnly: true }));
    }
  }

  overrides.forEach((entry, index) => {
    const profile = catalog[entry.profileId];
    // Length is bounded to 253. Target priorities 1001-1253 always exceed
    // initiator priorities 501-753, preserving documented conflict semantics.
    rules.push(profileRule(
      RULE_ID.overrideTargetBase + index,
      1000 + entry.domain.length,
      profile,
      'requestDomains',
      [entry.domain],
      { nativeOs },
    ));
    rules.push(profileRule(
      RULE_ID.overrideInitiatorBase + index,
      500 + entry.domain.length,
      profile,
      'initiatorDomains',
      [entry.domain],
      { excludeFrames: true, nativeOs },
    ));
  });

  // Per-profile language is intentionally an HTTP header rule separate from
  // identity rules. DNR cannot gate on Accept-CH/header presence; language is
  // only overridden when the user supplied a locale in the profile definition.
  const assignments = Array.isArray(profileLibrary?.assignments) ? profileLibrary.assignments : [];
  assignments.forEach((assignment, index) => {
    if (index >= 100 || !assignment || typeof assignment.domain !== 'string') return;
    const record = records.find((entry) => entry.id === assignment.profileId);
    if (record?.mode !== 'full' || record.surfaces?.intl !== true || !record.locale) return;
    const acceptLanguage = acceptLanguageFor(record.locale);
    const targetCondition = { requestDomains: [assignment.domain], resourceTypes: [...PROFILE_RESOURCE_TYPES] };
    const initiatorCondition = { initiatorDomains: [assignment.domain], excludedResourceTypes: ['main_frame', 'sub_frame'] };
    rules.push({ id: 300 + index, priority: 1100 + assignment.domain.length, action: { type: 'modifyHeaders', requestHeaders: [{ operation: 'set', header: 'accept-language', value: acceptLanguage }] }, condition: targetCondition });
    rules.push({ id: 400 + index, priority: 600 + assignment.domain.length, action: { type: 'modifyHeaders', requestHeaders: [{ operation: 'set', header: 'accept-language', value: acceptLanguage }] }, condition: initiatorCondition });
  });

  // Allow actions suppress all lower-priority modification rules on protected hosts.
  rules.push(allowRule(RULE_ID.reservedFrame, 2000, 'allowAllRequests', 'requestDomains', [...RESERVED_DOMAINS], { framesOnly: true }));
  rules.push(allowRule(RULE_ID.reservedTarget, 2000, 'allow', 'requestDomains', [...RESERVED_DOMAINS], { nonFramesOnly: true }));
  rules.push(allowRule(RULE_ID.reservedInitiator, 2000, 'allow', 'initiatorDomains', [...RESERVED_DOMAINS], { nonFramesOnly: true }));
  return rules;
}

import { InputValidationError } from './errors.js';

const MAX_DOMAIN_COUNT = 200;
const MAX_OVERRIDE_COUNT = 100;
const MAX_LINE_LENGTH = 253;
export const RESERVED_DOMAINS = Object.freeze(['challenges.cloudflare.com']);

/** Accept hostnames or URL-like entries, normalize IDN via URL, and reject paths/wildcards. */
export function normalizeDomain(input) {
  if (typeof input !== 'string') throw new InputValidationError('Domain must be text.');
  let value = input.trim();
  if (!value) return null;
  if (value.length > MAX_LINE_LENGTH + 32) throw new InputValidationError('Domain entry is too long.');
  if (/[\s*\\]/u.test(value)) throw new InputValidationError('Invalid domain syntax.');

  if (!/^[a-z][a-z\d+.-]*:\/\//iu.test(value)) value = `https://${value}`;
  const authority = value.match(/^[a-z][a-z\d+.-]*:\/\/([^/?#]*)/iu)?.[1] ?? '';
  const hostPort = authority.slice(authority.lastIndexOf('@') + 1);
  // WHATWG URL canonicalizes default ports away, so validate the raw authority first.
  if (hostPort.includes(':')) throw new InputValidationError('Ports are not allowed in domain rules.');
  let url;
  try { url = new URL(value); }
  catch { throw new InputValidationError('Invalid domain.'); }

  if (!['http:', 'https:'].includes(url.protocol)) throw new InputValidationError('Only HTTP(S) domains are supported.');
  if (url.username || url.password || !url.hostname) throw new InputValidationError('Invalid domain.');
  if (url.port) throw new InputValidationError('Ports are not allowed in domain rules.');
  if (url.pathname !== '/' || url.search || url.hash) {
    // URL-style input is accepted only when it points to a host, not a specific path.
    throw new InputValidationError('Enter a hostname only, without a path.');
  }

  const hostname = url.hostname.toLowerCase().replace(/\.$/u, '');
  if (!hostname || hostname.length > MAX_LINE_LENGTH || hostname.startsWith('.') || hostname.endsWith('..')) {
    throw new InputValidationError('Invalid hostname.');
  }
  if (!hostname.includes('.') && hostname !== 'localhost') throw new InputValidationError('Use a full hostname or localhost.');
  if (!/^[a-z\d.-]+$/u.test(hostname) || hostname.split('.').some((label) => !label || label.length > 63 || label.startsWith('-') || label.endsWith('-'))) {
    throw new InputValidationError('Invalid hostname.');
  }
  return hostname;
}

export function parseDomainList(text) {
  if (typeof text !== 'string') throw new InputValidationError('Domain list must be text.');
  const inputLines = text.split(/\r?\n/u).map((line) => line.trim()).filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith('#'));
  if (inputLines.length > MAX_DOMAIN_COUNT) throw new InputValidationError(`At most ${MAX_DOMAIN_COUNT} domains are allowed.`);
  const normalized = [];
  const seen = new Set();
  for (const line of inputLines) {
    const domain = normalizeDomain(line);
    if (domain && !seen.has(domain)) {
      normalized.push(domain);
      seen.add(domain);
    }
  }
  return normalized;
}

export function toMatchPatterns(domains) {
  return [...new Set(domains.flatMap((domain) => [`*://${domain}/*`, `*://*.${domain}/*`]))];
}

export function isHostnameIncluded(hostname, domains) {
  const host = String(hostname ?? '').toLowerCase().replace(/\.$/u, '');
  return domains.some((domain) => host === domain || host.endsWith(`.${domain}`));
}

export const DOMAIN_LIMITS = Object.freeze({ maxCount: MAX_DOMAIN_COUNT, maxOverrides: MAX_OVERRIDE_COUNT, maxLineLength: MAX_LINE_LENGTH });


/** Parse one explicit site profile per line: hostname = profile-id. */
export function parseOverrideList(text, validProfileIds) {
  if (typeof text !== 'string') throw new InputValidationError('Site overrides must be text.');
  if (!Array.isArray(validProfileIds) || validProfileIds.some((id) => typeof id !== 'string')) {
    throw new InputValidationError('A valid profile catalogue is required.');
  }
  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/u).map((line) => line.trim()).filter((line) => line && !line.startsWith('#'));
  if (lines.length > MAX_OVERRIDE_COUNT) throw new InputValidationError(`At most ${MAX_OVERRIDE_COUNT} site overrides are allowed.`);
  const byDomain = new Map();
  for (const line of lines) {
    const separator = line.indexOf('=');
    if (separator < 1 || separator !== line.lastIndexOf('=')) {
      throw new InputValidationError('Invalid site override. Use hostname = profile-id.');
    }
    const domain = normalizeDomain(line.slice(0, separator).trim());
    const profileId = line.slice(separator + 1).trim();
    if (!domain) throw new InputValidationError('Missing hostname in site override.');
    if (isHostnameIncluded(domain, RESERVED_DOMAINS)) {
      throw new InputValidationError('This protected domain cannot have a profile override.');
    }
    if (!validProfileIds.includes(profileId)) throw new InputValidationError('Unknown profile id in site override.');
    if (byDomain.has(domain)) throw new InputValidationError('Duplicate site override.');
    byDomain.set(domain, profileId);
  }
  return [...byDomain].map(([domain, profileId]) => ({ domain, profileId }));
}

export function formatOverrideList(overrides) {
  if (!Array.isArray(overrides)) return '';
  return overrides.map(({ domain, profileId }) => `${domain} = ${profileId}`).join('\n');
}

/** Errors intentionally safe to present to the user; never embed URLs or raw input. */
export class InputValidationError extends TypeError {
  constructor(message) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'InputValidationError';
  }
}

export class StateConflictError extends Error {
  constructor(message = 'Settings changed in another extension window. The latest state must be loaded before retrying.') {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'StateConflictError';
  }
}

export function getSafeFailureCode(error) {
  if (error instanceof StateConflictError) return 'STATE_CONFLICT';
  if (error instanceof InputValidationError) return 'INVALID_INPUT';
  if (error instanceof AggregateError) return 'ROLLBACK_FAILED';
  return 'OPERATION_FAILED';
}

export function getSafePublicError(error, operation = 'save') {
  if (error instanceof StateConflictError) return 'Settings changed in another extension window. The latest state must be loaded before retrying.';
  if (error instanceof InputValidationError) return error.message;
  switch (operation) {
    case 'load':
      return 'Could not load local settings. Reload the extension and retry.';
    case 'diagnostics':
      return 'Diagnostics could not run. Reload the extension and retry.';
    case 'reset':
    case 'save':
      return 'Could not safely apply these settings. Verify the inputs and retry.';
    default:
      return 'The operation failed. Reload the extension and retry.';
  }
}

import { PROFILES } from './profiles.js';
import { InputValidationError } from './errors.js';
import { isHostnameIncluded, parseOverrideList, RESERVED_DOMAINS } from './domains.js';

export const PROFILE_LIBRARY_KEY = 'veilProfileStudioProfileLibraryV1';
export const PROFILE_LIBRARY_RECOVERY_KEY = 'veilProfileStudioProfileLibraryRecoveryV1';
export const PROFILE_LIBRARY_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;
export const PROFILE_EXPORT_FORMAT = 'veil-profile-studio-profile-definitions';
export const PROFILE_EXPORT_VERSION = 1;
export const MAX_PROFILE_LIBRARY_BYTES = 512 * 1024;
export const MAX_PROFILE_RECORDS = 100;
export const PROFILE_MODES = Object.freeze(['identity', 'hardened', 'full']);

export const PROFILE_SURFACES = Object.freeze({
  canvas: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceCanvas', modes: ['hardened', 'full'], status: 'covered', warningKey: 'surfaceCanvasWarning' }),
  webgl: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceWebGL', modes: ['hardened', 'full'], status: 'covered', warningKey: 'surfaceWebGLWarning' }),
  webgpu: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceWebGPU', modes: ['hardened', 'full'], status: 'uncovered', warningKey: 'surfaceUncoveredWarning' }),
  audio: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceAudio', modes: ['hardened', 'full'], status: 'covered', warningKey: 'surfaceAudioWarning' }),
  geometry: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceGeometry', modes: ['hardened', 'full'], status: 'uncovered', warningKey: 'surfaceGeometryWarning' }),
  hardware: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceHardware', modes: ['hardened', 'full'], status: 'covered', warningKey: 'surfaceHardwareWarning' }),
  mediaDevices: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceMediaDevices', modes: ['hardened', 'full'], status: 'uncovered', warningKey: 'surfaceMediaDevicesWarning' }),
  screen: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceScreen', modes: ['full'], status: 'covered', warningKey: 'surfaceScreenWarning' }),
  touch: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceTouch', modes: ['full'], status: 'covered', warningKey: 'surfaceTouchWarning' }),
  plugins: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfacePlugins', modes: ['full'], status: 'covered', warningKey: 'surfacePluginsWarning' }),
  connection: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceConnection', modes: ['full'], status: 'uncovered', warningKey: 'surfaceUncoveredWarning' }),
  intl: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceIntl', modes: ['full'], status: 'covered', warningKey: 'surfaceIntlWarning' }),
  geolocation: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceGeolocation', modes: ['full'], status: 'covered', warningKey: 'surfaceGeolocationWarning' }),
  fonts: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceFonts', modes: ['full'], status: 'uncovered', warningKey: 'surfaceUncoveredWarning' }),
  webrtc: Object.freeze({ labelKey: 'surfaceWebRTC', modes: ['full'], status: 'covered', warningKey: 'surfaceWebRTCWarning' }),
});

const SURFACE_KEYS = Object.freeze(Object.keys(PROFILE_SURFACES));
const SEED_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,128}$/u;
const PROFILE_ID_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$/u;
const LOCALE_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z]{2,3}(?:-[A-Za-z0-9]{2,8})*$/u;
const MAX_NOTES_LENGTH = 2000;

function isPlainRecord(value) {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return false;
  const prototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
  return prototype === Object.prototype || prototype === null;
}

export function newSeed() {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(24);
  try {
    if (globalThis.crypto?.getRandomValues) globalThis.crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
    else for (let index = 0; index < bytes.length; index += 1) bytes[index] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
  } catch {
    for (let index = 0; index < bytes.length; index += 1) bytes[index] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
  }
  return [...bytes].map((value) => value.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

function nowIso() {
  return new Date().toISOString();
}

function defaultSurfaces(mode = 'identity') {
  const enabled = new Set(mode === 'identity' ? [] : ['canvas', 'webgl', 'audio', 'geometry', 'hardware', 'touch', 'plugins']);
  if (mode === 'full') {
    enabled.add('intl');
    enabled.add('geolocation');
    enabled.add('screen');
    enabled.add('mediaDevices');
    enabled.add('webrtc');
  }
  return Object.fromEntries(SURFACE_KEYS.map((key) => [key, enabled.has(key)]));
}

export function defaultProfileSurfaces(mode = 'identity') {
  if (!PROFILE_MODES.includes(mode)) throw new InputValidationError('Unsupported profile mode.');
  return { ...defaultSurfaces(mode) };
}

function safeTimezone(value, strict) {
  if (value === '' || value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.length > 100) {
    if (strict) throw new InputValidationError('Timezone must be a valid IANA timezone name.');
    return '';
  }
  try {
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en', { timeZone: value }).format(0);
    return value;
  } catch {
    if (strict) throw new InputValidationError('Timezone must be a valid IANA timezone name.');
    return '';
  }
}

function safeLocale(value, strict) {
  if (value === '' || value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.length > 64 || !LOCALE_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    if (strict) throw new InputValidationError('Locale must be a BCP 47-style language tag, such as en-US.');
    return '';
  }
  try {
    const [canonical] = Intl.getCanonicalLocales(value);
    return canonical;
  } catch {
    if (strict) throw new InputValidationError('Locale must be a valid language tag.');
    return '';
  }
}

function normalizeGeo(value, strict) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;
  if (!isPlainRecord(value)) {
    if (strict) throw new InputValidationError('Geolocation must be a latitude/longitude object.');
    return null;
  }
  const { latitude, longitude, accuracy = 100 } = value;
  if (!Number.isFinite(latitude) || latitude < -90 || latitude > 90
      || !Number.isFinite(longitude) || longitude < -180 || longitude > 180
      || !Number.isFinite(accuracy) || accuracy < 0 || accuracy > 100000) {
    if (strict) throw new InputValidationError('Geolocation coordinates or accuracy are outside valid bounds.');
    return null;
  }
  return { latitude, longitude, accuracy };
}

function templateProvenance(baseProfileId) {
  const profile = PROFILES[baseProfileId];
  const source = 'Bundled Veil UA/UA-CH template in shared/profiles.js';
  const field = (fieldSource, confidence = 'high') => ({ source: fieldSource, confidence });
  return {
    templateId: baseProfileId,
    fields: {
      userAgent: field(source),
      browserVersion: field(source),
      os: field(source),
      architecture: field(source),
      bitness: field(source),
      platformVersion: field(source),
      model: field(profile?.highEntropy?.model || (profile?.mobile ? 'Mobile Device' : 'Desktop PC')),
      mobileFormFactor: field(source),
      gpu: field('Stealth-aligned hardware GPU vendor and renderer'),
      screenResolution: field('Dynamic coherent viewport and screen profile'),
      devicePixelRatio: field('Aligned DPR scaling factor'),
      hardwareConcurrency: field('Coherent CPU concurrency level'),
      deviceMemory: field('Reported device memory capacity'),
      touchPointer: field('Unified touch points & pointer query'),
      pluginsMimeTypes: field('Complete desktop PDF plugin suite or mobile empty list'),
      locale: field('User or preset supplied locale', 'user-supplied'),
      timezone: field('User or preset supplied IANA timezone', 'user-supplied'),
      geolocation: field('User or preset supplied coordinates', 'user-supplied'),
      webrtc: field('Protected local IP shield policy'),
    },
  };
}

export function createProfileRecord(baseProfileId, options = {}) {
  const base = PROFILES[baseProfileId];
  if (!base) throw new InputValidationError('Unknown bundled device template.');
  const timestamp = options.createdAt ?? nowIso();
  const id = options.id ?? baseProfileId;
  const mode = options.mode ?? 'identity';
  if (!PROFILE_MODES.includes(mode)) throw new InputValidationError('Unsupported profile mode.');
  const record = {
    id,
    name: options.name ?? `${base.browser === 'edge' ? 'Edge' : 'Chrome'} ${base.major} — ${base.os}`,
    notes: options.notes ?? '',
    baseProfileId,
    deviceTemplate: templateProvenance(baseProfileId),
    seed: options.seed ?? newSeed(),
    mode,
    locale: options.locale ?? '',
    timezone: options.timezone ?? '',
    geolocation: options.geolocation ?? null,
    surfaces: { ...defaultSurfaces(mode), ...(options.surfaces ?? {}) },
    screenWidth: options.screenWidth,
    screenHeight: options.screenHeight,
    devicePixelRatio: options.devicePixelRatio,
    cpuCores: options.cpuCores,
    memoryGb: options.memoryGb,
    maxTouchPoints: options.maxTouchPoints,
    gpuVendor: options.gpuVendor,
    gpuRenderer: options.gpuRenderer,
    createdAt: timestamp,
    updatedAt: options.updatedAt ?? timestamp,
  };
  return normalizeProfileRecord(record, { strict: true });
}

export function normalizeProfileRecord(raw, { strict = false } = {}) {
  const fail = (message) => { if (strict) throw new InputValidationError(message); };
  if (!isPlainRecord(raw)) {
    fail('Each profile definition must be an object.');
    return null;
  }
  const baseProfileId = typeof raw.baseProfileId === 'string' && Object.hasOwn(PROFILES, raw.baseProfileId)
    ? raw.baseProfileId : null;
  if (!baseProfileId) {
    fail('Profile must reference a known bundled device template.');
    return null;
  }
  const base = PROFILES[baseProfileId];
  const id = typeof raw.id === 'string' ? raw.id.trim() : '';
  if (!PROFILE_ID_PATTERN.test(id)) {
    fail('Profile ID must use lowercase letters, numbers and hyphens (1–64 characters).');
    return null;
  }
  const name = typeof raw.name === 'string' ? raw.name.trim() : '';
  if (!name || name.length > 80) {
    fail('Profile name must contain 1–80 characters.');
    return null;
  }
  const notes = typeof raw.notes === 'string' ? raw.notes : '';
  if (notes.length > MAX_NOTES_LENGTH) {
    fail(`Profile notes may not exceed ${MAX_NOTES_LENGTH} characters.`);
    return null;
  }
  const seed = typeof raw.seed === 'string' ? raw.seed : '';
  if (!SEED_PATTERN.test(seed)) {
    fail('Seed must contain 16–128 URL-safe characters.');
    return null;
  }
  const mode = raw.mode ?? 'identity';
  if (!PROFILE_MODES.includes(mode)) {
    fail('Unsupported profile mode.');
    return null;
  }

  const locale = safeLocale(raw.locale, strict);
  const timezone = safeTimezone(raw.timezone, strict);
  const geolocation = normalizeGeo(raw.geolocation, strict);
  const surfacesRaw = isPlainRecord(raw.surfaces) ? raw.surfaces : {};
  if (strict && Object.keys(surfacesRaw).some((key) => !SURFACE_KEYS.includes(key))) {
    throw new InputValidationError('Profile contains an unknown surface toggle.');
  }
  const surfaces = defaultSurfaces(mode);
  for (const key of SURFACE_KEYS) {
    if (surfacesRaw[key] === undefined) continue;
    if (typeof surfacesRaw[key] !== 'boolean') {
      fail(`Surface toggle ${key} must be true or false.`);
      continue;
    }
    surfaces[key] = surfacesRaw[key] && PROFILE_SURFACES[key].modes.includes(mode);
  }

  return {
    id,
    name,
    notes,
    baseProfileId,
    deviceTemplate: templateProvenance(baseProfileId),
    seed,
    mode,
    locale,
    timezone,
    geolocation,
    surfaces,
    screenWidth: typeof raw.screenWidth === 'number' && raw.screenWidth > 0 ? raw.screenWidth : undefined,
    screenHeight: typeof raw.screenHeight === 'number' && raw.screenHeight > 0 ? raw.screenHeight : undefined,
    devicePixelRatio: typeof raw.devicePixelRatio === 'number' && raw.devicePixelRatio > 0 ? raw.devicePixelRatio : undefined,
    cpuCores: typeof raw.cpuCores === 'number' && raw.cpuCores > 0 ? raw.cpuCores : undefined,
    memoryGb: typeof raw.memoryGb === 'number' && raw.memoryGb > 0 ? raw.memoryGb : undefined,
    maxTouchPoints: typeof raw.maxTouchPoints === 'number' && raw.maxTouchPoints >= 0 ? raw.maxTouchPoints : undefined,
    gpuVendor: typeof raw.gpuVendor === 'string' && raw.gpuVendor ? raw.gpuVendor : undefined,
    gpuRenderer: typeof raw.gpuRenderer === 'string' && raw.gpuRenderer ? raw.gpuRenderer : undefined,
    deviceCategory: base.mobile ? 'mobile' : 'desktop',
    createdAt: typeof raw.createdAt === 'string' && !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(raw.createdAt)) ? raw.createdAt : nowIso(),
    updatedAt: typeof raw.updatedAt === 'string' && !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(raw.updatedAt)) ? raw.updatedAt : nowIso(),
  };
}

export function validateProfileRecord(raw) {
  try {
    const profile = normalizeProfileRecord(raw, { strict: true });
    const unsupported = Object.keys(profile.surfaces).filter((key) => profile.surfaces[key] && PROFILE_SURFACES[key].status === 'uncovered');
    const partial = Object.keys(profile.surfaces).filter((key) => profile.surfaces[key] && PROFILE_SURFACES[key].status === 'partial');
    const warnings = [];
    if (profile.mode === 'full' && profile.surfaces.intl && !profile.locale) warnings.push('Intl surface is enabled but no user-supplied locale is configured; native locale remains.');
    if (profile.mode === 'full' && profile.surfaces.intl && !profile.timezone) warnings.push('Intl surface is enabled but no user-supplied timezone is configured.');
    const consistencyScore = Math.max(0, 100 - unsupported.length * 10 - partial.length * 5 - warnings.length * 2);
    return { ok: true, profile, consistencyScore, warnings, unsupported, partial };
  } catch (error) {
    return { ok: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Invalid profile.', consistencyScore: 0, warnings: [], unsupported: [], partial: [] };
  }
}

export function normalizeProfileAssignments(value, profiles, { strict = false } = {}) {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
    if (strict) throw new InputValidationError('Site assignments must be a list.');
    return [];
  }
  if (value.length > 100) {
    if (strict) throw new InputValidationError('At most 100 site assignments are allowed.');
    value = value.slice(0, 100);
  }
  const ids = new Set(profiles.map((item) => item.id));
  const lines = [];
  for (const item of value) {
    if (!isPlainRecord(item) || typeof item.domain !== 'string' || typeof item.profileId !== 'string' || !ids.has(item.profileId)) {
      if (strict) throw new InputValidationError('Each site assignment must reference an existing profile.');
      continue;
    }
    lines.push(`${item.domain} = ${item.profileId}`);
  }
  let parsed;
  try { parsed = parseOverrideList(lines.join('\n'), [...ids]); }
  catch (error) { if (strict) throw error; return []; }
  for (const item of parsed) {
    if (isHostnameIncluded(item.domain, RESERVED_DOMAINS)) {
      if (strict) throw new InputValidationError('Reserved browser/security domains cannot be assigned profiles.');
      return [];
    }
  }
  return parsed;
}

export function normalizeProfileLibrary(raw, { strict = false } = {}) {
  const fail = (message) => { if (strict) throw new InputValidationError(message); };
  if (!isPlainRecord(raw)) {
    fail('Profile library must be an object.');
    raw = {};
  }
  if (raw.schemaVersion !== undefined && raw.schemaVersion !== PROFILE_LIBRARY_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
    fail('Unsupported profile-library schema version.');
  }
  const rawProfiles = Array.isArray(raw.profiles) ? raw.profiles : [];
  if (rawProfiles.length > MAX_PROFILE_RECORDS) {
    fail(`A profile library may contain at most ${MAX_PROFILE_RECORDS} profiles.`);
    return { schemaVersion: PROFILE_LIBRARY_SCHEMA_VERSION, activeProfileId: '', profiles: [], assignments: [] };
  }
  const profiles = [];
  const seen = new Set();
  for (const entry of rawProfiles) {
    try {
      const profile = normalizeProfileRecord(entry, { strict: true });
      if (seen.has(profile.id)) throw new InputValidationError('Profile IDs must be unique.');
      seen.add(profile.id);
      profiles.push(profile);
    } catch (error) {
      if (strict) throw error;
    }
  }
  if (!profiles.length && !strict) {
    for (const baseId of Object.keys(PROFILES)) profiles.push(createProfileRecord(baseId));
  }
  if (!profiles.length) {
    fail('Profile library must contain at least one profile.');
    return { schemaVersion: PROFILE_LIBRARY_SCHEMA_VERSION, activeProfileId: '', profiles: [], assignments: [] };
  }
  const requestedActiveId = typeof raw.activeProfileId === 'string' ? raw.activeProfileId : '';
  const activeProfileId = profiles.some((entry) => entry.id === requestedActiveId)
    ? requestedActiveId : profiles[0].id;
  const assignments = normalizeProfileAssignments(raw.assignments ?? [], profiles, { strict });
  return { schemaVersion: PROFILE_LIBRARY_SCHEMA_VERSION, activeProfileId, profiles, assignments };
}

export function createDefaultProfileLibrary(profileId = 'chrome-155-windows', existingOverrides = []) {
  const profiles = Object.keys(PROFILES).map((baseId) => createProfileRecord(baseId));
  const selected = profiles.find((item) => item.baseProfileId === profileId) ?? profiles[0];
  const byBaseId = new Map(profiles.map((item) => [item.baseProfileId, item]));
  const assignments = normalizeProfileAssignments(existingOverrides.map((item) => ({
    domain: item.domain,
    profileId: byBaseId.get(item.profileId)?.id ?? selected.id,
  })), profiles);
  return normalizeProfileLibrary({ schemaVersion: PROFILE_LIBRARY_SCHEMA_VERSION, activeProfileId: selected.id, profiles, assignments }, { strict: true });
}

export function cloneProfileRecord(source, { id, name, seed = newSeed() } = {}) {
  const clone = normalizeProfileRecord({
    ...source,
    id,
    name: name ?? `${source.name} copy`,
    seed,
    createdAt: nowIso(),
    updatedAt: nowIso(),
  }, { strict: true });
  return clone;
}

export function createProfileExport(library, exportedAt = nowIso()) {
  const normalized = normalizeProfileLibrary(library, { strict: true });
  return {
    format: PROFILE_EXPORT_FORMAT,
    exportVersion: PROFILE_EXPORT_VERSION,
    exportedAt,
    profiles: normalized.profiles,
    assignments: normalized.assignments,
    activeProfileId: normalized.activeProfileId,
  };
}

export function parseProfileImport(value) {
  if (!isPlainRecord(value) || value.format !== PROFILE_EXPORT_FORMAT || value.exportVersion !== PROFILE_EXPORT_VERSION || !Array.isArray(value.profiles)) {
    throw new InputValidationError('Unsupported profile definitions file.');
  }
  return normalizeProfileLibrary({
    schemaVersion: PROFILE_LIBRARY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    activeProfileId: value.activeProfileId,
    profiles: value.profiles,
    assignments: value.assignments ?? [],
  }, { strict: true });
}

export function profileForUrl(library, urlString, fallbackBaseProfileId) {
  let hostname;
  try { hostname = new URL(urlString).hostname.toLowerCase(); } catch { return null; }
  const matches = library.assignments
    .filter((entry) => isHostnameIncluded(hostname, [entry.domain]))
    .sort((a, b) => b.domain.length - a.domain.length);
  if (matches.length) return library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === matches[0].profileId) ?? null;
  const active = library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.id === library.activeProfileId);
  if (active?.baseProfileId === fallbackBaseProfileId) return active;
  return library.profiles.find((entry) => entry.baseProfileId === fallbackBaseProfileId) ?? null;
}

export function profileCoverage(profile) {
  const enabled = Object.keys(profile?.surfaces ?? {}).filter((key) => profile.surfaces[key]);
  return {
    mode: profile?.mode ?? 'identity',
    enabledSurfaces: enabled,
    note: 'Veil Profile Studio v3.0.0 Universal — Anti-Detect Engine with full prototype stealth across PC & Android.',
  };
}

export function randomizeProfileDynamic(category = 'all', currentProfile = null) {
  let candidates = [...DEVICE_PRESETS];
  if (category === 'pc' || category === 'desktop') {
    candidates = candidates.filter(p => !p.baseProfileId.includes('android'));
  } else if (category === 'android' || category === 'mobile') {
    candidates = candidates.filter(p => p.baseProfileId.includes('android'));
  }
  if (!candidates.length) candidates = [...DEVICE_PRESETS];
  const chosenPreset = candidates[Math.floor(Math.random() * candidates.length)];
  return {
    ...chosenPreset,
    seed: newSeed(),
  };
}

  // model is intentional where no specific physical model is claimed.
